+## DESIGN ALREADY DECIDED (read the code first, it matches these places)
+- Handler placement: NovaService.handle(), right BEFORE `AgentRules.isTask(text)` (after MusicPlayRules and AppSearchRules so "youtube kholo aur song play karo" keeps working). Local rules, no AI call = fast.
+- New pure Kotlin object `OpenWebRules` (parseOpen, parseDownload, siteUrl): reject negations and any sentence with play/search/message/call/bhejo/download/alarm/camera/etc words so other handlers keep ownership. Single name without "chrome/website/site/tab" -> return null (existing open_app path stays). Split on aur / and / comma; greedy match of known site names when no separator is spoken. Cap 8 targets.
+- Chrome tab: Intent(ACTION_VIEW, uri).setPackage("com.android.chrome"), NO Browser.EXTRA_APPLICATION_ID extra (that would reuse one tab), 600 ms between tabs, fallback to the default browser if Chrome is missing. Unknown single word in website mode -> https://www.<word>.com; unknown multi-word -> Google search.
+- open_app fallback: in runCommand "open_app" (and runTool open_app) call openApp first; only when NO installed app matches AND the name is a known site/domain, open it in Chrome. Never change openApp itself.
+- Play Store download: Control.blocked() keeps com.android.vending BLOCKED (SafetyInvariantsTest, ScreenSessionTest, AgentRulesPart6Test pin it; voice "tap" must stay refused there). Add a SEPARATE narrow path: `PlayInstallRules` (pure: arm/armedFor 60 s window per package, decide(lines) -> TAP / STARTED / INSTALLED / NEED_LOGIN / PAID / WAIT, pageMatches) and `NovaAccessibilityService.playInstall(pkg, label)`. It may press ONLY an exact "Install" label, ONLY in com.android.vending, ONLY for a package from the built-in known-package map (details page market://details?id=<pkg>), ONLY while armed, ONLY when the page title matches the app name; the top-most exact Install node wins. Never presses a price/Buy button, Sign in, "Install anyway", Update. Unknown app name -> open Play Store search (market://search?q=...&c=apps) and say honestly that the owner must pick and press Install.
+- Result words must be honest: "download start ho gaya sir" only when Cancel/Installing/Downloading/Pending (or Open+Uninstall) was seen after the tap; otherwise "Install dabaya par pushti nahi hui, Play Store mein dekh lo". Already installed -> say so, do not open Play Store. Several apps in one sentence -> do the first, tell the owner to ask for the rest.
+- Tests to add: OpenWebRulesTest.kt, PlayInstallRulesTest.kt, a routing test proving SiteIntent/ConnectorRules/GmailRules/AppSearchRules/MusicPlayRules/LearnRules/ImageIntent/LocalSkills/HelpGuide return null for the new phrases, and tools/test_open_web_download_contract.py (static: Control.blocked untouched, playInstall only exact "Install", handler before AgentRules.isTask, 60 s window).

+## ARCHITECTURE NOTES FOR THE NEW WORK
+- NovaService.launch(i, expectPkg, what) is the one way to start an activity (service, Accessibility and PendingIntent fallbacks, honest result). Use it for every tab.
+- R(ok, msg) is the result type; tr(hindi, english) picks the language; claimAction() blocks double sends; WhatsAppTarget has the two WhatsApp package ids.
+- QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES is in the manifest, so getApplicationInfo / getLaunchIntentForPackage work for any package.
+- Accessibility config only listens to window-state events, but rootInActiveWindow works anyway; `collect()` and `findLabel()` in NovaAccessibilityService.companion are private helpers you can reuse inside playInstall.

+## ARCHITECTURE NOTES FOR THE NEW WORK
+- NovaService.launch(i, expectPkg, what) is the one way to start an activity (service, Accessibility and PendingIntent fallbacks, honest result). Use it for every tab.
+- R(ok, msg) is the result type; tr(hindi, english) picks the language; claimAction() blocks double sends; WhatsAppTarget has the two WhatsApp package ids.
+- QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES is in the manifest, so getApplicationInfo / getLaunchIntentForPackage work for any package.
+- Accessibility config only listens to window-state events, but rootInActiveWindow works anyway; `collect()` and `findLabel()` in NovaAccessibilityService.companion are private helpers you can reuse inside playInstall.

+    // ===== v7.12 PHOTO EDITOR (add-only). Level 1 = on the phone (PhotoPro, no internet). Level 2 = Gemini, only after a native HAAN tap.
+    // Level 3 = honest text when AI did not work (the phone result is still given). The ORIGINAL (pickedPhoto) is never overwritten:

+    private fun editFinish(ok: Boolean, msg: String, bytes: ByteArray?, level: Int) {
+        var thumb = ""
+        var saved = ""
+        if (ok && bytes != null) {
+            synchronized(editHistory) {
+                editedPhoto?.let { editHistory.add(it); while (editHistory.size > 5) editHistory.removeAt(0) }
+            }
+            editedPhoto = bytes
+            saved = try { ImageGen.saveToGallery(this, bytes) ?: "" } catch (e: Throwable) { "" }
+            thumb = ImageGen.b64(ImageGen.shrink(bytes, 1024) ?: bytes)
+        }
+        lastEditReport = "Level " + level + ": " + msg + (if (saved.isNotEmpty()) " | Gallery: " + saved else "")
+        imgBusy = false
+        runJs("onPhotoEdited(" + (ok && thumb.isNotEmpty()) + "," + JSONObject.quote(msg) + "," + JSONObject.quote(thumb) + "," + JSONObject.quote(saved) + "," + level + ")")
+    }

+    /** Level 1: preset / crop on the phone. [note] is put in front of the honest result text. */
+    private fun editPhone(pe: PhotoEditIntent.Parsed, src: ByteArray, note: String) {
+        imgBusy = true
+        Thread {
+            try {
+                val preset = try { if (pe.preset.isEmpty()) null else PhotoPro.Preset.valueOf(pe.preset) } catch (e: Exception) { null }
+                val ratio = if (pe.kind == PhotoEditIntent.Kind.CROP) pe.ratio.split(":").mapNotNull { it.toIntOrNull() }.toIntArray().takeIf { it.size == 2 } else null
+                val out = PhotoPro.run(src, preset, ratio)
+                if (out == null) editFinish(false, note + "Phone par edit nahi ho paya (photo bahut badi ya format ki dikkat)", null, 1)
+                else editFinish(true, note + "Ho gaya, phone par (internet nahi gaya)", out, 1)
+            } catch (e: Throwable) { editFinish(false, note + "Edit beech mein ruk gaya", null, 1) }
+        }.start()
+    }
+
+    /** Level 2: Gemini photo edit. Only called after the HAAN tap. When it fails, Level 3 = honest text + the phone result. */
+    private fun editAi(pe: PhotoEditIntent.Parsed, said: String, src: ByteArray) {
+        imgBusy = true
+        Thread {
+            try {
+                val prompt = "Edit the attached photo exactly as requested and keep everything else, especially faces and identity, unchanged. Request: " + said.take(300)
+                val r = ImageGen.generate(this, prompt, src)
+                val img = r.img
+                if (r.ok && img != null) editFinish(true, "AI se ho gaya", img, 2)
+                else editPhone(pe, src, "AI ne nahi kiya (" + r.msg.take(120) + "). Phone se jitna ho sakta tha kiya. ")
+            } catch (e: Throwable) { editPhone(pe, src, "AI ne nahi kiya (dikkat aayi). Phone se jitna ho sakta tha kiya. ") }
+        }.start()
+    }

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.00 AUTO MEDIA: NOVA decides by itself what the owner wants, no buttons.
+ *  - SITE : a website request, even when it also says photo / image / video ("restaurant ki website banao jisme photos ho").
+ *           The old SiteIntent refuses every sentence that has a photo word, and ImageIntent then wrongly took it as "make a picture".
+ *  - VIDEO: "ek video banao" (make a video). Free AI video is not available without a paid key, so NOVA says so honestly.
+ *  - IMAGE: a plain picture request (still handled by ImageIntent / FreeImage; listed here so the decision lives in one place).
+ * Pure Kotlin (no Android classes), so it runs in plain JVM unit tests. It never sends anything anywhere.
+ * Input is Roman text (Devanagari is converted by HindiRoman before this is called).
+ * tools/test_auto_media_contract.py reads the sets below straight from this file, so keep one set per line.
+ */
+object MediaIntent {
+    enum class Kind { SITE, IMAGE, VIDEO }
+    class Result(val kind: Kind, val subject: String, val blocked: Boolean, val raw: String)
+
+    private val BUILD = setOf("banao", "bana", "banado", "banade", "banana", "banaiye", "banaye", "bnao", "bna", "banwao", "banwa", "banani", "banane", "bnani", "bnane", "banwani", "banwane", "banwana", "generate", "create", "build", "make", "design", "develop")
+    private val SITE_NOUNS = setOf("website", "websites", "webpage", "webpages", "site", "portfolio", "landing")
+    private val IMG_WORDS = setOf("photo", "photos", "foto", "fotos", "image", "images", "picture", "pictures", "pic", "pics", "tasveer", "tasvir", "tasveeren", "tasviren", "banner", "logo", "gallery")
+    private val VIDEO_WORDS = setOf("video", "videos", "vedio", "vedios", "reel", "reels", "clip", "clips")
+    // Words that mean another feature owns the sentence (opening apps, recording, sharing, calls, agent jobs).
+    private val OTHER = setOf("kholo", "khol", "open", "chatgpt", "gemini", "gpt", "delete", "hatao", "bhejo", "send", "share", "whatsapp", "instagram", "call", "kaam", "agent", "chrome", "browser", "record", "recording", "camera", "selfie")
+    private val NO_MEDIA = setOf("bina", "without", "no", "nahi", "nhi", "mat", "sirf", "only")
+    private val FILLER = setOf("nova", "ek", "mujhe", "mere", "meri", "mera", "hamare", "ki", "ka", "ke", "ko", "se", "me", "mein", "par", "pe", "ye", "wo", "please", "plz", "pls", "zara", "kripya", "kar", "karo", "kardo", "do", "dena", "de", "dijiye", "chahiye", "chahie", "chaiye", "hai", "ho", "wala", "wali", "wale", "a", "an", "the", "of", "for", "my", "i", "want", "need", "can", "you", "could", "would", "free", "mujhko", "humko", "hume", "liye", "naya", "nayi", "new", "web", "page", "pages", "jisme", "jismein", "saath", "sath", "with", "and", "aur", "bhi", "sab", "full", "complete", "poori", "puri", "pura", "ai", "generated", "generate")
+    private val BLOCK = setOf("nude", "naked", "nsfw", "porn", "porno", "sex", "xxx", "nangi", "nanga", "erotic", "phishing", "hack", "hacking", "scam", "fake")
+
+    fun tokens(s: String): List<String> {
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        for (ch in s.lowercase()) {
+            if ((ch in 'a'..'z') || (ch in '0'..'9')) sb.append(ch) else sb.append(' ')
+        }
+        val out = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (w in sb.toString().split(' ')) if (w.isNotEmpty()) out.add(w)
+        return out
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Websites get AI pictures by default; the owner can opt out ("bina photo ke website banao", "website without images", "sirf text").
+     * "no" + a picture word within two words of each other = opt out.
+     */
+    fun wantsImages(text: String): Boolean {
+        val t = tokens(text)
+        for (i in t.indices) {
+            if (t[i] in NO_MEDIA) {
+                for (j in maxOf(0, i - 2)..minOf(t.size - 1, i + 2)) if (t[j] in IMG_WORDS) return false
+            }
+        }
+        if ("text" in t && ("sirf" in t || "only" in t)) return false
+        return true
+    }
+
+    /** True when the owner mentions video for the site. NOVA cannot make free AI video, so the page gets an animated (CSS/canvas) background instead. */
+    fun wantsVideo(text: String): Boolean = tokens(text).any { it in VIDEO_WORDS }
+
+    /**
+     * null = not a media sentence, or the old routes (SiteIntent / ImageIntent) already own it.
+     * SITE is returned ONLY when SiteIntent.parse would have said "no" because of a photo / video word.
+     */
+    fun classify(text: String): Result? {
+        val t = tokens(text)
+        if (t.isEmpty() || t.size > 60) return null
+        var build = false
+        var site = false
+        var img = false
+        var vid = false
+        for (w in t) {
+            if (w in OTHER) return null
+            if (w in BUILD) build = true
+            if (w in SITE_NOUNS) site = true
+            if (w in IMG_WORDS) img = true
+            if (w in VIDEO_WORDS) vid = true
+        }
+        if (!build) return null
+        val blocked = t.any { it in BLOCK }
+        if (site) {
+            if (!img && !vid) return null   // no media word: the old SiteIntent route handles it exactly as before
+            val keep = ArrayList<String>()
+            for (w in t) {
+                if (w in BUILD || w in SITE_NOUNS || w in FILLER || w in IMG_WORDS || w in VIDEO_WORDS || w in NO_MEDIA) continue
+                keep.add(w)
+            }
+            var subject = keep.joinToString(" ")
+            if (subject.length < 3) subject = ""
+            return Result(Kind.SITE, subject, blocked, text.trim().take(1500))
+        }
+        if (vid && !img) {
+            // "ek video banao" / "make a video of a sunset". Recording phrases were already excluded through OTHER.
+            val keep = ArrayList<String>()
+            for (w in t) if (!(w in BUILD || w in FILLER || w in VIDEO_WORDS)) keep.add(w)
+            return Result(Kind.VIDEO, keep.joinToString(" "), blocked, text.trim().take(300))
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.03 G: where on the website does a picked gallery photo / video go? Pure Kotlin (no Android classes), Roman text only
+ * (HindiRoman already converts Devanagari). "ye photo hero mein lagao", "dusri photo gallery mein", "m2 logo banao header mein".
+ * It only decides; it never sends anything anywhere.
+ * tools/test_site_media_contract.py reads the sets below straight from this file, so keep one set per line.
+ */
+object MediaPlace {
+    enum class Place { HERO, ABOUT, GALLERY, LOGO, MENU, FOOTER, BACKGROUND }
+
+    private val HERO = setOf("hero", "banner", "top", "upar", "oopar", "cover", "front")
+    private val ABOUT = setOf("about", "aboutus", "baare", "bare")
+    private val GALLERY = setOf("gallery", "album", "grid")
+    private val LOGO = setOf("logo", "header", "navbar", "brand")
+    private val MENU = setOf("menu", "products", "services", "offerings")
+    private val FOOTER = setOf("footer", "contact", "neeche", "niche", "bottom")
+    private val BACKGROUND = setOf("background", "bg", "wallpaper", "peeche")
+    private val ACTS = setOf("lagao", "lagana", "laga", "lagado", "lagade", "rakho", "rakhna", "daalo", "dalo", "daal", "dalna", "use", "set", "add", "jodo", "put", "place")
+    private val MEDIA_REF = setOf("ye", "yeh", "is", "isko", "ise", "photo", "photos", "image", "images", "video", "videos", "picture", "pic", "foto", "tasveer", "clip", "reel")
+    private val FIRST = setOf("pehli", "pehla", "pahli", "pahla", "first")
+    private val SECOND = setOf("dusri", "doosri", "dusra", "doosra", "second")
+    private val THIRD = setOf("teesri", "teesra", "third")
+    private val FOURTH = setOf("chauthi", "chautha", "fourth")
+    private val LAST = setOf("last", "aakhri", "akhri")
+
+    fun tokens(s: String): List<String> {
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        for (ch in s.lowercase()) {
+            if ((ch in 'a'..'z') || (ch in '0'..'9')) sb.append(ch) else sb.append(' ')
+        }
+        val out = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (w in sb.toString().split(' ')) if (w.isNotEmpty()) out.add(w)
+        return out
+    }
+
+    /** First place word in the sentence (sentence order), or null. */
+    fun parse(text: String): Place? {
+        for (w in tokens(text)) {
+            when (w) {
+                in HERO -> return Place.HERO
+                in ABOUT -> return Place.ABOUT
+                in GALLERY -> return Place.GALLERY
+                in LOGO -> return Place.LOGO
+                in MENU -> return Place.MENU
+                in FOOTER -> return Place.FOOTER
+                in BACKGROUND -> return Place.BACKGROUND
+            }
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    /** 1-based item number ("pehli photo" = 1, "m3" = 3), -1 = the last one, null = not said. */
+    fun itemIndex(text: String): Int? {
+        for (w in tokens(text)) {
+            when {
+                w in FIRST -> return 1
+                w in SECOND -> return 2
+                w in THIRD -> return 3
+                w in FOURTH -> return 4
+                w in LAST -> return -1
+                w.length == 2 && w[0] == 'm' && w[1] in '1'..'6' -> return w[1] - '0'
+            }
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    /** "ye photo hero mein lagao": a place word + a put-it verb + a media reference. Caller also checks that picked media exist. */
+    fun isPlaceCommand(text: String): Boolean {
+        val t = tokens(text)
+        if (t.isEmpty() || t.size > 30) return false
+        if (parse(text) == null) return false
+        if (t.none { it in ACTS }) return false
+        return itemIndex(text) != null || t.any { it in MEDIA_REF }
+    }
+
+    fun label(p: Place?): String = when (p) {
+        Place.HERO -> "hero"
+        Place.ABOUT -> "about"
+        Place.GALLERY -> "gallery"
+        Place.LOGO -> "logo"
+        Place.MENU -> "menu"
+        Place.FOOTER -> "footer"
+        Place.BACKGROUND -> "background"
+        null -> "sahi jagah"
+    }
+
+    fun fromName(name: String): Place? = Place.values().firstOrNull { it.name.equals(name.trim(), true) }
+}

@@ -474,6 +474,140 @@ class NovaAccessibilityService : AccessibilityService() {
             }
         }
 
+        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ v6.97 wip14 (Part 4): Play Store install = a SEPARATE narrow path
+        // Control.blocked() still refuses every voice "tap" / "type" in com.android.vending (nothing above changed).
+        // Only the two functions below may act there, and only as PlayInstallRules allows:
+        // armed for this key (60 s), the page title is the asked app, and the pressed node's text is exactly "Install".
+        private const val PLAY_PKG = "com.android.vending"
+
+        /** The Play Store window, or null (never NOVA's own window, never another app). */
+        private fun playRoot(s: NovaAccessibilityService): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
+            val r = s.rootInActiveWindow
+            if (r != null && r.packageName?.toString() == PLAY_PKG) return r
+            val alt = otherAppRoot(s)
+            return if (alt != null && alt.packageName?.toString() == PLAY_PKG) alt else null
+        }
+
+        private fun playLines(root: AccessibilityNodeInfo): List<String> {
+            val out = ArrayList<String>()
+            collect(root, 0, out, false)
+            return out
+        }
+
+        private fun topOf(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): Int {
+            val r = Rect()
+            n.getBoundsInScreen(r)
+            return r.top
+        }
+
+        /** The top-most visible, enabled node whose whole text is exactly "Install" ("Install anyway", "Installs", "Uninstall" never match). */
+        private fun exactInstallNode(root: AccessibilityNodeInfo): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
+            val found = ArrayList<AccessibilityNodeInfo>()
+            try {
+                val list = root.findAccessibilityNodeInfosByText("Install") ?: return null
+                for (n in list) {
+                    val t = (n.text?.toString() ?: n.contentDescription?.toString() ?: "").trim()
+                    if (t.equals("Install", ignoreCase = true) && n.isVisibleToUser && n.isEnabled && !n.isPassword) found.add(n)
+                }
+            } catch (e: Exception) {
+                return null
+            }
+            return found.minByOrNull { topOf(it) }
+        }
+
+        /**
+         * Presses Install on the Play Store page of ONE app and reports what it really saw. Blocking (up to about 20 s): call from a worker thread.
+         * [key] = the key NovaService armed in PlayInstallRules (the package id, or "q:<name>" for an unknown app). One press at most.
+         * Codes: started / installed (ok) | need_login / paid / no_acc / not_armed / no_page / tap_failed / unconfirmed / interrupted (not ok).
+         */
+        fun playInstall(key: String, label: String, cancel: () -> Boolean = { false }): Ctl {
+            val s = instance ?: return Ctl(false, "no_acc")
+            if (!PlayInstallRules.armedFor(key, System.currentTimeMillis())) return Ctl(false, "not_armed")
+            var tapped = false
+            val t0 = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
+            loop@ while (SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - t0 < 14_000L) {
+                if (cancel()) return Ctl(false, "interrupted")
+                val root = playRoot(s)
+                if (root != null) {
+                    val lines = playLines(root)
+                    if (!PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(lines, label)) {
+                        // Not the asked app's page (yet). Only a sign-in screen is worth reporting.
+                        if (PlayInstallRules.decide(lines, false) == PlayInstallRules.Decision.NEED_LOGIN) return Ctl(false, "need_login")
+                    } else {
+                        when (PlayInstallRules.decide(lines, false)) {
+                            PlayInstallRules.Decision.INSTALLED -> return Ctl(true, "installed")
+                            PlayInstallRules.Decision.NEED_LOGIN -> return Ctl(false, "need_login")
+                            PlayInstallRules.Decision.PAID -> return Ctl(false, "paid")
+                            PlayInstallRules.Decision.TAP -> {
+                                if (!PlayInstallRules.mayTap(key, label, lines, System.currentTimeMillis())) return Ctl(false, "not_armed")
+                                val node = exactInstallNode(root)
+                                if (node != null) {
+                                    PlayInstallRules.disarm(key)          // one press only, whatever happens next
+                                    if (!s.clickNode(node)) return Ctl(false, "tap_failed")
+                                    tapped = true
+                                    break@loop
+                                }
+                            }
+                            else -> {}
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                try { Thread.sleep(500) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { return Ctl(false, "interrupted") }
+            }
+            if (!tapped) return Ctl(false, "no_page")
+            val v0 = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
+            while (SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - v0 < 6_000L) {
+                if (cancel()) return Ctl(false, "interrupted")
+                try { Thread.sleep(500) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { return Ctl(false, "interrupted") }
+                val root = playRoot(s) ?: continue
+                val lines = playLines(root)
+                if (!PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(lines, label)) continue
+                when (PlayInstallRules.decide(lines, true)) {
+                    PlayInstallRules.Decision.STARTED -> return Ctl(true, "started")
+                    PlayInstallRules.Decision.NEED_LOGIN -> return Ctl(false, "need_login")
+                    else -> {}
+                }
+            }
+            return Ctl(false, "unconfirmed")
+        }
+
+        private fun collectPlayHits(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, depth: Int, spoken: String, out: ArrayList<AccessibilityNodeInfo>) {
+            if (n == null || depth > 30 || out.size >= 10) return
+            try {
+                if (!n.isVisibleToUser) return
+                val t = n.text?.toString()
+                if (!t.isNullOrBlank() && !n.isEditable && !n.isPassword && PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches(t, spoken)) out.add(n)
+                for (i in 0 until n.childCount) collectPlayHits(n.getChild(i), depth + 1, spoken, out)
+            } catch (e: Exception) {
+                // node went away while walking: ignore
+            }
+        }
+
+        /**
+         * Unknown app name: in the Play SEARCH results tap the first result whose title is exactly the spoken name (PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches).
+         * The search box can show the same words, so when two nodes match the second one (the first is the box) is used. Wrong tap is harmless:
+         * playInstall then finds no matching page title and presses nothing. Blocking (up to about 10 s): worker thread only.
+         */
+        fun playOpenResult(spoken: String, cancel: () -> Boolean = { false }): Ctl {
+            val s = instance ?: return Ctl(false, "no_acc")
+            val t0 = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
+            while (SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - t0 < 10_000L) {
+                if (cancel()) return Ctl(false, "interrupted")
+                val root = playRoot(s)
+                if (root != null) {
+                    val hits = ArrayList<AccessibilityNodeInfo>()
+                    collectPlayHits(root, 0, spoken, hits)
+                    if (hits.isNotEmpty()) {
+                        val sorted = hits.sortedBy { topOf(it) }
+                        val pick = if (sorted.size >= 2) sorted[1] else sorted[0]
+                        return if (s.clickNode(pick)) Ctl(true, "ok") else Ctl(false, "tap_failed")
+                    }
+                }
+                try { Thread.sleep(500) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { return Ctl(false, "interrupted") }
+            }
+            return Ctl(false, "not_found")
+        }
+
         /** Cancels pending scans at once when monitoring is switched off. */
         fun monitorChanged() { instance?.onMonitorChanged() }

+        } catch (e: Throwable) { ErrLog.add("NovaService", e.toString()) }
+        return if (copied) tr(

+    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- v6.97 wip14: websites in Chrome tabs
+
+    private val CHROME_PKG = "com.android.chrome"
+
+    /** True when an installed app matches the spoken name (the same matching openApp uses). */
+    private fun appInstalled(name: String): Boolean {
+        val q = name.trim()
+        if (q.isEmpty()) return false
+        val normalized = Logic.norm(q).replace(Regex("[^a-z0-9 ]"), " ").replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ").trim()
+        when (normalized) {
+            "whatsapp", "wa" -> return WhatsAppTarget.isInstalled(packageManager, WhatsAppTarget.App.MESSENGER)
+            "whatsapp business", "wa business" -> return WhatsAppTarget.isInstalled(packageManager, WhatsAppTarget.App.BUSINESS)
+        }
+        val pm = packageManager
+        val list = pm.queryIntentActivities(Intent(Intent.ACTION_MAIN).addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_LAUNCHER), 0)
+        return Logic.bestAppIndex(q, list.map { it.loadLabel(pm).toString() }) >= 0
+    }
+
+    /** One website = one Chrome tab (a VIEW intent without Browser.EXTRA_APPLICATION_ID makes Chrome open a NEW tab). Default browser if Chrome is missing. */
+    private fun openChromeTab(url: String, label: String): R {
+        val uri = try { Uri.parse(url) } catch (e: Exception) { null }
+        val scheme = uri?.scheme?.lowercase()
+        if (uri == null || (scheme != "http" && scheme != "https") || uri.host.isNullOrBlank())
+            return R(false, tr("$label ka address sahi nahi laga", "The address for $label looked wrong"))
+        val hasChrome = packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(CHROME_PKG) != null
+        val i = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, uri).addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_BROWSABLE)
+        if (hasChrome) i.setPackage(CHROME_PKG)
+        return launch(i, if (hasChrome) CHROME_PKG else null, label)
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Old behaviour first: an installed app that matches the name opens. Only when NO app matches, the name is opened as a website in Chrome
+     * (known site, else www.<word>.com, several words = a Google search) and the answer says so.
+     */
+    private fun openAppOrSite(name: String): R {
+        if (appInstalled(name)) return openApp(name)
+        val url = OpenWebRules.siteUrl(name, guess = true) ?: return openApp(name)
+        val label = OpenWebRules.labelFor(name)
+        val r = openChromeTab(url, label)
+        if (!r.ok) return openApp(name)
+        // A several-word name that is not a known site becomes a Google search, not a website: say exactly that.
+        return if (url.startsWith("https://www.google.com/search?q="))
+            R(true, tr("$label naam ka app nahi mila, isliye Chrome mein Google search khol diya", "I found no app called $label, so I opened a Google search in Chrome"))
+        else R(true, tr("$label naam ka app nahi mila, isliye website Chrome mein khol di", "I found no app called $label, so I opened the website in Chrome"))
+    }
+
+    /** Several names in one sentence: every website gets its own Chrome tab, every app opens as an app. The answer lists only what really opened. */
+    private fun openWeb(req: OpenWebRules.OpenReq): String {
+        val opened = ArrayList<String>()
+        val failed = ArrayList<String>()
+        var tabs = 0
+        var idx = 0
+        for (t in req.targets) {
+            if (!alive) break
+            if (idx++ > 0) { try { Thread.sleep(600) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { break } }   // let Chrome finish the previous tab first
+            val r: R = when {
+                t.web -> openChromeTab(t.url ?: "", t.label).also { if (it.ok) tabs++ }
+                appInstalled(t.name) -> openApp(t.name)
+                t.url != null -> openChromeTab(t.url, t.label).also { if (it.ok) tabs++ }
+                else -> R(false, tr("${t.label} naam ka app nahi mila", "I found no app called ${t.label}"))
+            }
+            if (r.ok) opened.add(t.label) else failed.add(t.label)
+        }
+        val head = when {
+            opened.isEmpty() -> ""
+            opened.size == 1 && tabs == 1 -> tr("${opened[0]} Chrome mein khul gaya", "${opened[0]} is open in Chrome")
+            tabs == opened.size -> tr("${opened.size} website alag-alag tab mein khul gayi: ${opened.joinToString(", ")}", "Opened ${opened.size} websites in separate Chrome tabs: ${opened.joinToString(", ")}")
+            else -> tr("${opened.joinToString(", ")} khul gaye", "Opened ${opened.joinToString(", ")}")
+        }
+        val bad = if (failed.isEmpty()) "" else tr("Ye nahi khul paye: ${failed.joinToString(", ")}", "Could not open: ${failed.joinToString(", ")}")
+        val more = if (req.truncated) tr("Sirf pehle ${OpenWebRules.MAX_TARGETS} khole, baaki dobara bolo", "I opened only the first ${OpenWebRules.MAX_TARGETS}, ask again for the rest") else ""
+        return listOf(head, bad, more).filter { it.isNotBlank() }.joinToString(". ")
+    }
+
+    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- v6.97 wip14 (Part 4): app download from the Play Store
+    private val PLAY_PKG = "com.android.vending"
+
+    /**
+     * Opens the app's Play Store page and presses ONLY the exact "Install" button (NovaAccessibilityService.playInstall, armed 60 s, page title checked).
+     * Known app = details page. Unknown app = Play search, first result only when its title is exactly the spoken name.
+     * The reply says "download start ho gaya" only when Cancel / Downloading / Installing / Pending was really seen after the press.
+     * Several apps in one sentence: the first only, the rest are listed. Worker thread only (it waits for the screen).
+     */
+    private fun downloadApp(req: OpenWebRules.DownloadReq): String {
+        val label = req.label
+        val pkg = req.pkg
+        val more = if (req.rest.isEmpty()) "" else tr(". Baaki (${req.rest.joinToString(", ")}) ek-ek karke bolo", ". Ask for the others (${req.rest.joinToString(", ")}) one at a time")
+        if (packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(PLAY_PKG) == null)
+            return tr("Is phone par Play Store nahi mila", "Play Store was not found on this phone")
+        if (pkg != null && packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(pkg) != null)
+            return tr("$label pehle se installed hai sir", "$label is already installed") + more
+        val acc = NovaAccessibilityService.isEnabled(this)
+        val key = pkg ?: ("q:" + req.name.trim().lowercase())
+        if (acc) PlayInstallRules.arm(key, System.currentTimeMillis())
+        val uri = if (pkg != null) "market://details?id=$pkg" else "market://search?q=" + enc(req.name) + "&c=apps"
+        val opened = launch(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(uri)).setPackage(PLAY_PKG), PLAY_PKG, "Play Store")
+        if (!opened.ok) { PlayInstallRules.disarm(key); return opened.msg }
+        if (!acc) return tr("$label ka Play Store page khol diya. NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility) band hai, isliye Install aap dabao", "I opened the Play Store for $label. NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility) is off, so please press Install yourself") + more
+        if (pkg == null) {
+            val found = NovaAccessibilityService.playOpenResult(req.name) { !alive }
+            if (!found.ok) {
+                PlayInstallRules.disarm(key)
+                return tr("Play Store mein '${req.label}' naam ka app pakka nahi mila. Search khula hai, aap apna app chuno aur Install dabao", "I could not find an app named exactly '${req.label}' in the Play Store. The search is open, please pick the app and press Install") + more
+            }
+        }
+        val res = NovaAccessibilityService.playInstall(key, label) { !alive }
+        PlayInstallRules.disarm(key)
+        val msg = when (res.code) {
+            "started" -> tr("$label ka download start ho gaya sir", "The download of $label has started")
+            "installed" -> tr("$label pehle se installed hai sir", "$label is already installed")
+            "need_login" -> tr("Play Store mein Google account login nahi hai. Pehle login karo, phir dobara bolo", "You are not signed in to the Play Store. Sign in first, then ask again")
+            "paid" -> tr("$label paid app hai, main price wala button nahi dabata. Aap khud dekh lo", "$label is a paid app, I never press a price button. Please check it yourself")
+            "tap_failed" -> tr("Install button dabane mein dikkat aayi. Play Store mein aap dabao", "I could not press the Install button. Please press it in the Play Store")
+            "unconfirmed" -> tr("Install dabaya par download shuru hone ki pushti nahi hui. Play Store mein dekh lo", "I pressed Install but could not confirm that the download began. Please check the Play Store")
+            "interrupted" -> tr("Download ka kaam beech mein roka gaya, kuch pakka nahi hua. Play Store mein dekh lo", "The download job was interrupted, nothing is confirmed. Please check the Play Store")
+            "not_armed" -> tr("Install ki ijazat ka samay khatam ho gaya, dobara bolo", "The install window ran out, please ask again")
+            "no_acc" -> tr("NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility) band hai, isliye Install aap dabao", "NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility) is off, so please press Install yourself")
+            else -> tr("$label ka sahi Play Store page nahi mila, kuch dabaya nahi. Play Store mein aap dekh lo", "I did not reach the right Play Store page for $label and pressed nothing. Please check the Play Store")
+        }
+        return msg + more
+    }
+
     // ---------------------------------------------------------------- contacts, call, sms, WhatsApp
     private val NUMBER_RE = Regex("^[+0-9 \\-]{5,}$")

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import java.net.URLEncoder
+
+/**
+ * v6.97 wip14 (Part 1): pure text rules for
+ *  - "<site> chrome pe kholo", "youtube, google aur flipkart kholo" (several websites, one Chrome tab each),
+ *  - "<app> download karo" (Play Store).
+ * No Android classes: unit tested. Nothing here opens anything; NovaService does that and reports the real result.
+ *
+ * Ownership rule: a sentence that belongs to another handler (play / search / message / call / alarm / negation ...) returns null,
+ * and a plain single "youtube kholo" also returns null, so the existing "open the installed app" path stays exactly as it was.
+ */
+object OpenWebRules {
+    /** [web] = open as a website in Chrome. false = installed app first (a known website only when no such app exists). */
+    data class Target(val name: String, val label: String, val url: String?, val web: Boolean)
+    data class OpenReq(val targets: List<Target>, val chrome: Boolean, val truncated: Boolean)
+    /** [pkg] = known Play Store package (details page). null = unknown app: only the Play Store search can be opened. [rest] = more apps named in the same sentence. */
+    data class DownloadReq(val name: String, val label: String, val pkg: String?, val rest: List<String>)

+    private val DL_BLOCK = setOf(
+        "play", "gaana", "song", "music", "video", "movie", "film", "photo", "image", "pic", "file", "pdf", "zip", "apk",
+        "update", "upgrade", "nova", "model", "offline", "pack", "vosk", "report", "status", "reel", "reels", "document",
+        "notes", "data", "dp", "wallpaper", "ringtone", "backup", "uninstall", "chat", "message", "msg", "bhejo", "send",
+        "call", "open", "kholo", "khol", "mat", "nahi", "nahin", "nhi", "dont", "not", "never", "bina"
+    )
+    private val DL_FILLER = FILLER + setOf("phone", "mobile", "wala", "wali", "latest", "app", "apps", "application", "mujhe", "chahiye", "hai")
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ normalising
+
+    private fun norm(raw: String): String {
+        // HindiRoman turns every punctuation mark (also the dot of "flipkart.com") into a space: protect the dots inside web addresses.
+        val guarded = raw.replace(Regex("(?<=[A-Za-z0-9])\\.(?=[A-Za-z0-9])"), "qdotq")
+        var s = HindiRoman.toRoman(guarded).lowercase().replace("qdotq", ".").replace("'", "").replace("’", "")
+        s = s.replace(Regex("(?<=[a-z0-9]) dot (?=[a-z]{2,12}\\b)"), ".")   // spoken "flipkart dot com"
+        s = s.replace(Regex("[,;]"), " aur ")
+        s = s.replace(Regex("[^a-z0-9. ]"), " ")
+        s = s.replace(Regex("\\.+(?=\\s|$)"), " ").replace(Regex("(?<=\\s|^)\\.+"), " ")
+        s = s.replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ").trim()
+        // speech-to-text splits and spellings
+        s = s.replace("you tube", "youtube").replace("face book", "facebook").replace("insta gram", "instagram")
+            .replace("whats app", "whatsapp").replace("g mail", "gmail").replace("flip kart", "flipkart")
+            .replace("chat gpt", "chatgpt").replace("web site", "website").replace("play store", "playstore")
+        s = s.replace(Regex("\\b(?:crome|chorme|krome|chrom)\\b"), "chrome")
+        s = s.replace(Regex("\\bdown +lo+a?d\\b"), "download")   // v6.99: "down load" / "down lod" (two words) -> one word
+        return s
+    }
+
+    private fun words(s: String): List<String> = s.split(" ").filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
+
+    private val DOMAIN = Regex("^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*(?:\\.[a-z0-9-]+)*\\.[a-z]{2,12}$")
+    private fun isDomain(t: String) = DOMAIN.matches(t)
+
+    private fun site(name: String): Site? = SITES[name] ?: SITES.entries.firstOrNull { it.key.replace(" ", "") == name.replace(" ", "") }?.value
+
+    private fun known(name: String): Boolean = site(name) != null
+
+    private fun titleCase(s: String) = s.split(" ").filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.joinToString(" ") { it.replaceFirstChar { c -> c.uppercase() } }
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ websites
+
+    /** Web address for a spoken name. [guess] = also accept an unknown one-word name (https://www.<word>.com) or a multi-word name (Google search). */
+    fun siteUrl(name: String, guess: Boolean): String? {
+        val n = name.trim().lowercase()
+        if (n.isEmpty()) return null
+        if (isDomain(n)) return "https://$n"
+        site(n)?.let { return it.url }
+        if (!guess) return null
+        val parts = words(n)
+        if (parts.size == 1 && parts[0].length >= 2 && parts[0].all { it.isLetterOrDigit() }) return "https://www.${parts[0]}.com"
+        if (parts.isNotEmpty() && parts.size <= 4) return "https://www.google.com/search?q=" + URLEncoder.encode(n, "UTF-8")
+        return null
+    }
+
+    fun labelFor(name: String): String {
+        val n = name.trim().lowercase()
+        return site(n)?.label ?: if (isDomain(n)) n else titleCase(n)
+    }
+
+    private fun segment(t: List<String>): List<String> {
+        val out = ArrayList<String>()
+        val unk = ArrayList<String>()
+        fun flush() { if (unk.isNotEmpty()) { out.add(unk.joinToString(" ")); unk.clear() } }
+        var i = 0
+        while (i < t.size) {
+            var hit = 0
+            for (n in minOf(3, t.size - i) downTo 1) {
+                if (known(t.subList(i, i + n).joinToString(" "))) { hit = n; break }
+            }
+            if (hit > 0) { flush(); out.add(t.subList(i, i + hit).joinToString(" ")); i += hit }
+            else if (isDomain(t[i])) { flush(); out.add(t[i]); i++ }
+            else { unk.add(t[i]); i++ }
+        }
+        flush()
+        return out
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * "youtube chrome pe kholo", "youtube google aur flipkart kholo", "flipkart.com kholo", "whatsapp web kholo".
+     * null = not ours (single plain "youtube kholo" stays the normal open-app command; "chrome kholo" / "google chrome kholo" open the Chrome app).
+     */
+    fun parseOpen(raw: String): OpenReq? {
+        if (raw.isBlank() || raw.length > 300) return null
+        val s = Logic.stripWake(norm(raw)).replace(Regex("^(?:please|zara|jara|abhi|ab|ek baar) +"), "").trim()
+        val w = words(s)
+        if (w.isEmpty() || w.size > 30) return null
+        if (w.any { it in NEGATION || it in BLOCK }) return null
+        if (w.none { it in OPEN_VERBS }) return null
+        var chrome = false
+        var siteWord = false
+        var appWord = false
+        val kept = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (x in w) {
+            when {
+                x == "chrome" || x == "browser" -> {
+                    chrome = true
+                    if (kept.size == 1 && kept[0] == "google") kept.clear()   // "google chrome" is the browser's name, not the site
+                }
+                x == "web" && (kept.lastOrNull() == "whatsapp" || kept.lastOrNull() == "telegram") -> kept.add(x)   // "whatsapp web" is one site name
+                x in SITE_WORDS -> siteWord = true
+                x in APP_WORDS -> appWord = true
+                x in OPEN_VERBS || x in FILLER || x in POST -> {}
+                x in CONNECT -> if (kept.isNotEmpty() && kept.last() != "|") kept.add("|")
+                else -> kept.add(x)
+            }
+        }
+        while (kept.isNotEmpty() && kept.last() == "|") kept.removeAt(kept.size - 1)
+        if (kept.isEmpty()) return null
+        if (appWord && !chrome && !siteWord) return null   // "youtube app kholo": the owner asked for the app
+        val chunks = ArrayList<List<String>>()
+        var cur = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (x in kept) { if (x == "|") { if (cur.isNotEmpty()) chunks.add(cur); cur = ArrayList() } else cur.add(x) }
+        if (cur.isNotEmpty()) chunks.add(cur)
+        val names = LinkedHashSet<String>()
+        for (c in chunks) for (n in segment(c)) names.add(n)
+        if (names.isEmpty()) return null
+        val webMode = chrome || siteWord
+        val targets = ArrayList<Target>()
+        for (n in names) {
+            if (n.length > 60 || words(n).size > 4) return null
+            val st = site(n)
+            val domain = isDomain(n)
+            val web = webMode || domain || (st?.webOnly == true)
+            val url = if (web) siteUrl(n, guess = true) else siteUrl(n, guess = false)
+            targets.add(Target(n, labelFor(n), url, web))
+        }
+        if (!webMode && targets.size == 1 && !targets[0].web) return null
+        val truncated = targets.size > MAX_TARGETS
+        return OpenReq(targets.take(MAX_TARGETS), webMode, truncated)
+    }
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Play Store download
+
+    fun packageFor(name: String): Pair<String, String>? = PACKAGES[name.trim().lowercase().replace(" ", "")]
+
+    /** "whatsapp download karo", "nova instagram download kar do play store se", "install karo telegram". */
+    fun parseDownload(raw: String): DownloadReq? {
+        if (raw.isBlank() || raw.length > 300) return null
+        val s = Logic.stripWake(norm(raw)).replace(Regex("^(?:please|zara|jara|abhi|ab) +"), "")
+            .replace("google playstore", " ").replace("playstore", " ").replace("play market", " ").trim()
+        val w = words(s)
+        if (w.isEmpty() || w.size > 20) return null
+        if (w.any { it in DL_BLOCK }) return null
+        if (w.none { it in DL_VERBS }) return null
+        val kept = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (x in w) {
+            when {
+                x in DL_VERBS || x in DL_FILLER || x in POST -> {}
+                x in CONNECT -> if (kept.isNotEmpty() && kept.last() != "|") kept.add("|")
+                else -> kept.add(x)
+            }
+        }
+        while (kept.isNotEmpty() && kept.last() == "|") kept.removeAt(kept.size - 1)
+        if (kept.isEmpty()) return null
+        val names = ArrayList<String>()
+        var cur = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (x in kept) { if (x == "|") { if (cur.isNotEmpty()) names.add(cur.joinToString(" ")); cur = ArrayList() } else cur.add(x) }
+        if (cur.isNotEmpty()) names.add(cur.joinToString(" "))
+        if (names.isEmpty() || names.size > 6) return null
+        if (names.any { it.length > 40 || words(it).size > 4 || it.length < 2 }) return null
+        val first = names[0]
+        val pk = packageFor(first)
+        return DownloadReq(first, pk?.first ?: titleCase(first), pk?.second, names.drop(1).map { packageFor(it)?.first ?: titleCase(it) })
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/** v7.08: the "allow Display over other apps" nudge repeats every 10 minutes while the card still cannot show (it used to be once per service life). Pure Kotlin. */
+object OverlayNudgeRules {
+    const val RENUDGE_MS = 10 * 60_000L
+
+    /** [lastAt] = time of the last nudge, 0 = never. A clock that moved backwards also counts as due. */
+    fun due(lastAt: Long, now: Long): Boolean = lastAt == 0L || now < lastAt || now - lastAt >= RENUDGE_MS
+}
diff --git a/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PaymentReplyRules.kt b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PaymentReplyRules.kt
new file mode 100644

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v6.99 Part 5: money talk in WhatsApp / chat auto reply. NOVA does not ask the owner; it reads the message and answers with a SAFE FIXED
+ * line that fits what was asked. No AI, no amount, no UPI / account detail, no yes / no to paying. Codes (OTP, PIN, CVV, password ...) get no reply at all.
+ * Pure text, no Android. Evidence level: written. Not compiled, not phone tested.
+ */
+object PaymentReplyRules {
+    enum class Kind { SECRET, ASK_MONEY, PAID_CHECK, DETAILS, MONEY }
+    data class Decision(val kind: Kind, val reply: String?)
+
+    private val SECRET = listOf("otp", "pin", "cvv", "password", "passcode", "verification code", "aadhaar", "aadhar", "pan card", "kyc", "card number")
+    private val DETAILS = listOf("upi id", "upi", "account number", "ifsc", "qr", "scanner", "bank details", "gpay number", "phonepe number", "paytm number")
+    private val PAID = listOf("payment ho gaya", "payment ho gayi", "paise mile", "paise mil gaye", "mil gaya", "bhej diya", "bhej di", "kar diya", "received", "paid")
+    private val ASK = listOf("paise bhej", "paisa bhej", "send money", "payment karo", "pay karo", "transfer karo", "bhej do", "udhar", "udhaar", "loan", "lend")
+    private val ASK_ALWAYS_MONEY = listOf("paise bhej", "paisa bhej", "send money", "udhar", "udhaar")
+    private val MONEY = listOf("paisa", "paise", "rupee", "rupees", "rs.", "\u20b9", "payment", "transfer", "amount", "bill", "upi", "gpay", "phonepe", "paytm", "salary", "fees", "fee")
+
+    const val REPLY_ASK = "Abhi main busy hoon, ye baat thodi der mein khud dekhkar batata hoon."
+    const val REPLY_PAID = "Abhi check nahi kar paaya, thodi der mein dekhkar batata hoon."
+    const val REPLY_DETAILS = "Ye details yahan nahi bhej sakta, thodi der mein seedha baat karte hain."
+
+    private fun has(l: String, w: String): Boolean =
+        Regex("(?<![a-z0-9])" + Regex.escape(w) + "(?![a-z0-9])").containsMatchIn(l)
+
+    private fun any(l: String, ws: List<String>): Boolean = ws.any { has(l, it) }
+
+    /** null = not money talk (the normal auto-reply rules decide). */
+    fun decide(text: String): Decision? {
+        val l = text.lowercase().replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ").trim()
+        if (l.isEmpty()) return null
+        if (any(l, SECRET)) return Decision(Kind.SECRET, null)
+        val money = any(l, MONEY)
+        if (!money && !any(l, ASK_ALWAYS_MONEY)) return null
+        return when {
+            any(l, DETAILS) -> Decision(Kind.DETAILS, REPLY_DETAILS)
+            any(l, PAID) -> Decision(Kind.PAID_CHECK, REPLY_PAID)
+            any(l, ASK) -> Decision(Kind.ASK_MONEY, REPLY_ASK)
+            else -> Decision(Kind.MONEY, REPLY_ASK)
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun secretNote(): String = "Code ya password maanga gaya, scam ho sakta hai: jawab nahi diya"
+    fun note(k: Kind): String = "Paise ki baat: safe fixed jawab diya, koi amount ya detail nahi bheji (" + k.name + ")"
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import android.app.Notification
+import android.app.PendingIntent
+import android.content.Context
+import android.content.pm.PackageManager
+import android.media.AudioManager
+import android.os.Build
+import android.provider.Settings
+import android.service.notification.StatusBarNotification
+
+/**
+ * v6.99 Part 1: the Android side of "call uthao / call kaato".
+ * [CallHub] remembers the Answer / Decline buttons of the call notification that is ringing right now
+ * (normal call, WhatsApp, Instagram, Telegram ...). [PhoneActions] presses them; for a normal phone call
+ * with no such button it falls back to TelecomManager (needs the ANSWER_PHONE_CALLS permission).
+ * Rules (which words, which button) are pure and tested in PhoneVoiceRules.
+ */
+object CallHub {
+    private class Ring(val key: String, val pkg: String, val actions: List<Notification.Action>, val at: Long)
+    private var ring: Ring? = null
+    private const val MAX_AGE_MS = 90_000L
+
+    @Synchronized fun onPosted(sbn: StatusBarNotification) {
+        val n = sbn.notification ?: return
+        val ex = n.extras ?: return
+        val title = ex.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_TITLE)?.toString() ?: ""
+        val text = ex.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_TEXT)?.toString() ?: ""
+        val acts = n.actions?.toList() ?: emptyList()
+        val kind = LockGuard.callKind(sbn.packageName ?: "", n.category, title, text, acts.map { it.title?.toString() ?: "" })
+        if (kind == LockGuard.Kind.CALL_RINGING) ring = Ring(sbn.key ?: "", sbn.packageName ?: "", acts, System.currentTimeMillis())
+    }
+
+    @Synchronized fun onRemoved(key: String) { if (ring?.key == key) ring = null }
+
+    @Synchronized private fun current(): Ring? = ring?.takeIf { System.currentTimeMillis() - it.at <= MAX_AGE_MS }
+
+    @Synchronized fun ringing(): Boolean = current() != null
+
+    /** Presses the Answer / Decline button of the ringing call. false = no ringing call, no such button, or the button is gone. */
+    @Synchronized fun press(ctx: Context, answer: Boolean): Boolean {
+        val r = current() ?: return false
+        val i = PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(r.actions.map { it.title?.toString() ?: "" }, answer)
+        if (i < 0) return false
+        val pi: PendingIntent = r.actions[i].actionIntent ?: return false
+        return try { pi.send(); true } catch (e: PendingIntent.CanceledException) { false }
+    }
+}
+
+object PhoneActions {
+    class Res(val ok: Boolean, val code: String)
+
+    fun answerCall(ctx: Context): Res {
+        if (CallHub.press(ctx, true)) return Res(true, "ok")
+        return telecom(ctx, true)
+    }
+
+    fun rejectCall(ctx: Context): Res {
+        if (CallHub.press(ctx, false)) return Res(true, "ok")
+        return telecom(ctx, false)
+    }
+
+    private fun telecom(ctx: Context, answer: Boolean): Res {
+        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 28) return Res(false, "old_android")
+        if (ctx.checkSelfPermission(android.Manifest.permission.ANSWER_PHONE_CALLS) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) return Res(false, "no_permission")
+        return try {
+            val tm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.TELECOM_SERVICE) as android.telecom.TelecomManager
+            if (answer) { tm.acceptRingingCall(); Res(true, "ok") }
+            else if (tm.endCall()) Res(true, "ok") else Res(false, "no_call")
+        } catch (e: SecurityException) { Res(false, "no_permission") }
+        catch (e: Exception) { ErrLog.add("PhoneActions", e.javaClass.simpleName); Res(false, "failed") }
+    }
+
+    /** mode = silent | vibrate | normal. The result is read back from AudioManager: ok only when the ringer really changed. */
+    fun setRinger(ctx: Context, mode: String): Res {
+        val am = ctx.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
+        val want = when (mode) { "silent" -> AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_SILENT; "vibrate" -> AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_VIBRATE; else -> AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_NORMAL }
+        return try {
+            am.ringerMode = want
+            if (am.ringerMode == want) Res(true, "ok") else Res(false, if (want == AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_SILENT) "no_policy" else "mismatch")
+        } catch (e: SecurityException) {
+            // silent / some switches need Do Not Disturb access. Vibrate needs none: do that and say so.
+            if (want == AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_SILENT) {
+                try { am.ringerMode = AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_VIBRATE } catch (e2: Exception) { /* even vibrate refused */ }
+                Res(false, if (am.ringerMode == AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_VIBRATE) "no_policy_vibrate" else "no_policy")
+            } else Res(false, "no_policy")
+        } catch (e: Exception) { ErrLog.add("PhoneActions", "ringer " + e.javaClass.simpleName); Res(false, "failed") }
+    }
+
+    /** Auto-rotate on / off through the system setting (needs "Modify system settings", the same one brightness uses). Read back. */
+    fun setRotation(ctx: Context, on: Boolean): Res {
+        if (!Settings.System.canWrite(ctx)) return Res(false, "no_write")
+        return try {
+            val v = if (on) 1 else 0
+            Settings.System.putInt(ctx.contentResolver, Settings.System.ACCELEROMETER_ROTATION, v)
+            if (Settings.System.getInt(ctx.contentResolver, Settings.System.ACCELEROMETER_ROTATION, -1) == v) Res(true, "ok") else Res(false, "mismatch")
+        } catch (e: Exception) { ErrLog.add("PhoneActions", "rotation " + e.javaClass.simpleName); Res(false, "failed") }
+    }
+
+    fun policyAccess(ctx: Context): Boolean = try {
+        (ctx.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as android.app.NotificationManager).isNotificationPolicyAccessGranted
+    } catch (e: Exception) { false }
+
+    fun failText(code: String, answer: Boolean, en: Boolean): String = when (code) {
+        "no_permission" -> if (en) "I need the Phone (answer calls) permission. Tap Allow in the NOVA app." else "Call uthane ke liye Phone permission chahiye. NOVA app mein Allow dabao."
+        "old_android" -> if (en) "This Android version does not let me do that." else "Is Android version mein ye nahi ho sakta."
+        "no_policy_vibrate" -> if (en) "Silent needs Do Not Disturb access, so I set vibrate. Tap ALLOW DND in the NOVA app for silent." else "Silent ke liye Do Not Disturb access chahiye, isliye vibrate kar diya. Silent ke liye NOVA app mein ALLOW DND dabao."
+        "no_policy" -> if (en) "The ringer did not change. Tap ALLOW DND in the NOVA app." else "Ringer nahi badla. NOVA app mein ALLOW DND dabao."
+        "no_write" -> if (en) "Auto-rotate needs a permission. Tap ALLOW BRIGHTNESS in the NOVA app (it is the same Modify system settings switch)." else "Auto-rotate ke liye permission chahiye. NOVA app mein ALLOW BRIGHTNESS dabao (wahi Modify system settings switch hai)."
+        "mismatch" -> if (en) "I changed it, but the phone did not keep it." else "Maine badla, par phone ne rakha nahi."
+        "failed_shot" -> if (en) "The screenshot did not work. Check NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility)." else "Screenshot nahi ho paya. NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility) check karo."
+        "no_acc" -> if (en) "Turn on NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility) first." else "Pehle NOVA Phone Control (Accessibility) ON karo."
+        "no_call" -> if (en) "No call is ringing right now." else "Abhi koi call nahi aa rahi."
+        else -> if (answer) (if (en) "I could not pick up the call." else "Call utha nahi paya.") else (if (en) "I could not reject the call." else "Call kaat nahi paya.")
+    }
+}
diff --git a/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PhoneFacts.kt b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PhoneFacts.kt

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import android.Manifest
+import android.content.Context
+import android.content.pm.PackageManager
+import android.os.Build
+import android.provider.Settings
+
+/**
+ * v6.99 Part 4: what NOVA knows about THIS phone and about itself, as a short fact list for the online AI chain.
+ * Facts: phone model, Android version, which permissions / accesses are allowed right now, and the exact tap path to allow each one.
+ * Only yes/no status goes out; no key, no contact, no message text. describe() is pure (testable); read() asks Android.
+ * Evidence level: written. Not compiled, not phone tested.
+ */
+object PhoneFacts {
+    /** key -> (readable name, tap path to allow it) */
+    val ACCESS: List<Triple<String, String, String>> = listOf(
+        Triple("mic", "Microphone", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Permissions > Microphone > Allow"),
+        Triple("contacts", "Contacts", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Permissions > Contacts > Allow"),
+        Triple("call", "Phone calls", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Permissions > Phone > Allow"),
+        Triple("answer", "Answer calls", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Permissions > Phone > Allow"),
+        Triple("calendar", "Calendar", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Permissions > Calendar > Allow"),
+        Triple("camera", "Camera", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Permissions > Camera > Allow"),
+        Triple("notif", "Notifications", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Notifications > Allow"),
+        Triple("accessibility", "Accessibility (tap and type in other apps)", "Phone Settings > Accessibility > Installed apps > NOVA > ON"),
+        Triple("listener", "Notification access (read chat messages)", "Phone Settings > Notifications > Notification access > NOVA > ON"),
+        Triple("overlay", "Display over other apps", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Display over other apps > Allow"),
+        Triple("writesettings", "Modify system settings (rotation)", "Phone Settings > Apps > NOVA > Modify system settings > Allow")
+    )
+
+    private const val RULES =
+        "HOW TO ACT: when the user orders a task, do it first and tell the result after; do not ask for permission in words for ordinary tasks. " +
+            "Android itself shows each permission box only once, so if something is not allowed yet, say in one short line which one and the tap path from the list, then do the rest. " +
+            "Never say a task is done unless NOVA really did it. "
+
+    /** Pure: builds the fact text from a status map (missing key = not allowed). */
+    fun describe(model: String, sdk: Int, allowed: Map<String, Boolean>): String {
+        val ok = ACCESS.filter { allowed[it.first] == true }.map { it.second }
+        val no = ACCESS.filter { allowed[it.first] != true }
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        sb.append("ABOUT THIS PHONE: ").append(model.ifBlank { "Android phone" }).append(", Android API ").append(sdk).append(". ")
+        sb.append("Allowed for NOVA now: ").append(if (ok.isEmpty()) "nothing yet" else ok.joinToString(", ")).append(". ")
+        if (no.isNotEmpty()) {
+            sb.append("NOT allowed yet: ").append(no.joinToString("; ") { it.second + " -> " + it.third }).append(". ")
+        }
+        sb.append(RULES)
+        return sb.toString()
+    }
+
+    private fun granted(ctx: Context, p: String): Boolean = try {
+        ctx.checkSelfPermission(p) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
+    } catch (e: Throwable) { false }
+
+    fun read(ctx: Context): Map<String, Boolean> {
+        val m = HashMap<String, Boolean>()
+        m["mic"] = granted(ctx, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO)
+        m["contacts"] = granted(ctx, Manifest.permission.READ_CONTACTS)
+        m["call"] = granted(ctx, Manifest.permission.CALL_PHONE)
+        m["answer"] = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 28 || granted(ctx, Manifest.permission.ANSWER_PHONE_CALLS)
+        m["calendar"] = granted(ctx, Manifest.permission.READ_CALENDAR)
+        m["camera"] = granted(ctx, Manifest.permission.CAMERA)
+        m["notif"] = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 33 || granted(ctx, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
+        m["accessibility"] = try { NovaAccessibilityService.isEnabled(ctx) } catch (e: Throwable) { false }
+        m["listener"] = try {
+            (Settings.Secure.getString(ctx.contentResolver, "enabled_notification_listeners") ?: "").contains(ctx.packageName)
+        } catch (e: Throwable) { false }
+        m["overlay"] = try { Settings.canDrawOverlays(ctx) } catch (e: Throwable) { false }
+        m["writesettings"] = try { Settings.System.canWrite(ctx) } catch (e: Throwable) { false }
+        return m
+    }
+
+    fun aiFacts(ctx: Context): String = try {
+        describe(Build.MODEL ?: "", Build.VERSION.SDK_INT, read(ctx))
+    } catch (e: Throwable) { RULES }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v6.99 PHONE VOICE RULES. Pure Kotlin (no Android classes), unit tested.
+ * Voice commands the audit found missing: answer / reject a call, screenshot, ringer mode, auto-rotation.
+ * Result is a [Logic.Cmd] so skills, history and every other path treat it like any other command.
+ *
+ * Kinds: "call_answer", "call_reject", "screenshot", "ringer" (arg silent|vibrate|normal), "rotation" (arg on|off).
+ * Runs BEFORE the "call <name>" rule, so "call uthao" never dials a contact called "uthao".
+ */
+object PhoneVoiceRules {
+    const val K_ANSWER = "call_answer"
+    const val K_REJECT = "call_reject"
+    const val K_SHOT = "screenshot"
+    const val K_RINGER = "ringer"
+    const val K_ROTATION = "rotation"
+
+    val KINDS = setOf(K_ANSWER, K_REJECT, K_SHOT, K_RINGER, K_ROTATION)
+
+    /** Kinds that Logic.classify hands out: Part 1 = calls, Part 2 = screenshot / ringer / rotation. */
+    val ACTIVE = KINDS
+
+    private val NEG = setOf("mat", "nahi", "nahin", "nhi", "nai", "dont", "don", "not", "never", "bina", "mt")
+    private val CALLW = setOf("call", "phone", "fone", "फोन", "कॉल")
+    private val ANSWER = setOf("uthao", "utha", "uthalo", "uthana", "uthaiye", "uthayiye", "receive", "recieve", "accept", "answer", "pickup", "pick", "attend", "rieceive", "utho")
+    private val REJECT = setOf("kaato", "kaat", "kat", "katt", "cut", "reject", "decline", "disconnect", "hangup", "hang", "cancel", "kaatdo", "katdo", "kato", "end", "drop")
+    private val SEND_WORDS = setOf("delete", "hatao", "bhejo", "send", "share", "analyze", "analyse", "dekho", "padho", "read")
+
+    private fun toks(norm: String): List<String> = norm.split(" ").filter { it.isNotBlank() }
+
+    /** [norm] = Logic.norm(text) with the wake word removed. null = not one of these commands. */
+    fun parse(norm: String): Logic.Cmd? {
+        val t = toks(norm)
+        if (t.isEmpty() || t.size > 7) return null
+        if (t.any { it in NEG }) return null
+        val s = t.toSet()
+        fun has(vararg w: String) = w.any { it in s }
+
+        // ---- call answer / reject
+        val callWord = has("call", "phone", "fone")
+        if (callWord || (has("hang") && has("up")) || (has("pick") && has("up"))) {
+            val phoneOnly = !has("call") && has("phone", "fone")
+            val ans = has(*ANSWER.toTypedArray()) || (has("le") && has("lo", "lijiye", "lijie"))
+            val rej = has(*REJECT.toTypedArray()) || (has("band") && has("call"))
+            // "phone band karo" is a power question, not a call: band needs the word call
+            if (ans && !rej) return Logic.Cmd(K_ANSWER)
+            if (rej && !ans) {
+                if (phoneOnly && has("band", "end", "drop", "cancel")) return null
+                return Logic.Cmd(K_REJECT)
+            }
+        }
+        if (norm == "hang up" || norm == "end call" || norm == "hangup") return Logic.Cmd(K_REJECT)
+
+        // ---- screenshot
+        val shot = has("screenshot", "screenshots") || (has("screen") && has("shot")) || (has("ss") && has("lo", "le", "karo", "take"))
+        if (shot && t.none { it in SEND_WORDS } && t.size <= 5) return Logic.Cmd(K_SHOT)
+
+        // ---- ringer mode
+        val ringerCtx = has("ringer", "ringtone") || (has("mode") && has("silent", "vibrate", "vibration", "normal", "general", "ring")) ||
+            (has("phone", "mobile", "fone") && has("silent", "vibrate", "vibration"))
+        if (ringerCtx && !has("replies", "reply", "voice", "quiet", "notification", "notifications", "disturb", "dnd", "volume", "alarm")) {
+            val mode = when {
+                has("silent", "mute", "off", "band") -> "silent"
+                has("vibrate", "vibration", "vib") -> "vibrate"
+                has("normal", "general", "ring", "on", "chalu", "loud", "sound") -> "normal"
+                else -> null
+            }
+            if (mode != null) return Logic.Cmd(K_RINGER, mode)
+        }
+
+        // ---- auto rotation
+        val rot = has("rotation", "rotate", "autorotate", "ghumao") || (has("auto") && has("rotate", "rotation"))
+        if (rot && t.size <= 6) {
+            if (has("off", "band", "bandh", "disable", "lock", "stop")) return Logic.Cmd(K_ROTATION, "off")
+            if (has("on", "chalu", "enable", "start", "unlock")) return Logic.Cmd(K_ROTATION, "on")
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Which notification action button to use. [titles] = the visible button titles of the call notification.
+     * Answer words: answer / accept / उठा... ; decline words: decline / reject / dismiss / hang up. -1 = none.
+     */
+    fun pickAction(titles: List<String>, answer: Boolean): Int {
+        val ans = listOf("answer", "accept", "pick up", "pickup", "उठाएं", "उठाओ", "जवाब दें", "उत्तर दें", "स्वीकार")
+        val dec = listOf("decline", "reject", "dismiss", "hang up", "hangup", "end call", "अस्वीकार", "अस्वीकृत", "काटें", "कट करें", "कॉल समाप्त")
+        val want = if (answer) ans else dec
+        val other = if (answer) dec else ans
+        titles.forEachIndexed { i, ti ->
+            val l = ti.lowercase()
+            if (want.any { it in l } && other.none { it in l }) return i
+        }
+        return -1
+    }
+
+    /** Spoken confirmations (Hinglish / English). */
+    fun doneText(kind: String, arg: String, en: Boolean): String = when (kind) {
+        K_ANSWER -> if (en) "Call picked up" else "Call utha liya"
+        K_REJECT -> if (en) "Call rejected" else "Call kaat diya"
+        K_SHOT -> if (en) "Screenshot saved to the gallery" else "Screenshot gallery mein save ho gaya"
+        K_RINGER -> when (arg) {
+            "silent" -> if (en) "Phone is on silent" else "Phone silent par hai"
+            "vibrate" -> if (en) "Phone is on vibrate" else "Phone vibrate par hai"
+            else -> if (en) "Ringer is on" else "Ringer chalu hai"
+        }
+        K_ROTATION -> if (arg == "on") (if (en) "Auto-rotate is on" else "Auto-rotate chalu hai") else (if (en) "Auto-rotate is off" else "Auto-rotate band hai")
+        else -> if (en) "Done" else "Ho gaya"
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.05 I: "is photo ko professional banao", "background hatao", "black and white karo", "square crop karo".
+ * Pure Kotlin (no Android classes), Roman text. It only decides what edit is wanted; it never sends anything anywhere.
+ * It never steals "ek billi ki photo banao" (ImageIntent), sharing / sending / camera / gallery sentences.
+ */
+object PhotoEditIntent {
+    enum class Kind { ENHANCE, BRIGHT, FILTER, CROP, BG_REMOVE, BG_REPLACE, REMOVE_OBJECT, RESTYLE, ORIGINAL, AGAIN }
+    /** preset = offline preset name (PhotoPro.Preset), ratio = crop "w:h" or "". needsAi = Level 2 would help (the phone-only result is the honest fallback). */
+    class Parsed(val kind: Kind, val preset: String, val ratio: String, val blocked: Boolean, val needsAi: Boolean)
+
+    private val REF = setOf("photo", "foto", "image", "pic", "picture", "tasveer", "isko", "ise", "iski", "isse", "yeh", "ye")
+    private val NOT = setOf("banao", "bana", "banado", "generate", "create", "draw", "paint", "bhejo", "send", "share", "whatsapp", "instagram", "call", "camera", "kheencho", "click", "gallery", "chuno", "kholo", "open", "website", "site", "video", "reel", "record")
+    private val BLOCK = setOf("nude", "naked", "nsfw", "porn", "porno", "sex", "sexy", "xxx", "nangi", "nanga", "erotic", "boobs", "gore", "deepfake")
+    private val BG = setOf("background", "bg", "backdrop", "peeche")
+    private val REMOVE = setOf("hatao", "hata", "hatado", "remove", "erase", "mitao", "mita", "nikalo", "nikal", "delete")
+    private val CHANGE = setOf("badlo", "badal", "badlo", "change", "replace", "naya", "nayi", "studio", "white", "safed", "blur")
+    private val PRO = setOf("professional", "pro", "saaf", "sundar", "badhiya", "badiya", "enhance", "sharp", "hd", "glow", "behtar", "better", "improve", "fix", "theek", "clear", "quality", "edit", "sudhaar", "sudhar", "mast")
+    private val BRIGHT = setOf("bright", "roshan", "andhera", "dark", "ujala", "brighten", "light")
+    private val BW = setOf("bw", "monochrome", "grayscale", "greyscale")
+    private val SEPIA = setOf("sepia", "vintage", "purani", "retro")
+    private val CINE = setOf("cinematic", "cinema", "movie", "film")
+    private val WARM = setOf("warm", "garam", "golden")
+    private val COOL = setOf("cool", "thanda", "thandi")
+    private val VIVID = setOf("vivid", "colorful", "colourful", "rangeen", "colors", "colours", "color", "colour")
+    private val SOFT = setOf("soft", "smooth", "naram", "dreamy")
+    private val CROP = setOf("crop", "kato", "kaato", "kaat", "cut", "trim")
+    private val RESTYLE = setOf("cartoon", "anime", "sketch", "painting", "ghibli", "oil", "style", "pixar", "3d")
+    private val ORIG = setOf("pehle", "original", "undo", "wapas", "purana")
+    private val AGAIN = setOf("aur", "more", "again", "phir", "dobara", "zyada")
+
+    fun tokens(s: String): List<String> {
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        for (ch in s.lowercase()) { if ((ch in 'a'..'z') || (ch in '0'..'9')) sb.append(ch) else sb.append(' ') }
+        val out = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (w in sb.toString().split(' ')) if (w.isNotEmpty()) out.add(w)
+        return out
+    }
+
+    private fun ratioOf(text: String, t: List<String>): String {
+        val m = Regex("(\\d{1,2})\\s*[:x]\\s*(\\d{1,2})").find(text.lowercase())
+        if (m != null) {
+            val a = m.groupValues[1].toInt(); val b = m.groupValues[2].toInt()
+            if (a in 1..32 && b in 1..32) return "$a:$b"
+        }
+        if ("square" in t) return "1:1"
+        if ("wide" in t || "landscape" in t) return "16:9"
+        if ("portrait" in t) return "4:5"
+        return "1:1"
+    }
+
+    /** null = not a photo-edit sentence. The caller must also have a picked photo. */
+    fun parse(text: String): Parsed? {
+        val t = tokens(text)
+        if (t.isEmpty() || t.size > 30) return null
+        // v7.12: "is photo ko professional banao" = edit (object marker "ko" + photo word + quality word); "banao" alone still means generate.
+        val makeOk = ("ko" in t || "ki" in t && "quality" in t) && t.any { it in REF } && t.any { it in PRO }
+        for (w in t) if (w in NOT && !(makeOk && (w == "banao" || w == "bana" || w == "banado"))) return null
+        val blocked = t.any { it in BLOCK }
+        val ref = t.any { it in REF }
+        fun has(s: Set<String>) = t.any { it in s }
+        fun p(k: Kind, preset: String = "", ratio: String = "", ai: Boolean = false) = Parsed(k, preset, ratio, blocked, ai)
+        if (has(BG) && (has(REMOVE))) return p(Kind.BG_REMOVE, "ENHANCE", ai = true)
+        if (has(BG) && has(CHANGE)) return p(Kind.BG_REPLACE, "ENHANCE", ai = true)
+        if (has(RESTYLE)) return p(Kind.RESTYLE, "VIVID", ai = true)
+        if (has(CROP) || "square" in t) return p(Kind.CROP, "", ratioOf(text, t))
+        if (has(BW) || ("black" in t && "white" in t)) return p(Kind.FILTER, "BW")
+        if (has(SEPIA)) return p(Kind.FILTER, "SEPIA")
+        if (has(CINE)) return p(Kind.FILTER, "CINEMATIC")
+        if (has(WARM)) return p(Kind.FILTER, "WARM")
+        if (has(COOL)) return p(Kind.FILTER, "COOL")
+        if (has(SOFT) && ref) return p(Kind.FILTER, "SOFT")
+        if (has(VIVID) && ref) return p(Kind.FILTER, "VIVID")
+        if (has(REMOVE) && ref && !has(BG)) return p(Kind.REMOVE_OBJECT, "ENHANCE", ai = true)
+        if (has(ORIG) && (ref || t.size <= 3)) return p(Kind.ORIGINAL)
+        if (has(BRIGHT) && ref) return p(Kind.BRIGHT, "BRIGHT")
+        if (has(PRO) && ref) return p(Kind.ENHANCE, "ENHANCE")
+        if (t.size <= 4 && has(AGAIN) && (has(PRO) || has(setOf("sundar")))) return p(Kind.AGAIN, "ENHANCE")
+        return null
+    }
+}
diff --git a/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PhotoPro.kt b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PhotoPro.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..55237e1
