+++ b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PhotoPro.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,176 @@
+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import android.graphics.Bitmap
+import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
+
+/**
+ * v7.05 I level 1: on-device professional photo edit. NO network, NO key, nothing leaves the phone.
+ * The maths works on ARGB int arrays (pure Kotlin, JVM unit tested); only decode / encode touch android.graphics.
+ * Presets: ENHANCE (auto levels, contrast, skin-safe saturation, light noise reduction, unsharp mask), BRIGHT (dark photo fix),
+ * BW, SEPIA, CINEMATIC, WARM, COOL, VIVID, SOFT. Crop to a ratio. Never touches the original: callers keep their own copy.
+ */
+object PhotoPro {
+    enum class Preset { ENHANCE, BRIGHT, BW, SEPIA, CINEMATIC, WARM, COOL, VIVID, SOFT }
+
+    private fun clamp(v: Float): Int = if (v < 0f) 0 else if (v > 255f) 255 else (v + 0.5f).toInt()
+    private fun r(p: Int) = (p shr 16) and 0xFF
+    private fun g(p: Int) = (p shr 8) and 0xFF
+    private fun b(p: Int) = p and 0xFF
+    private fun rgb(a: Int, r: Int, g: Int, b: Int) = (a shl 24) or (r shl 16) or (g shl 8) or b
+    private fun lum(p: Int): Float = 0.299f * r(p) + 0.587f * g(p) + 0.114f * b(p)
+
+    /** Crop window (x, y, w, h) of the biggest centred area with the ratio rw:rh. */
+    fun cropRect(w: Int, h: Int, rw: Int, rh: Int): IntArray {
+        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0 || rw <= 0 || rh <= 0) return intArrayOf(0, 0, w, h)
+        var cw = w
+        var ch = (w.toLong() * rh / rw).toInt()
+        if (ch > h) { ch = h; cw = (h.toLong() * rw / rh).toInt() }
+        cw = cw.coerceIn(1, w); ch = ch.coerceIn(1, h)
+        return intArrayOf((w - cw) / 2, (h - ch) / 2, cw, ch)
+    }
+
+    /** Stretch each channel's 1%..99% range to 0..255 (keeps colour balance by using ONE common range from luminance). */
+    fun autoLevels(px: IntArray) {
+        if (px.isEmpty()) return
+        val hist = IntArray(256)
+        for (p in px) hist[lum(p).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)]++
+        val cut = (px.size * 0.01f).toInt()
+        var lo = 0; var acc = 0
+        while (lo < 255 && acc + hist[lo] <= cut) { acc += hist[lo]; lo++ }
+        var hi = 255; acc = 0
+        while (hi > 0 && acc + hist[hi] <= cut) { acc += hist[hi]; hi-- }
+        if (hi - lo < 16) return
+        val sc = 255f / (hi - lo)
+        for (i in px.indices) {
+            val p = px[i]
+            px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, clamp((r(p) - lo) * sc), clamp((g(p) - lo) * sc), clamp((b(p) - lo) * sc))
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Gentle S-curve: amount 0..1 (0 = none). */
+    fun contrast(px: IntArray, amount: Float) {
+        val lut = IntArray(256) { v ->
+            val x = v / 255f
+            val s = x * x * (3f - 2f * x)
+            clamp(255f * (x + (s - x) * amount))
+        }
+        for (i in px.indices) { val p = px[i]; px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, lut[r(p)], lut[g(p)], lut[b(p)]) }
+    }
+
+    /** factor 1 = same. Pixels that look like skin (r > g > b, moderate saturation) get only half of the change, so faces do not turn orange. */
+    fun saturation(px: IntArray, factor: Float) {
+        for (i in px.indices) {
+            val p = px[i]
+            val l = lum(p); val rr = r(p); val gg = g(p); val bb = b(p)
+            val mx = maxOf(rr, maxOf(gg, bb)); val mn = minOf(rr, minOf(gg, bb))
+            val sat = if (mx == 0) 0f else (mx - mn) / mx.toFloat()
+            val skin = rr > gg && gg > bb && sat in 0.15f..0.6f && rr > 90
+            val f = if (skin) 1f + (factor - 1f) * 0.5f else factor
+            px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, clamp(l + (rr - l) * f), clamp(l + (gg - l) * f), clamp(l + (bb - l) * f))
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun brightness(px: IntArray, gamma: Float) {   // gamma < 1 brightens
+        val lut = IntArray(256) { v -> clamp(255f * Math.pow((v / 255f).toDouble(), gamma.toDouble()).toFloat()) }
+        for (i in px.indices) { val p = px[i]; px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, lut[r(p)], lut[g(p)], lut[b(p)]) }
+    }
+
+    fun tint(px: IntArray, dr: Int, dg: Int, db: Int) {
+        for (i in px.indices) { val p = px[i]; px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, clamp((r(p) + dr).toFloat()), clamp((g(p) + dg).toFloat()), clamp((b(p) + db).toFloat())) }
+    }
+
+    fun grayscale(px: IntArray) {
+        for (i in px.indices) { val p = px[i]; val l = clamp(lum(p)); px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, l, l, l) }
+    }
+
+    fun sepia(px: IntArray) {
+        for (i in px.indices) {
+            val p = px[i]; val rr = r(p).toFloat(); val gg = g(p).toFloat(); val bb = b(p).toFloat()
+            px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, clamp(0.393f * rr + 0.769f * gg + 0.189f * bb), clamp(0.349f * rr + 0.686f * gg + 0.168f * bb), clamp(0.272f * rr + 0.534f * gg + 0.131f * bb))
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun vignette(px: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, strength: Float) {
+        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return
+        val cx = w / 2f; val cy = h / 2f; val maxD = Math.sqrt((cx * cx + cy * cy).toDouble()).toFloat()
+        for (y in 0 until h) for (x in 0 until w) {
+            val d = Math.sqrt(((x - cx) * (x - cx) + (y - cy) * (y - cy)).toDouble()).toFloat() / maxD
+            val f = 1f - strength * d * d
+            val i = y * w + x; val p = px[i]
+            px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, clamp(r(p) * f), clamp(g(p) * f), clamp(b(p) * f))
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** 3x3 box blur copy (edges clamped). */
+    fun blur3(px: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int): IntArray {
+        val out = IntArray(px.size)
+        for (y in 0 until h) for (x in 0 until w) {
+            var sr = 0; var sg = 0; var sb = 0; var n = 0
+            for (dy in -1..1) { val yy = y + dy; if (yy < 0 || yy >= h) continue
+                for (dx in -1..1) { val xx = x + dx; if (xx < 0 || xx >= w) continue
+                    val p = px[yy * w + xx]; sr += r(p); sg += g(p); sb += b(p); n++ } }
+            out[y * w + x] = rgb(px[y * w + x] ushr 24, sr / n, sg / n, sb / n)
+        }
+        return out
+    }
+
+    /** Light noise reduction (mix with the blurred copy) then unsharp mask. amount ~0.6 = professional, not crunchy. */
+    fun denoiseSharpen(px: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, denoise: Float, sharp: Float) {
+        if (w < 3 || h < 3) return
+        val bl = blur3(px, w, h)
+        for (i in px.indices) {
+            val p = px[i]; val q = bl[i]
+            px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, clamp(r(p) + (r(q) - r(p)) * denoise), clamp(g(p) + (g(q) - g(p)) * denoise), clamp(b(p) + (b(q) - b(p)) * denoise))
+        }
+        val bl2 = blur3(px, w, h)
+        for (i in px.indices) {
+            val p = px[i]; val q = bl2[i]
+            px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, clamp(r(p) + (r(p) - r(q)) * sharp), clamp(g(p) + (g(p) - g(q)) * sharp), clamp(b(p) + (b(p) - b(q)) * sharp))
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** All presets on an ARGB array, in place. */
+    fun apply(px: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, preset: Preset) {
+        when (preset) {
+            Preset.ENHANCE -> { autoLevels(px); contrast(px, 0.25f); saturation(px, 1.12f); denoiseSharpen(px, w, h, 0.2f, 0.6f) }
+            Preset.BRIGHT -> { autoLevels(px); brightness(px, 0.7f); contrast(px, 0.15f); denoiseSharpen(px, w, h, 0.25f, 0.4f) }
+            Preset.BW -> { autoLevels(px); grayscale(px); contrast(px, 0.3f) }
+            Preset.SEPIA -> { autoLevels(px); sepia(px) }
+            Preset.CINEMATIC -> { autoLevels(px); contrast(px, 0.45f); saturation(px, 0.85f); tint(px, -6, 0, 10); vignette(px, w, h, 0.45f) }
+            Preset.WARM -> { tint(px, 14, 4, -12); autoLevels(px) }
+            Preset.COOL -> { tint(px, -12, 0, 14); autoLevels(px) }
+            Preset.VIVID -> { autoLevels(px); contrast(px, 0.3f); saturation(px, 1.4f); denoiseSharpen(px, w, h, 0.1f, 0.5f) }
+            Preset.SOFT -> { val bl = blur3(px, w, h); for (i in px.indices) { val p = px[i]; val q = bl[i]; px[i] = rgb(p ushr 24, (r(p) + r(q)) / 2, (g(p) + g(q)) / 2, (b(p) + b(q)) / 2) }; brightness(px, 0.9f) }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Android part: JPEG bytes in, JPEG bytes out (null on any failure, incl. OutOfMemory). ratio = (rw, rh) crops first; preset may be null for crop only.
+     * Works on a copy, off the main thread (the caller starts a Thread).
+     */
+    fun run(raw: ByteArray, preset: Preset?, ratio: IntArray? = null, maxSide: Int = 2048): ByteArray? {
+        return try {
+            val sm = ImageGen.shrink(raw, maxSide) ?: return null
+            var bmp: Bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(sm, 0, sm.size) ?: return null
+            if (ratio != null && ratio.size == 2) {
+                val c = cropRect(bmp.width, bmp.height, ratio[0], ratio[1])
+                val cropped = Bitmap.createBitmap(bmp, c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3])
+                bmp = cropped
+            }
+            val w = bmp.width; val h = bmp.height
+            val px = IntArray(w * h)
+            bmp.getPixels(px, 0, w, 0, 0, w, h)
+            if (preset != null) apply(px, w, h, preset)
+            val out = Bitmap.createBitmap(w, h, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
+            out.setPixels(px, 0, w, 0, 0, w, h)
+            val bo = java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()
+            out.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 93, bo)
+            bo.toByteArray()
+        } catch (e: OutOfMemoryError) {
+            null
+        } catch (e: Throwable) {
+            ErrLog.add("PhotoPro", e.toString())
+            null
+        }
+    }
+}
diff --git a/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PlayInstallRules.kt b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PlayInstallRules.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..67e4c61
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/PlayInstallRules.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,110 @@
+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v6.97 wip14 (Part 3): pure rules for the narrow Play Store "Install" press. No Android classes, so it is unit tested.
+ *
+ * What this file decides (NovaAccessibilityService.playInstall will act on it in Part 4):
+ *  - arm / armedFor: the press is allowed only inside a 60 s window that NOVA opened itself for ONE package.
+ *  - pageMatches: the Play page on screen really is the app that was asked for (exact title, never a lookalike).
+ *  - decide: from the screen text only, TAP / STARTED / INSTALLED / NEED_LOGIN / PAID / WAIT.
+ *  - searchResultMatches: for an UNKNOWN app name, a search result is accepted only when its title is exactly the spoken name.
+ *
+ * It never presses anything. Only an exact "Install" line can ever give TAP. Price / Buy, Sign in, "Install anyway" and Update never do.
+ * Limit (honest): button words are matched in English. A Play Store in another language gives WAIT, never a guess.
+ */
+object PlayInstallRules {
+    const val WINDOW_MS = 60_000L
+
+    enum class Decision { TAP, STARTED, INSTALLED, NEED_LOGIN, PAID, WAIT }
+
+    private val ARMED = HashMap<String, Long>()
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ armed window (per package)
+
+    fun arm(pkg: String, now: Long, store: MutableMap<String, Long> = ARMED) {
+        val p = pkg.trim()
+        if (p.isEmpty()) return
+        store[p] = now
+    }
+
+    fun armedFor(pkg: String, now: Long, store: MutableMap<String, Long> = ARMED): Boolean {
+        val p = pkg.trim()
+        if (p.isEmpty()) return false
+        val at = store[p] ?: return false
+        val age = now - at
+        return age in 0..WINDOW_MS
+    }
+
+    fun disarm(pkg: String, store: MutableMap<String, Long> = ARMED) {
+        store.remove(pkg.trim())
+    }
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ screen text helpers
+
+    private fun clean(raw: String): String =
+        raw.replace("\u200B", "").replace("\u00A0", " ").trim().lowercase()
+            .removeSuffix("...").removeSuffix("\u2026").trim()
+            .replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ")
+
+    /** Only short lines can be a button label. A long line is description text and is ignored. */
+    private fun buttons(lines: List<String>): Set<String> =
+        lines.map { clean(it) }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() && it.length <= 40 }.toSet()
+
+    private val PRICE = Regex("^(?:[\\u20B9$\\u20AC\\u00A3]|rs\\.?|inr|usd)\\s?\\d[\\d,.]*$")
+    private val PROGRESS = setOf("installing", "downloading", "pending", "waiting for download", "waiting to download", "installing app", "download pending")
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ page + search matching
+
+    private val TITLE_SUFFIX = setOf("messenger", "app", "official")
+    private val NAME_FILLER = setOf("app", "apps", "the", "official", "play", "store")
+
+    private fun nameWords(s: String): List<String> =
+        s.lowercase().replace(Regex("[^\\p{L}\\p{N} ]"), " ").split(" ").filter { it.isNotEmpty() && it !in NAME_FILLER }
+
+    /**
+     * True when one line on screen is the app's title: exactly [label], or [label] plus one harmless word ("WhatsApp Messenger").
+     * "WhatsApp Business", "YouTube Music", "Instagram Lite" are different apps and do NOT match "WhatsApp" / "YouTube" / "Instagram".
+     */
+    fun pageMatches(lines: List<String>, label: String): Boolean {
+        val want = clean(label)
+        if (want.isEmpty()) return false
+        return lines.map { clean(it) }.any { l ->
+            l == want || (l.startsWith("$want ") && l.removePrefix("$want ") in TITLE_SUFFIX)
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Unknown app: a Play search result is accepted only when its cleaned words equal the cleaned spoken words. */
+    fun searchResultMatches(resultTitle: String, spokenName: String): Boolean {
+        val a = nameWords(resultTitle)
+        val b = nameWords(spokenName)
+        return a.isNotEmpty() && a == b
+    }
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the decision
+
+    /**
+     * [afterTap] = false: look at the page BEFORE pressing. true: look again AFTER the press.
+     * Order matters: anything unsafe wins over TAP.
+     */
+    fun decide(lines: List<String>, afterTap: Boolean): Decision {
+        val b = buttons(lines)
+        if (b.any { it.startsWith("install anyway") }) return Decision.WAIT      // a warning dialog: never press it
+        val hasInstall = "install" in b
+        val installed = "open" in b && ("uninstall" in b || "update" in b) && !hasInstall
+        if (afterTap) {
+            if (b.any { it in PROGRESS }) return Decision.STARTED
+            if ("cancel" in b && !hasInstall) return Decision.STARTED
+            if (installed && "uninstall" in b) return Decision.STARTED           // finished so fast that only Open is left
+        } else {
+            if (installed) return Decision.INSTALLED
+        }
+        if (b.any { it == "sign in" || it == "log in" || it == "add account" || it.startsWith("sign in to") }) return Decision.NEED_LOGIN
+        if (!hasInstall && b.any { PRICE.matches(it) || it == "buy" || it == "buy now" }) return Decision.PAID
+        if (!afterTap && hasInstall) return Decision.TAP
+        return Decision.WAIT
+    }
+
+    /** The one gate NovaAccessibilityService must pass before it presses: armed for this package AND the right app page AND TAP. */
+    fun mayTap(pkg: String, label: String, lines: List<String>, now: Long, store: MutableMap<String, Long> = ARMED): Boolean =
+        armedFor(pkg, now, store) && pageMatches(lines, label) && decide(lines, afterTap = false) == Decision.TAP
+}

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7d294f1

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.05 H: which place does the owner want the website on? "nova website vercel par publish karo", "lovable par daalo".
+ * Pure Kotlin (no Android classes), Roman text. It only decides; the caller asks before anything leaves the phone.
+ * GitHub stays the old SiteIntent.isPublish route; this only adds Vercel and Lovable.
+ */
+object PublishTarget {
+    enum class Target { VERCEL, LOVABLE }
+
+    private val VERCEL = setOf("vercel", "versel", "varcel")
+    private val LOVABLE = setOf("lovable", "lavable", "lovabel")
+    private val PUB = setOf("publish", "publis", "deploy", "upload", "host", "live", "daalo", "dalo", "daal", "rakho", "chalao", "bhejo", "send")
+    private val BUILD = setOf("banao", "bana", "banado", "banade", "banana", "banani", "banane", "bnao", "generate", "create", "build", "make", "design")
+    private val BLOCK = setOf("delete", "hatao", "remove", "kholo", "open")
+
+    fun tokens(s: String): List<String> {
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        for (ch in s.lowercase()) { if ((ch in 'a'..'z') || (ch in '0'..'9')) sb.append(ch) else sb.append(' ') }
+        val out = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (w in sb.toString().split(' ')) if (w.isNotEmpty()) out.add(w)
+        return out
+    }
+
+    fun parse(text: String): Target? {
+        val t = tokens(text)
+        if (t.isEmpty() || t.size > 30) return null
+        var v = false; var l = false; var pub = false
+        for (w in t) {
+            if (w in BUILD || w in BLOCK) return null
+            if (w in VERCEL) v = true
+            if (w in LOVABLE) l = true
+            if (w in PUB) pub = true
+        }
+        if (!pub) return null
+        return if (v) Target.VERCEL else if (l) Target.LOVABLE else null
+    }
+}
diff --git a/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/ScheduleRestoreRules.kt b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/ScheduleRestoreRules.kt

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.12 AUTOPSY R4 (pure Kotlin, no Android classes): what to do with a saved scheduled job when alarms are re-armed
+ * (after a reboot, an app update, a clock / zone change, or when the app is opened), and which system broadcast does what in BootReceiver.
+ */
+object ScheduleRestoreRules {
+    enum class Plan { KEEP, MOVE_NEXT, FIRE_NOW, DROP_MISSED }
+
+    /** Past-due by up to 10 minutes still fires right away (the phone was only just back on, or Android delayed the alarm); older one-shot jobs are reported as missed. */
+    const val GRACE_MS = 600_000L
+
+    fun planFor(nextAtMs: Long, repeating: Boolean, nowMs: Long): Plan = when {
+        nextAtMs > nowMs -> Plan.KEEP
+        repeating -> Plan.MOVE_NEXT
+        nowMs - nextAtMs <= GRACE_MS -> Plan.FIRE_NOW
+        else -> Plan.DROP_MISSED
+    }
+
+    class BootPlan(val resetActive: Boolean, val rearm: Boolean, val askToStart: Boolean)
+
+    /** null = not an action this receiver handles. Only a real boot switches NOVA to OFF and asks for the start tap. */
+    fun bootPlan(action: String?): BootPlan? = when (action) {
+        "android.intent.action.BOOT_COMPLETED", "android.intent.action.QUICKBOOT_POWERON" -> BootPlan(true, true, true)
+        "android.intent.action.MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED", "android.intent.action.TIME_SET", "android.intent.action.TIMEZONE_CHANGED",
+        "android.app.action.SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM_PERMISSION_STATE_CHANGED" -> BootPlan(false, true, false)
+        else -> null
+    }
+}

+    /** Makes the AI pictures a page asks for (max 4, only while the build is young); failures simply stay pixels. */
+    private fun aiPictures(ctx: Context, template: String, start: Long, allowNew: Boolean): Map<String, String> {
+        val wanted = SiteMedia.placeholders(template).take(4)
+        if (wanted.isEmpty()) return emptyMap()
+        for (d in wanted) {
+            if (aiCache.containsKey(d) || !allowNew) continue
+            if (System.currentTimeMillis() - start > 170_000L) break
+            try {
+                val r = ImageGen.generate(ctx, d, null)
+                val raw = r.img
+                if (r.ok && raw != null) {
+                    val small = ImageGen.shrink(raw, 1024) ?: raw
+                    aiCache[d] = "data:image/jpeg;base64," + ImageGen.b64(small)
+                }
+            } catch (e: Throwable) { }
+        }
+        val out = HashMap<String, String>()
+        for (d in wanted) aiCache[d]?.let { out[d] = it }
+        return out
+    }

+    // ===== v7.08 PART 5 (add-only, pure Kotlin, no Android classes): AI picture placeholders + owner-picked media =====
+    class UserApplied(val html: String, val placed: List<String>, val missing: List<String>)
+
+    private val IMG_PH = Regex("NOVA_IMG:([^\"'<>)]+)")
+
+    /** Distinct NOVA_IMG:<description> placeholders of a page, in order of first appearance. */
+    fun placeholders(html: String): List<String> {
+        val out = LinkedHashSet<String>()
+        for (m in IMG_PH.findAll(html)) { val d = clean(m.groupValues[1]); if (d.isNotEmpty()) out.add(d) }
+        return out.toList()
+    }
+
+    /** One short line (max 160 chars) for a picture prompt. */
+    fun clean(s: String): String = s.replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ").trim().take(160)
+
+    /** Extra rule text for the website prompt. Mentions video only when asked. */
+    fun promptBlock(images: Boolean, video: Boolean): String {
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        if (images) sb.append("PICTURES: where a photo fits, write src=\"NOVA_IMG:<short English description of the picture>\" (or url('NOVA_IMG:...') in CSS). NOVA makes each one with AI. Use 3 to 6 different descriptions, never real brand or person names.\n")
+        else sb.append("The owner does not want photos: use no <img> and no NOVA_IMG placeholders, only text, colours and CSS shapes.\n")
+        if (video) sb.append("The owner asked for VIDEO: NOVA cannot make AI video, so give the page an animated CSS/canvas background instead of a <video> tag.\n")
+        return sb.toString()
+    }
+
+    private fun mediaTag(it: SiteMediaStore.Item, style: String): String =
+        if (it.kind == "video") "<video src=\"${it.uri}\" controls muted playsinline loop style=\"$style\"></video>"
+        else "<img src=\"${it.uri}\" alt=\"\" style=\"$style\">"
+
+    /** Prompt text listing the owner's picked media so the AI puts NOVA_USER_MEDIA:<id> where it belongs. "" when none. */
+    fun userBlock(items: List<SiteMediaStore.Item>): String {
+        if (items.isEmpty()) return ""
+        val sb = StringBuilder("OWNER MEDIA (use each one exactly once as src=\"NOVA_USER_MEDIA:<id>\", never invent other ids):\n")
+        for (i in items) {
+            sb.append("- NOVA_USER_MEDIA:").append(i.id).append("  ").append(i.id).append(" = ").append(i.kind)
+            if (i.place != null) sb.append(", place: ").append(i.place!!.name.lowercase())
+            sb.append('\n')
+        }
+        return sb.toString()
+    }
+
+    /** Swap NOVA_USER_MEDIA:<id> for the real data: URI; items the AI forgot are injected by their place. Dollar signs in data are safe (literal replace). */
+    fun applyUser(html: String, items: List<SiteMediaStore.Item>): UserApplied {
+        if (items.isEmpty()) return UserApplied(html, emptyList(), emptyList())
+        var h = html
+        val placed = ArrayList<String>()
+        val missing = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (it in items) {
+            val tok = "NOVA_USER_MEDIA:" + it.id
+            if (!h.contains(tok)) { missing.add(it.id); continue }
+            placed.add(it.id)
+            if (it.kind == "video") {
+                h = Regex("<img\\b[^>]*" + Regex.escape(tok) + "[^>]*>").replace(h) { _ -> mediaTag(it, "max-width:100%") }
+            }
+            h = h.replace(tok, it.uri)
+        }
+        for (it in items) {
+            if (it.id !in missing) continue
+            val place = it.place
+            when (place) {
+                MediaPlace.Place.BACKGROUND -> {
+                    val css = "<style>body{background-image:url('${it.uri}');background-size:cover;background-attachment:fixed}</style>"
+                    h = if (h.contains("</head>", true)) insertAt(h, "</head>", css, false) else css + h
+                }
+                MediaPlace.Place.LOGO -> h = afterBodyOpen(h, mediaTag(it, "height:56px;width:auto;display:block;margin:8px auto"))
+                MediaPlace.Place.HERO -> {
+                    val tag = mediaTag(it, "width:100%;max-height:60vh;object-fit:cover;display:block")
+                    h = if (h.contains("</header>", true)) insertAt(h, "</header>", tag, true) else afterBodyOpen(h, tag)
+                }
+                else -> {
+                    val sec = "<section style=\"padding:16px;text-align:center\">" + mediaTag(it, "max-width:100%;border-radius:12px") + "</section>"
+                    h = if (h.contains("</body>", true)) insertAt(h, "</body>", sec, false) else h + sec
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        h = Regex("<img\\b[^>]*NOVA_USER_MEDIA:[A-Za-z0-9_]+[^>]*>").replace(h, "")
+        h = Regex("NOVA_USER_MEDIA:[A-Za-z0-9_]+").replace(h, "")
+        return UserApplied(h, placed, missing)
+    }
+
+    /** Insert text before (after=false) or after (after=true) the first case-insensitive marker. Literal, so $ is safe. */
+    private fun insertAt(h: String, marker: String, text: String, after: Boolean): String {
+        val i = h.indexOf(marker, 0, true)
+        if (i < 0) return h + text
+        val cut = if (after) i + marker.length else i
+        return h.substring(0, cut) + text + h.substring(cut)
+    }
+
+    private fun afterBodyOpen(h: String, tag: String): String {
+        val m = Regex("<body[^>]*>", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE).find(h)
+        return if (m == null) tag + h else h.substring(0, m.range.last + 1) + tag + h.substring(m.range.last + 1)
+    }
+
+    // ===== v7.12: where the owner wants each picked photo / video ("dusri photo gallery mein lagao"), kept in memory per token =====
+    private val places = java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<String, MediaPlace.Place>()
+
+    /** Pure: which of [n] items (0-based) a sentence points at, and the place. null = nothing to do. Never overrides an item that already has a place unless it is named. */
+    fun placeTarget(n: Int, text: String, hasPlace: (Int) -> Boolean): Pair<Int, MediaPlace.Place>? {
+        if (n <= 0) return null
+        val place = MediaPlace.parse(text) ?: return null
+        val idx = MediaPlace.itemIndex(text)
+        val i = when (idx) {
+            null -> (n - 1 downTo 0).firstOrNull { !hasPlace(it) } ?: return null
+            -1 -> n - 1
+            else -> idx - 1
+        }
+        if (i !in 0 until n) return null
+        return Pair(i, place)
+    }
+
+    /** Applies the sentence to the media files in the app folder. Returns how many items got a place. */
+    fun placeText(ctx: Context, text: String): Int {
+        val items = list(ctx)
+        val t = placeTarget(items.size, text) { places.containsKey(items[it].token) } ?: return 0
+        places[items[t.first].token] = t.second
+        return 1
+    }
+
+    // ===== v7.12 (add-only, pure): AI picture placeholders -> data URIs =====
+    private val PIC_WORDS = setOf("photo", "photos", "foto", "fotos", "image", "images", "picture", "pictures", "pic", "pics", "tasveer", "tasveeren", "tasviren")
+
+    /** True when the owner explicitly asked for pictures in the site (and did not opt out): only then NOVA spends AI image calls. */
+    fun wantsAiPictures(text: String): Boolean = MediaIntent.wantsImages(text) && MediaIntent.tokens(text).any { it in PIC_WORDS }
+
+    /** Replaces each NOVA_IMG:<description> with its data URI from [done] (key = clean(description)); an unknown one becomes a 1x1 pixel, so no broken link stays. */
+    fun fillImages(html: String, done: Map<String, String>): String {
+        if (!html.contains("NOVA_IMG:")) return html
+        return IMG_PH.replace(html) { m -> done[clean(m.groupValues[1])] ?: PIXEL_GIF }
+    }
+
+    const val PIXEL_GIF = "data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw=="
 }

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.03 G: the photos / videos the owner picked from the phone gallery for the website (max 6, kept 6 hours, memory only).
+ * Each item already holds its final data: URI, so the website stays ONE self-contained html file. Pure Kotlin (no Android classes).
+ * The Android side (MainActivity) reads and shrinks the file, then calls add().
+ */
+object SiteMediaStore {
+    class Item(val id: String, val kind: String, val uri: String, val thumb: String, val addedAt: Long, @Volatile var place: MediaPlace.Place?)
+
+    const val MAX_ITEMS = 6
+    const val MAX_TOTAL_CHARS = 12_000_000      // data URIs are base64: ~9 MB of real files; the whole page must stay far below 16 MB
+    private const val KEEP_MS = 6 * 60 * 60_000L
+    private val list = ArrayList<Item>()
+
+    @Synchronized private fun prune(now: Long) { list.removeAll { now - it.addedAt > KEEP_MS } }
+
+    @Synchronized fun items(now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): List<Item> { prune(now); return ArrayList(list) }
+
+    /** Returns the new id (m1..m6) or null when full / too big. kind = "image" | "video". */
+    @Synchronized fun add(kind: String, uri: String, thumb: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): String? {
+        prune(now)
+        if (kind != "image" && kind != "video") return null
+        if (list.size >= MAX_ITEMS) return null
+        if (list.sumOf { it.uri.length } + uri.length > MAX_TOTAL_CHARS) return null
+        val used = list.map { it.id }.toSet()
+        val id = (1..MAX_ITEMS).map { "m$it" }.firstOrNull { it !in used } ?: return null
+        list.add(Item(id, kind, uri, thumb, now, null))
+        return id
+    }
+
+    @Synchronized fun setPlace(id: String, place: MediaPlace.Place?): Boolean {
+        val it = list.firstOrNull { x -> x.id == id } ?: return false
+        it.place = place
+        return true
+    }
+
+    @Synchronized fun clear() { list.clear() }
+
+    /**
+     * Typed / spoken placement: "dusri photo gallery mein lagao" (item 2), "ye photo hero mein" (the newest item that has no place yet).
+     * Returns how many items got a place. Never touches an item that already has a place unless the sentence names its number.
+     */
+    @Synchronized fun applyText(text: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Int {
+        prune(now)
+        if (list.isEmpty()) return 0
+        val place = MediaPlace.parse(text) ?: return 0
+        val idx = MediaPlace.itemIndex(text)
+        val target: Item? = when {
+            idx == null -> list.lastOrNull { it.place == null }
+            idx == -1 -> list.last()
+            else -> list.getOrNull(idx - 1)
+        }
+        if (target == null) return 0
+        target.place = place
+        return 1
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v6.98 (owner request 16 Oct 2026): Instagram / WhatsApp messages and comments.
+ *  - an insult or shaming message / comment: NOVA does NOT reply, tells the owner, and (Instagram) marks it "to report";
+ *  - a birthday / festival wish or a normal nice comment: NOVA replies with a short fixed thank-you and tells the owner;
+ *  - anything else (questions, prices, business talk, unknown): unchanged, the old auto-reply rules decide.
+ * Pure Kotlin, no Android classes, unit tested. Local keyword rules: fast, no AI call, no upload. The reply text is FIXED
+ * (never invented), so it cannot promise anything. Per person limit (2 per hour) lives in AutoReplyRules.Gate.
+ * NOT done here: the reel / post caption is not read (only the comment text), and the Instagram "Report" taps are not automated.
+ */
+object SocialReplyRules {
+    enum class Kind { INSULT, WISH, NORMAL, OTHER }
+
+    class Decision(val kind: Kind, val reply: String?, val report: Boolean)
+
+    const val THANKS_WISH = "Thank you so much 😊🙏"
+    const val THANKS_NORMAL = "Thank you 😊"
+
+    private const val IG = "com.instagram.android"
+    private val WA = setOf("com.whatsapp", "com.whatsapp.w4b")
+    const val MAX_LEN = 220
+
+    private val INSULT_WORDS = setOf(
+        "chutiya", "chutiye", "madarchod", "behenchod", "bhosdi", "bhosdike", "bhosda", "randi", "harami", "haramkhor", "kutta", "kutte",
+        "kamina", "kamine", "gandu", "gaandu", "lund", "mc", "bc", "besharam", "nikamma", "dhokebaaz", "chor", "ullu",
+        "fuck", "fucking", "bitch", "bastard", "asshole", "idiot", "stupid", "loser", "useless", "trash", "garbage", "scammer", "cheater", "thug", "fraud", "scam"
+    )
+    private val INSULT_PHRASES = listOf(
+        "shame on you", "sharam karo", "sharam nahi", "tumhe sharam", "hate you", "mar ja", "marja", "mar jao", "go die",
+        "tera baap", "teri maa", "teri ma ", "bakwas", "gandagi", "fake hai", "fake page", "dhokha"
+    )
+    private val WISH_PHRASES = listOf(
+        "happy birthday", "hbd", "bday", "b day", "birthday", "janamdin", "janmdin", "janam din", "janmadin", "anniversary",
+        "congratulations", "congrats", "mubarak", "badhai", "shubhkamnaye", "best wishes", "happy returns", "happy diwali", "happy holi", "eid"
+    )
+    private val NICE_WORDS = setOf(
+        "nice", "great", "awesome", "superb", "love", "loved", "beautiful", "amazing", "wow", "mast", "badiya", "badhiya", "zabardast",
+        "best", "good", "excellent", "perfect", "cute", "gorgeous", "stunning", "wonderful", "lovely", "pretty", "fire",
+        "thanks", "thank", "thanku", "shukriya", "dhanyawad"
+    )
+    private val NICE_PHRASES = listOf("well done", "keep it up", "thank you", "so nice", "very nice")
+    private val QUESTION_WORDS = setOf("kya", "kitna", "kitne", "kitni", "price", "rate", "kaise", "kab", "kahan", "kaha", "how", "what", "when", "where", "why", "cost", "available", "link", "order", "size")
+    private val EMOJI = listOf("❤", "😍", "👍", "🔥", "👏", "😊", "🙏", "💯", "😘", "🥰", "🎉", "🎂", "💐")
+
+    private fun norm(s: String): String {
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        for (ch in s.lowercase()) sb.append(if (ch.isLetterOrDigit()) ch else ' ')
+        return " " + sb.toString().trim().split(' ').filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.joinToString(" ") + " "
+    }
+
+    fun classify(text: String): Kind {
+        val raw = text.trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty() || raw.length > MAX_LEN) return Kind.OTHER
+        val t = norm(raw)
+        val toks = t.trim().split(' ').filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
+        if (toks.any { it in INSULT_WORDS } || INSULT_PHRASES.any { t.contains(" " + it.trim() + " ") }) return Kind.INSULT
+        if (raw.contains('?') || toks.any { it in QUESTION_WORDS }) return Kind.OTHER
+        if (WISH_PHRASES.any { t.contains(" $it ") }) return Kind.WISH
+        if (toks.any { it in NICE_WORDS } || NICE_PHRASES.any { t.contains(" $it ") }) return Kind.NORMAL
+        if (toks.isEmpty() && EMOJI.any { raw.contains(it) }) return Kind.NORMAL
+        if (toks.size <= 2 && EMOJI.any { raw.contains(it) }) return Kind.NORMAL
+        return Kind.OTHER
+    }
+
+    /** null = this app / text is not handled here (old auto-reply rules stay in charge). */
+    fun decide(pkg: String, text: String): Decision? {
+        val isIg = pkg == IG
+        if (!isIg && pkg !in WA) return null
+        return when (classify(text)) {
+            Kind.INSULT -> Decision(Kind.INSULT, null, isIg)
+            Kind.WISH -> Decision(Kind.WISH, THANKS_WISH, false)
+            Kind.NORMAL -> Decision(Kind.NORMAL, THANKS_NORMAL, false)
+            Kind.OTHER -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun insultNote(report: Boolean, hinglish: Boolean): String = when {
+        hinglish && report -> "Beizzati wala message/comment: jawab nahi diya, report karna hai"
+        hinglish -> "Beizzati wala message: jawab nahi diya"
+        report -> "Insulting message/comment: no reply sent, needs a report"
+        else -> "Insulting message: no reply sent"
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v6.99 Part 2: pure text rules for a spoken STOP, no Android.
+ * Only a WHOLE short utterance counts ("ruko", "ruk jao", "bas karo"); a stop word inside a longer command never stops anything.
+ * Evidence level: written. Not compiled, not phone tested.
+ */
+object StopRules {
+    private val PHRASES = setOf(
+        "stop", "cancel", "chup", "quiet", "ruko", "be quiet", "shut up",
+        "ruk jao", "ruk ja", "ruk jaiye", "ruko ruko", "roko", "ise roko", "isko roko",
+        "bas karo", "rehne do", "mat karo", "stop it", "stop that", "stop now",
+        "cancel it", "cancel karo", "cancel kar do"
+    )
+
+    /** True when the whole utterance (filler words already removed or not) is a stop request. */
+    fun isStop(text: String): Boolean {
+        val s = text.trim().lowercase().replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ")
+        if (s.isEmpty()) return false
+        if (s in PHRASES) return true
+        val noNova = s.removePrefix("nova ").trim()
+        return noNova in PHRASES
+    }
+
+    /** A background job (picture, website) remembers the stop counter when it starts; if the counter moved, the owner said stop meanwhile. */
+    fun wasStopped(startedAt: Int, now: Int): Boolean = startedAt != now
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import android.content.Context
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import java.net.HttpURLConnection
+import java.net.URL
+
+/**
+ * v7.05 H: publish the newest website on Vercel with the owner's OWN Vercel token (CONNECT tab). One POST to the deployments API
+ * with index.html inline (media is already embedded as data: URIs). The token goes in a request header only (never a URL or a log).
+ * The result URL comes ONLY from Vercel's answer, never invented. Called only after the owner's yes (askFirst) and only when the
+ * Vercel connector has WRITE permission. Evidence: written + compiled with stubs + unit tested body/URL parsing; real Vercel call NOT TESTED.
+ */
+object VercelPublish {
+    class Out(val ok: Boolean, val url: String = "", val msg: String = "")
+
+    const val MAX_BYTES = 12_000_000
+
+    /** Pure: Vercel project names are lower-case letters, digits and '-'. */
+    fun projectName(subject: String, stamp: Long): String {
+        val s = SiteGen.slug(subject).replace('_', '-')
+        return ("nova-" + s + "-" + (stamp % 10000)).take(60)
+    }
+
+    /** Pure: the deployment request body. */
+    fun buildBody(name: String, html: String): String {
+        val file = JSONObject().put("file", "index.html").put("data", html).put("encoding", "utf-8")
+        return JSONObject()
+            .put("name", name)
+            .put("files", org.json.JSONArray().put(file))
+            .put("projectSettings", JSONObject().put("framework", JSONObject.NULL))
+            .put("target", "production")
+            .toString()
+    }
+
+    /** Pure: "https://..." from Vercel's answer (first alias when there is one, else url). Empty when the answer has none. */
+    fun parseUrl(body: String): String {
+        return try {
+            val o = JSONObject(body)
+            var host = ""
+            val al = o.optJSONArray("alias")
+            if (al != null && al.length() > 0) host = al.optString(0)
+            if (host.isBlank()) host = o.optString("url")
+            host = host.trim()
+            if (host.isEmpty() || !host.all { it.isLetterOrDigit() || it == '.' || it == '-' }) "" else "https://$host"
+        } catch (e: Exception) { "" }
+    }
+
+    private fun failFor(code: Int): String = when (code) {
+        401, 403 -> "Vercel token galat hai ya permission nahi hai. CONNECT tab mein dobara jodo"
+        400, 409, 422 -> "Vercel ne website nahi li. Thodi der baad dobara try karo"
+        429 -> "Vercel abhi busy hai (limit). Thodi der baad try karo"
+        598 -> "Internet nahi mil raha"
+        else -> "Vercel par abhi publish nahi ho paya. Thodi der baad dobara try karo"
+    }
+
+    fun run(ctx: Context, subject: String, html: String): Out {
+        if (!ConnectorStore.connected(ctx, ConnectorCatalog.VERCEL)) return Out(false, "", "Vercel connect nahi hai. CONNECT tab mein token jodo")
+        if (!ConnectorCatalog.allows(ConnectorStore.perm(ctx, ConnectorCatalog.VERCEL), true)) return Out(false, "", "Vercel par sirf padhne ki ijazat hai. CONNECT tab mein WRITE chalu karo")
+        val token = ConnectorStore.token(ctx, ConnectorCatalog.VERCEL)
+        if (token.isEmpty()) return Out(false, "", "Vercel token nahi mila. CONNECT tab mein jodo")
+        if (html.length < 200 || html.length > MAX_BYTES) return Out(false, "", "Website file theek nahi hai")
+        return try {
+            val c = URL("https://api.vercel.com/v13/deployments?skipAutoDetectionConfirmation=1").openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
+            c.requestMethod = "POST"
+            c.connectTimeout = 15000
+            c.readTimeout = 90000
+            c.instanceFollowRedirects = false
+            c.doOutput = true
+            c.setRequestProperty("Authorization", "Bearer " + token)
+            c.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json")
+            c.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "NOVA-assistant")
+            c.outputStream.use { it.write(buildBody(projectName(subject, System.currentTimeMillis()), html).toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)) }
+            val code = c.responseCode
+            val text = (if (code in 200..299) c.inputStream else c.errorStream)?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() } ?: ""
+            c.disconnect()
+            if (code !in 200..299) return Out(false, "", failFor(code))
+            val url = parseUrl(text)
+            if (url.isEmpty()) Out(false, "", "Vercel ne link nahi diya. Vercel dashboard mein dekho") else Out(true, url, "")
+        } catch (e: java.net.UnknownHostException) {
+            Out(false, "", failFor(598))
+        } catch (e: Exception) {
+            Out(false, "", failFor(599))
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/VideoResultRules.kt b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/VideoResultRules.kt

+++ b/app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/VideoResultRules.kt

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.12 AUTOPSY R3 (pure Kotlin, no Android classes): the visible result line for a video recording that ended or never started.
+ * Before, auto-stops and failed starts only made the notification disappear. Texts never claim a save that did not happen.
+ */
+object VideoResultRules {
+    class Text(val title: String, val body: String, val good: Boolean)
+
+    fun forStatus(status: String): Text? = when {
+        status == "saved" -> Text("Video save ho gayi", "Gallery > Movies > NOVA mein hai.", true)
+        status == "saved_max" -> Text("Video save ho gayi (3 ghante poore)", "Limit par rukne se pehle tak ki video Gallery > Movies > NOVA mein hai.", true)
+        status == "saved_space" -> Text("Video save ho gayi (jagah kam thi)", "Phone ki jagah kam hui to rok di. Jitni record hui wo Gallery > Movies > NOVA mein hai.", true)
+        status == "no_frames" -> Text("Video record nahi hui", "Camera se koi frame nahi aaya, isliye kuch save nahi hua.", false)
+        status == "stop_failed" -> Text("Video theek se band nahi hui", "File NOVA ke app folder mein rakhi hai, Gallery mein nahi gayi. Recordings screen se recover try karo.", false)
+        status == "save_failed" -> Text("Video save nahi hui", "Gallery mein save nahi ho paayi.", false)
+        status == "permission" -> Text("Video shuru nahi hui", "Camera ya mic ki permission nahi hai.", false)
+        status == "not_visible" -> Text("Video shuru nahi hui", "Android 14 mein NOVA screen par khuli honi chahiye, tab hi camera chalta hai.", false)
+        status.startsWith("failed:") -> Text("Video shuru nahi hui", "Camera ya mic nahi khula (" + status.removePrefix("failed:").take(60) + ").", false)
+        else -> null
+    }
+}

+/** v7.03 G: pure tests for MediaPlace (where a picked gallery file goes) and SiteMediaStore (memory list, max 6). */
+class MediaPlaceTest {
+    @Test fun parsesPlaces() {
+        assertEquals(MediaPlace.Place.HERO, MediaPlace.parse("ye photo hero mein lagao"))
+        assertEquals(MediaPlace.Place.GALLERY, MediaPlace.parse("dusri photo gallery mein daalo"))
+        assertEquals(MediaPlace.Place.LOGO, MediaPlace.parse("isko logo banao header mein"))
+        assertEquals(MediaPlace.Place.BACKGROUND, MediaPlace.parse("video background mein lagao"))
+        assertNull(MediaPlace.parse("ek restaurant ki website banao"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun itemNumbers() {
+        assertEquals(1, MediaPlace.itemIndex("pehli photo hero mein"))
+        assertEquals(2, MediaPlace.itemIndex("dusri photo gallery mein"))
+        assertEquals(3, MediaPlace.itemIndex("m3 logo banao"))
+        assertEquals(-1, MediaPlace.itemIndex("last photo footer mein"))
+        assertNull(MediaPlace.itemIndex("ye photo hero mein"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun placeCommandNeedsPlaceVerbAndMedia() {
+        assertTrue(MediaPlace.isPlaceCommand("ye photo hero mein lagao"))
+        assertTrue(MediaPlace.isPlaceCommand("m2 gallery mein rakho"))
+        assertFalse(MediaPlace.isPlaceCommand("hero mein"))
+        assertFalse(MediaPlace.isPlaceCommand("photo lagao"))
+        assertFalse(MediaPlace.isPlaceCommand("aaj ka mausam batao"))
+        assertFalse(MediaPlace.isPlaceCommand(""))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun storeAddsMax6AndAppliesText() {
+        SiteMediaStore.clear()
+        val now = 1_000_000L
+        for (n in 1..6) assertEquals("m$n", SiteMediaStore.add("image", "data:image/jpeg;base64,AA", "", now))
+        assertNull(SiteMediaStore.add("image", "data:image/jpeg;base64,AA", "", now))
+        assertNull(SiteMediaStore.add("audio", "x", "", now))
+        assertEquals(1, SiteMediaStore.applyText("dusri photo gallery mein lagao", now))
+        assertEquals(MediaPlace.Place.GALLERY, SiteMediaStore.items(now)[1].place)
+        assertEquals(1, SiteMediaStore.applyText("ye photo hero mein lagao", now))   // newest item without a place = m6
+        assertEquals(MediaPlace.Place.HERO, SiteMediaStore.items(now)[5].place)
+        SiteMediaStore.clear()
+        assertEquals(0, SiteMediaStore.items(now).size)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun storeForgetsAfterSixHours() {
+        SiteMediaStore.clear()
+        SiteMediaStore.add("image", "data:image/jpeg;base64,AA", "", 0L)
+        assertEquals(0, SiteMediaStore.items(7 * 60 * 60_000L).size)
+        SiteMediaStore.clear()
+    }
+
+    @Test fun storeRefusesTooBigTotal() {
+        SiteMediaStore.clear()
+        val big = "x".repeat(SiteMediaStore.MAX_TOTAL_CHARS - 10)
+        assertEquals("m1", SiteMediaStore.add("video", big, "", 5L))
+        assertNull(SiteMediaStore.add("image", "y".repeat(100), "", 5L))
+        SiteMediaStore.clear()
+    }

+/**
+ * v6.97 wip14 Part 2: NovaService.handle() runs the web-open rules AFTER these earlier specialist handlers.
+ * None of them may take a "open site in Chrome" sentence, and the web rules must not take sentences those handlers own.
+ */
+class OpenWebRoutingTest {
+    private val webPhrases = listOf(
+        "youtube chrome pe kholo",
+        "nova youtube google aur flipkart chrome pe kholo",
+        "flipkart.com kholo",
+        "whatsapp web kholo",
+        "instagram aur facebook kholo",
+        "amazon website kholo",
+        "open youtube in chrome",
+        "youtube google amazon flipkart myntra websites kholo",
+        "github chrome me kholo"
+    )
+
+    @Test fun earlierHandlersDoNotStealWebSentences() {
+        for (p in webPhrases) {
+            assertNotNull("web rules must take: $p", OpenWebRules.parseOpen(p))
+            assertNull("SiteIntent: $p", SiteIntent.parse(p))
+            assertFalse("SiteIntent.isPublish: $p", SiteIntent.isPublish(p))
+            assertNull("ConnectorRules: $p", ConnectorRules.parse(p))
+            assertNull("ConnectorRules plan: $p", ConnectorRules.parsePlan(p))
+            assertNull("GmailRules: $p", GmailRules.parse(p))
+            assertNull("AppSearchRules: $p", AppSearchRules.parse(p))
+            assertNull("MusicPlayRules: $p", MusicPlayRules.parse(p))
+            assertNull("LearnRules: $p", LearnRules.parse(p))
+            assertNull("ImageIntent: $p", ImageIntent.parse(p))
+            assertNull("RecordRules: $p", RecordRules.parse(p))
+            assertNull("WhatsAppCommandRules: $p", WhatsAppCommandRules.parse(p))
+            assertFalse("TeachRules: $p", TeachRules.isIntent(p))
+            assertFalse("HelpGuide: $p", HelpGuide.isAsk(p))
+            assertFalse("LeadRules: $p", LeadRules.isLeadAcquisitionRequest(p))
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test fun webRulesDoNotStealOtherHandlersSentences() {
+        val others = listOf(
+            "youtube me arijit ke gaane play karo",
+            "youtube kholo aur arijit play karo",
+            "anand ko whatsapp bhejo kal milte hain",
+            "whatsapp download karo",
+            "gmail me mail bhejo",
+            "wifi settings kholo",
+            "camera kholo",
+            "recording on karo",
+            "mera ek website banao",
+            "github repos dikhao"
+        )
+        for (p in others) assertNull("web rules must leave: $p", OpenWebRules.parseOpen(p))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun plainOpenCommandsKeepTheOldAppPath() {
+        for (p in listOf("youtube kholo", "open whatsapp", "instagram khol do", "calculator kholo", "chrome kholo")) {
+            assertNull("old path: $p", OpenWebRules.parseOpen(p))
+            assertNotNull("Logic still reads it: $p", Logic.classify(p))
+        }
+    }

+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test

+/** v6.97 wip14 Part 1: website / app / Play Store wording rules. Pure text, no Android. */
+class OpenWebRulesTest {
+    private fun names(r: OpenWebRules.OpenReq?) = r?.targets?.map { it.name }
+

+        assertTrue(r.targets[0].web)
+        assertEquals("https://www.youtube.com", r.targets[0].url)
+        assertEquals("YouTube", r.targets[0].label)
+        assertEquals(listOf("youtube"), names(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("open youtube in chrome")))
+        assertEquals(listOf("flipkart"), names(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("nova chrome mein flipkart kholo")))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun severalSitesEachGetOneTarget() {
+        val r = OpenWebRules.parseOpen("nova youtube google aur flipkart chrome pe kholo")!!
+        assertEquals(listOf("youtube", "google", "flipkart"), names(r))
+        assertTrue(r.targets.all { it.web && it.url != null })
+        val r2 = OpenWebRules.parseOpen("youtube, google, amazon, flipkart, myntra websites kholo")!!
+        assertEquals(listOf("youtube", "google", "amazon", "flipkart", "myntra"), names(r2))
+        assertFalse(r2.truncated)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun severalNamesWithoutChromeStayAppFirst() {
+        val r = OpenWebRules.parseOpen("instagram aur facebook kholo")!!
+        assertEquals(listOf("instagram", "facebook"), names(r))
+        assertFalse(r.chrome)
+        assertTrue(r.targets.none { it.web })
+        assertNotNull(r.targets[0].url)   // known site stays as the fallback when the app is not installed
+    }
+
+    @Test fun domainsAndWebOnlySitesAlwaysOpenAsWebsites() {
+        val r = OpenWebRules.parseOpen("flipkart.com kholo")!!
+        assertEquals("https://flipkart.com", r.targets[0].url)
+        assertTrue(r.targets[0].web)
+        assertEquals("https://flipkart.com", OpenWebRules.parseOpen("flipkart dot com kholo")!!.targets[0].url)
+        val w = OpenWebRules.parseOpen("whatsapp web kholo")!!
+        assertEquals("https://web.whatsapp.com", w.targets[0].url)
+        assertTrue(w.targets[0].web)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun unknownNameInWebsiteModeIsGuessedOrSearched() {
+        assertEquals("https://www.xyzshop.com", OpenWebRules.parseOpen("xyzshop website kholo")!!.targets[0].url)
+        val multi = OpenWebRules.parseOpen("raju ki dukan website kholo")
+        assertNotNull(multi)
+        assertTrue(multi!!.targets[0].url!!.startsWith("https://www.google.com/search?q="))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.siteUrl("xyzshop", guess = false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun morethanEightIsTruncatedHonestly() {
+        val r = OpenWebRules.parseOpen("youtube google gmail facebook instagram amazon flipkart myntra netflix chrome pe kholo")!!
+        assertEquals(OpenWebRules.MAX_TARGETS, r.targets.size)
+        assertTrue(r.truncated)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun otherHandlersKeepTheirSentences() {
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("youtube chrome pe kholo aur song play karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("youtube me arijit search karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("anand ko whatsapp message bhejo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("youtube mat kholo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("camera kholo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("wifi settings kholo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("whatsapp download karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("kya tum website bana sakte ho"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen(""))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun voiceSpellingsAreRepaired() {
+        assertEquals(listOf("youtube"), names(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("you tube crome pe kholo")))
+        assertEquals(listOf("facebook", "instagram"), names(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("face book aur insta gram chrome me kholo")))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun downloadKnownApps() {
+        val a = OpenWebRules.parseDownload("whatsapp download karo")!!
+        assertEquals("com.whatsapp", a.pkg)
+        assertEquals("WhatsApp", a.label)
+        assertEquals("com.instagram.android", OpenWebRules.parseDownload("nova instagram download kar do play store se")!!.pkg)
+        assertEquals("com.whatsapp.w4b", OpenWebRules.parseDownload("download whatsapp business")!!.pkg)
+        assertEquals("org.telegram.messenger", OpenWebRules.parseDownload("install karo telegram")!!.pkg)
+        assertEquals("com.google.android.youtube", OpenWebRules.parseDownload("youtube download karo google play store se")!!.pkg)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun downloadUnknownAppHasNoPackage() {
+        val a = OpenWebRules.parseDownload("xyz game download karo")!!
+        assertNull(a.pkg)
+        assertEquals("Xyz Game", a.label)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun downloadSeveralAppsDoesTheFirstAndKeepsTheRest() {
+        val a = OpenWebRules.parseDownload("whatsapp aur instagram download karo")!!
+        assertEquals("com.whatsapp", a.pkg)
+        assertEquals(listOf("Instagram"), a.rest)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun downloadLeavesOtherDownloadsAlone() {
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("nova update download karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("offline pack download karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("ye song download karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("model download karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("whatsapp uninstall karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("whatsapp download mat karo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("youtube kholo"))
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseDownload("download"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun siteUrlHelpers() {
+        assertEquals("https://example.org", OpenWebRules.siteUrl("example.org", guess = false))
+        assertEquals("https://mail.google.com", OpenWebRules.siteUrl("gmail", guess = false))
+        assertEquals("YouTube", OpenWebRules.labelFor("youtube"))
+        assertEquals("Raju Shop", OpenWebRules.labelFor("raju shop"))
+    }
+
+    // ---- v6.99 Part 3: speech-to-text spellings of download / install (written, NOT RUN)
+    @Test fun downloadSpeechSpellingsAreUnderstood() {
+        for (s in listOf(
+            "whatsapp daunlod karo", "whatsapp dawnload karo", "whatsapp down load karo", "whatsapp down lod kar do",
+            "instol karo whatsapp", "nova whatsapp downlod karo",
+            "whatsapp \u0921\u093e\u0909\u0928\u0932\u094b\u0921 \u0915\u0930\u094b",
+            "whatsapp \u0907\u0902\u0938\u094d\u091f\u0949\u0932 \u0915\u0930\u094b"
+        )) assertEquals(s, "com.whatsapp", OpenWebRules.parseDownload(s)?.pkg)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun misspelledDownloadIsNeverTakenAsOpenOrOtherDownloads() {
+        assertNull(OpenWebRules.parseOpen("whatsapp daunlod karo"))
+        for (s in listOf("nova update daunlod karo", "video daunlod karo", "mat daunlod karo whatsapp", "gaana down load karo"))
+            assertNull(s, OpenWebRules.parseDownload(s))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun helpUnderstandsMisspelledDownloadQuestion() {
+        assertEquals("download", HelpGuide.topicFor("kya tum app daunlod kar sakte ho")?.id)
+    }

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.junit.Assert.*
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v6.99 Part 5: money talk auto reply rules. Pure text. Written, NOT RUN. */
+class PaymentReplyRulesTest {
+    private fun kind(s: String) = PaymentReplyRules.decide(s)?.kind
+
+    @Test fun codesGetNoReply() {
+        assertEquals(PaymentReplyRules.Kind.SECRET, kind("mera otp 123456 bhej do"))
+        assertNull(PaymentReplyRules.decide("otp bata do")?.reply)
+        assertEquals(PaymentReplyRules.Kind.SECRET, kind("cvv aur card number bhejo"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun eachMoneyAskGetsItsOwnSafeLine() {
+        assertEquals(PaymentReplyRules.Kind.ASK_MONEY, kind("bhai paise bhej do urgent"))
+        assertEquals(PaymentReplyRules.Kind.PAID_CHECK, kind("payment ho gaya check karo"))
+        assertEquals(PaymentReplyRules.Kind.DETAILS, kind("apna upi id bhejo"))
+        assertEquals(PaymentReplyRules.REPLY_DETAILS, PaymentReplyRules.decide("account number bhejo payment ke liye")?.reply)
+        assertEquals(PaymentReplyRules.Kind.MONEY, kind("bill ka amount kitna hai"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun repliesNeverPromiseOrShareAnything() {
+        for (r in listOf(PaymentReplyRules.REPLY_ASK, PaymentReplyRules.REPLY_PAID, PaymentReplyRules.REPLY_DETAILS)) {
+            assertFalse(r, Regex("[0-9]").containsMatchIn(r))
+            assertFalse(r, r.lowercase().contains("haan") || r.lowercase().contains("bhej diya") || r.lowercase().contains("upi"))
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test fun ordinaryTalkIsLeftToTheNormalRules() {
+        assertNull(kind("kal milte hain"))
+        assertNull(kind("shopping chalein"))
+        assertNull(kind(""))
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v6.99 Part 1: voice "call uthao / call kaato" and the pure parts of the other phone commands. */
+class PhoneVoiceRulesTest {
+    private fun k(s: String) = PhoneVoiceRules.parse(Logic.norm(s))?.kind
+
+    @Test fun answerPhrases() {
+        for (s in listOf("call uthao", "call utha lo", "phone uthao", "answer the call", "pick up the call", "call receive karo", "accept call"))
+            assertEquals(s, PhoneVoiceRules.K_ANSWER, k(s))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun rejectPhrases() {
+        for (s in listOf("call kaato", "call kaat do", "reject call", "call cut karo", "call band karo", "hang up", "end call", "decline the call"))
+            assertEquals(s, PhoneVoiceRules.K_REJECT, k(s))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun notCallCommands() {
+        assertNull(k("rahul ko call karo"))
+        assertNull(k("call mummy"))
+        assertNull(k("phone band karo"))          // a power question, not a call
+        assertNull(k("call mat uthao"))           // negated
+        assertNull(k("kal subah call karna"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun classifyGivesCallKinds() {
+        assertEquals(PhoneVoiceRules.K_ANSWER, Logic.classify("call uthao")?.kind)
+        assertEquals(PhoneVoiceRules.K_REJECT, Logic.classify("call kaato")?.kind)
+        assertEquals("call", Logic.classify("call rahul")?.kind)   // a normal contact call is untouched
+    }
+
+    @Test fun pickAction() {
+        assertEquals(0, PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(listOf("Answer", "Decline"), true))
+        assertEquals(1, PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(listOf("Answer", "Decline"), false))
+        assertEquals(1, PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(listOf("Decline", "Answer"), true))
+        assertEquals(0, PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(listOf("Hang Up"), false))
+        assertEquals(-1, PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(listOf("Hang Up"), true))
+        assertEquals(-1, PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(listOf("Reply", "Mute"), true))
+        assertEquals(-1, PhoneVoiceRules.pickAction(emptyList(), false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun part2ScreenshotRingerRotation() {
+        assertEquals(PhoneVoiceRules.K_SHOT, Logic.classify("screenshot lo")?.kind)
+        assertEquals(PhoneVoiceRules.K_SHOT, Logic.classify("take screenshot")?.kind)
+        val r = Logic.classify("ringer silent karo")
+        assertEquals(PhoneVoiceRules.K_RINGER, r?.kind); assertEquals("silent", r?.arg)
+        assertEquals("vibrate", Logic.classify("vibrate mode")?.arg)
+        assertEquals("normal", Logic.classify("ringer normal")?.arg)
+        val o = Logic.classify("rotation off karo")
+        assertEquals(PhoneVoiceRules.K_ROTATION, o?.kind); assertEquals("off", o?.arg)
+        assertEquals("on", Logic.classify("auto rotate on karo")?.arg)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun part2DoesNotStealOtherCommands() {
+        assertEquals("volume", Logic.classify("volume mute")?.kind)
+        assertEquals("volume", Logic.classify("awaaz band karo")?.kind)
+        assertEquals("quiet_on", Logic.classify("silent replies on")?.kind)
+        assertNull(k("screenshot delete karo"))
+        assertNull(k("rotation"))                 // no on / off word: nothing is guessed
+        assertNull(k("ringer"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun allFiveKindsActive() {
+        assertEquals(PhoneVoiceRules.KINDS, PhoneVoiceRules.ACTIVE)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun texts() {
+        assertEquals("Call utha liya", PhoneVoiceRules.doneText(PhoneVoiceRules.K_ANSWER, "", false))
+        assertEquals("Call rejected", PhoneVoiceRules.doneText(PhoneVoiceRules.K_REJECT, "", true))
+        assertNotNull(PhoneActions.failText("no_permission", true, false))
+    }
+}

+        assertNull(k("ye photo whatsapp par bhejo"))
+        assertNull(k("camera kholo photo kheencho"))
+        assertNull(k("restaurant ki website banao photos ke saath"))
+        assertNull(k("aaj ka mausam batao"))
+        assertNull(k(""))
+    }
+    @Test fun blockedWords() { assertTrue(PhotoEditIntent.parse("is photo ko nude professional banao") == null || PhotoEditIntent.parse("is photo ko nude professional banao")!!.blocked) ; assertNotNull(PhotoEditIntent.parse("is photo ko nsfw edit karo")); assertTrue(PhotoEditIntent.parse("is photo ko nsfw edit karo")!!.blocked) }
+    @Test fun v712BanaoNeedsObjectMarker() { assertNull(k("professional photo banao")); assertNull(k("ek sundar photo banao")); assertEquals(PhotoEditIntent.Kind.ENHANCE, k("is photo ko sundar banao")) }

diff --git a/app/src/test/java/com/nova/assistant/PhotoProTest.kt b/app/src/test/java/com/nova/assistant/PhotoProTest.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7814d28
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/src/test/java/com/nova/assistant/PhotoProTest.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v7.05 I: PhotoPro pixel maths (pure, no android classes touched). */
+class PhotoProTest {
+    private fun px(r: Int, g: Int, b: Int) = (0xFF shl 24) or (r shl 16) or (g shl 8) or b
+    private fun rr(p: Int) = (p shr 16) and 0xFF
+    private fun gg(p: Int) = (p shr 8) and 0xFF
+    private fun bb(p: Int) = p and 0xFF
+
+    @Test fun cropSquareFromWide() { assertArrayEquals(intArrayOf(50, 0, 300, 300), PhotoPro.cropRect(400, 300, 1, 1)) }
+    @Test fun cropWideFromTall() { val c = PhotoPro.cropRect(300, 600, 16, 9); assertEquals(300, c[2]); assertEquals(168, c[3]); assertEquals(216, c[1]) }
+    @Test fun cropKeepsBadInput() { assertArrayEquals(intArrayOf(0, 0, 10, 10), PhotoPro.cropRect(10, 10, 0, 0)) }
+
+    @Test fun autoLevelsStretchesFlatPhoto() {
+        val a = IntArray(1000) { i -> val v = 100 + i * 40 / 1000; px(v, v, v) }
+        PhotoPro.autoLevels(a)
+        assertTrue(rr(a.min()) < 20 || rr(a.minOf { rr(it) }) < 20)
+        assertTrue(a.maxOf { rr(it) } > 235)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun autoLevelsLeavesAlreadyWideRange() {
+        val a = IntArray(1000) { i -> val v = i * 255 / 999; px(v, v, v) }
+        val b = a.copyOf(); PhotoPro.autoLevels(b)
+        assertTrue(Math.abs(rr(b[500]) - rr(a[500])) < 8)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun grayscaleMakesChannelsEqualAndKeepsAlpha() {
+        val a = intArrayOf(px(200, 50, 10), px(10, 20, 240)); PhotoPro.grayscale(a)
+        for (p in a) { assertEquals(rr(p), gg(p)); assertEquals(gg(p), bb(p)); assertEquals(0xFF, p ushr 24) }
+    }
+
+    @Test fun sepiaIsWarm() { val a = intArrayOf(px(120, 120, 120)); PhotoPro.sepia(a); assertTrue(rr(a[0]) > bb(a[0])) }
+
+    @Test fun brightnessGammaBrightensDark() { val a = intArrayOf(px(40, 40, 40)); PhotoPro.brightness(a, 0.6f); assertTrue(rr(a[0]) > 60) }
+
+    @Test fun saturationOneIsIdentity() { val a = intArrayOf(px(30, 90, 200)); PhotoPro.saturation(a, 1f); assertEquals(px(30, 90, 200), a[0]) }
+
+    @Test fun skinToneChangesLessThanOtherColours() {
+        val skin = intArrayOf(px(210, 150, 120)); val blue = intArrayOf(px(60, 90, 200))
+        val s0 = rr(skin[0]) - bb(skin[0]); val b0 = bb(blue[0]) - rr(blue[0])
+        PhotoPro.saturation(skin, 1.4f); PhotoPro.saturation(blue, 1.4f)
+        val dSkin = (rr(skin[0]) - bb(skin[0])) - s0; val dBlue = (bb(blue[0]) - rr(blue[0])) - b0
+        assertTrue(dSkin < dBlue)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun sharpenKeepsFlatImageFlat() {
+        val a = IntArray(25) { px(90, 90, 90) }; PhotoPro.denoiseSharpen(a, 5, 5, 0.2f, 0.6f)
+        for (p in a) assertEquals(90, rr(p))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun sharpenIncreasesEdgeContrast() {
+        val w = 8; val h = 8
+        val a = IntArray(w * h) { i -> if (i % w < 4) px(80, 80, 80) else px(170, 170, 170) }
+        PhotoPro.denoiseSharpen(a, w, h, 0f, 1f)
+        assertTrue(rr(a[3]) < 80 || rr(a[4]) > 170)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun everyPresetKeepsSizeAndAlpha() {
+        for (pr in PhotoPro.Preset.values()) {
+            val w = 6; val h = 5
+            val a = IntArray(w * h) { i -> px((i * 9) % 256, (i * 17) % 256, (i * 31) % 256) }
+            PhotoPro.apply(a, w, h, pr)
+            assertEquals(w * h, a.size)
+            for (p in a) assertEquals(0xFF, p ushr 24)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test fun vignetteDarkensCornersNotCentre() {
+        val w = 21; val h = 21; val a = IntArray(w * h) { px(200, 200, 200) }
+        PhotoPro.vignette(a, w, h, 0.6f)
+        assertEquals(200, rr(a[10 * w + 10])); assertTrue(rr(a[0]) < 150)
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import com.nova.assistant.PlayInstallRules.Decision
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v6.97 wip14 Part 3: Play Store install decision rules. Pure text, no Android. */
+class PlayInstallRulesTest {
+    private val page = listOf("WhatsApp Messenger", "WhatsApp LLC", "4.3 star", "Install", "Add to wishlist", "About this app")
+
+    // ---- armed window
+    @Test fun armedOnlyForThatPackageAndOnly60Seconds() {
+        val st = HashMap<String, Long>()
+        PlayInstallRules.arm("com.whatsapp", 1_000L, st)
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("com.whatsapp", 1_000L, st))
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("com.whatsapp", 61_000L, st))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("com.whatsapp", 61_001L, st))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("com.instagram.android", 2_000L, st))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("", 2_000L, st))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun neverArmedMeansNever() {
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("com.nobody", 5L, HashMap()))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("com.whatsapp", 500L, hashMapOf("com.whatsapp" to 1_000L)))   // clock went backwards
+    }
+
+    @Test fun disarmClosesTheWindow() {
+        val st = HashMap<String, Long>()
+        PlayInstallRules.arm("com.whatsapp", 1_000L, st)
+        PlayInstallRules.disarm("com.whatsapp", st)
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.armedFor("com.whatsapp", 1_500L, st))
+    }
+
+    // ---- page title
+    @Test fun titleMatchesExactOrHarmlessSuffix() {
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(page, "WhatsApp"))
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(listOf("Instagram", "Install"), "Instagram"))
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(listOf("  instagram  "), "Instagram"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun lookalikeAppsDoNotMatch() {
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(listOf("WhatsApp Business", "Install"), "WhatsApp"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(listOf("YouTube Music", "Install"), "YouTube"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(listOf("Instagram Lite", "Install"), "Instagram"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(listOf("Install"), "WhatsApp"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.pageMatches(page, ""))
+    }
+
+    // ---- search result for an unknown app
+    @Test fun searchResultMustBeTheSpokenNameExactly() {
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches("Snapseed", "snapseed"))
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches("Snapseed App", "snapseed"))
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches("Google Keep", "google keep"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches("Snapseed Pro Editor", "snapseed"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches("Snap", "snapseed"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches("", "snapseed"))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.searchResultMatches("Snapseed", ""))
+    }
+
+    // ---- decide BEFORE the press
+    @Test fun exactInstallGivesTap() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.TAP, PlayInstallRules.decide(page, afterTap = false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun alreadyInstalledIsNotPressed() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.INSTALLED, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("WhatsApp Messenger", "Open", "Uninstall"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.INSTALLED, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("WhatsApp Messenger", "Open", "Update"), false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun paidAppIsNeverPressed() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.PAID, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Some App", "\u20B9149.00", "Add to wishlist"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.PAID, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Some App", "$4.99"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.PAID, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Some App", "Buy"), false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun signInIsNeverPressed() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.NEED_LOGIN, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Sign in"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.NEED_LOGIN, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Add account"), false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun installAnywayAndUpdateNeverGiveTap() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Install anyway", "Cancel"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Install anyway", "Install"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Some App", "Update"), false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun longDescriptionTextIsNotAButton() {
+        val txt = "Tap Install to download this app and enjoy messaging with friends"
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("WhatsApp Messenger", txt), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(emptyList(), false))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun installLabelMustBeExactNotJustContainingTheWord() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Installs: 5B+"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Install on more devices"), false))
+        assertEquals(Decision.TAP, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf(" INSTALL "), false))
+    }
+
+    // ---- decide AFTER the press
+    @Test fun progressWordsMeanStarted() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.STARTED, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("WhatsApp Messenger", "Cancel", "Downloading"), true))
+        assertEquals(Decision.STARTED, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Pending"), true))
+        assertEquals(Decision.STARTED, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Installing\u2026"), true))
+        assertEquals(Decision.STARTED, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Cancel"), true))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun finishedAtOnceCountsAsStarted() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.STARTED, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Open", "Uninstall"), true))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun installStillThereAfterTheTapIsNotSuccess() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(page, true))
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Install", "Cancel"), true))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun warningDialogAfterTheTapIsNotSuccess() {
+        assertEquals(Decision.WAIT, PlayInstallRules.decide(listOf("Install anyway", "Cancel"), true))
+    }
+
+    // ---- the single gate
+    @Test fun mayTapNeedsArmedAndRightPageAndInstall() {
+        val st = HashMap<String, Long>()
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.mayTap("com.whatsapp", "WhatsApp", page, 1_000L, st))          // not armed
+        PlayInstallRules.arm("com.whatsapp", 1_000L, st)
+        assertTrue(PlayInstallRules.mayTap("com.whatsapp", "WhatsApp", page, 2_000L, st))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.mayTap("com.whatsapp", "WhatsApp", page, 70_000L, st))         // window over
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.mayTap("com.whatsapp", "Instagram", page, 2_000L, st))         // wrong page
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.mayTap("com.whatsapp", "WhatsApp", listOf("WhatsApp Messenger", "\u20B9149.00"), 2_000L, st))
+        assertFalse(PlayInstallRules.mayTap("com.instagram.android", "WhatsApp", page, 2_000L, st)) // other package not armed
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v7.12 AUTOPSY R4: re-arming saved jobs (pure rules). The real AlarmManager behaviour is NOT tested here. */
+class ScheduleRestoreRulesTest {
+    private val now = 1_000_000_000L
+
+    @Test fun futureJobIsKept() { assertEquals(ScheduleRestoreRules.Plan.KEEP, ScheduleRestoreRules.planFor(now + 5_000, false, now)) }
+
+    @Test fun repeatingPastDueMovesToNext() { assertEquals(ScheduleRestoreRules.Plan.MOVE_NEXT, ScheduleRestoreRules.planFor(now - 3_600_000, true, now)) }
+
+    @Test fun oneShotJustPastDueFiresNow() { assertEquals(ScheduleRestoreRules.Plan.FIRE_NOW, ScheduleRestoreRules.planFor(now - 60_000, false, now)) }
+
+    @Test fun oneShotLongPastDueIsMissedNotMovedToTomorrow() {
+        assertEquals(ScheduleRestoreRules.Plan.DROP_MISSED, ScheduleRestoreRules.planFor(now - 3_600_000, false, now))
+        assertEquals(ScheduleRestoreRules.Plan.DROP_MISSED, ScheduleRestoreRules.planFor(now - ScheduleRestoreRules.GRACE_MS - 1, false, now))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun bootPlanOnlyBootResetsActive() {
+        val boot = ScheduleRestoreRules.bootPlan("android.intent.action.BOOT_COMPLETED")!!
+        assertTrue(boot.resetActive && boot.rearm && boot.askToStart)
+        for (a in listOf("android.intent.action.MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED", "android.intent.action.TIME_SET", "android.intent.action.TIMEZONE_CHANGED", "android.app.action.SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM_PERMISSION_STATE_CHANGED")) {
+            val p = ScheduleRestoreRules.bootPlan(a)!!
+            assertTrue(a, p.rearm && !p.resetActive && !p.askToStart)
+        }
+        assertNotNull(ScheduleRestoreRules.bootPlan("android.intent.action.QUICKBOOT_POWERON"))
+        assertNull(ScheduleRestoreRules.bootPlan("android.intent.action.SOMETHING_ELSE"))
+        assertNull(ScheduleRestoreRules.bootPlan(null))
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v7.03 G: SiteMedia.userBlock / applyUser are pure (no android classes). */
+class SiteMediaApplyTest {
+    private fun item(id: String, kind: String, place: MediaPlace.Place?, uri: String = "data:$kind/x;base64,QUJD") =
+        SiteMediaStore.Item(id, kind, uri, "", 0L, place)
+
+    @Test fun noItemsIsNoOp() {
+        val h = "<html><body>hi</body></html>"
+        val o = SiteMedia.applyUser(h, emptyList())
+        assertEquals(h, o.html); assertTrue(o.placed.isEmpty()); assertEquals("", SiteMedia.userBlock(emptyList()))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun imgPlaceholderGetsData() {
+        val o = SiteMedia.applyUser("<body><img src=\"NOVA_USER_MEDIA:m1\" alt=\"x\"></body>", listOf(item("m1", "image", MediaPlace.Place.HERO)))
+        assertTrue(o.html.contains("src=\"data:image/x;base64,QUJD\""))
+        assertFalse(o.html.contains("NOVA_USER_MEDIA"))
+        assertTrue(o.missing.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    @Test fun imgForVideoItemBecomesVideoTag() {
+        val o = SiteMedia.applyUser("<body><img src=\"NOVA_USER_MEDIA:m2\" alt=\"v\"></body>", listOf(item("m2", "video", MediaPlace.Place.GALLERY)))
+        assertTrue(o.html.contains("<video src=\"data:video/x;base64,QUJD\""))
+        assertFalse(o.html.contains("<img"))
+        assertFalse(o.html.contains("NOVA_USER_MEDIA"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun unreferencedHeroIsInjectedAfterHeader() {
+        val o = SiteMedia.applyUser("<html><body><header>H</header><main>M</main></body></html>", listOf(item("m1", "image", MediaPlace.Place.HERO)))
+        assertEquals(listOf("m1"), o.missing)
+        assertTrue(o.html.indexOf("data:image/x") > o.html.indexOf("</header>"))
+        assertTrue(o.html.indexOf("data:image/x") < o.html.indexOf("<main>"))
+        assertFalse(o.html.contains("NOVA_USER_MEDIA"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun unreferencedWithoutPlaceGoesBeforeBodyEnd() {
+        val o = SiteMedia.applyUser("<html><body><p>a</p></body></html>", listOf(item("m3", "image", null)))
+        assertTrue(o.html.indexOf("data:image/x") < o.html.indexOf("</body>"))
+        assertTrue(o.html.contains("<section"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun logoAndBackgroundAreInjected() {
+        val h = "<html><head></head><body><p>a</p></body></html>"
+        val o = SiteMedia.applyUser(h, listOf(item("m1", "image", MediaPlace.Place.LOGO), item("m2", "image", MediaPlace.Place.BACKGROUND)))
+        assertTrue(o.html.contains("background-image:url('data:image/x;base64,QUJD')"))
+        assertTrue(o.html.indexOf("height:56px") in 0 until o.html.indexOf("<p>a</p>"))
+        assertFalse(o.html.contains("NOVA_USER_MEDIA"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun dollarInDataIsSafe() {
+        val o = SiteMedia.applyUser("<body><img src=\"NOVA_USER_MEDIA:m1\"></body>", listOf(item("m1", "image", null, "data:image/jpeg;base64,A\$1B")))
+        assertTrue(o.html.contains("A\$1B"))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun promptBlockListsEveryItem() {
+        val b = SiteMedia.userBlock(listOf(item("m1", "image", MediaPlace.Place.HERO), item("m2", "video", null)))
+        assertTrue(b.contains("NOVA_USER_MEDIA:"))
+        assertTrue(b.contains("m1 = image, place: hero"))
+        assertTrue(b.contains("m2 = video"))
+    }
+}
