+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+class SocialReplyRulesTest {
+    private val ig = "com.instagram.android"
+    private val wa = "com.whatsapp"
+
+    @Test fun wishesAreWishes() {
+        for (t in listOf("Happy birthday bhai ❤️", "HBD sir 🎂", "Janamdin mubarak ho", "Congratulations!!"))
+            assertEquals(t, SocialReplyRules.Kind.WISH, SocialReplyRules.classify(t))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun niceCommentsAreNormal() {
+        for (t in listOf("Nice reel 🔥", "Bahut badiya video", "❤️❤️", "Thank you"))
+            assertEquals(t, SocialReplyRules.Kind.NORMAL, SocialReplyRules.classify(t))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun insultsAndShamingAreInsults() {
+        for (t in listOf("Tum fraud ho, sharam karo", "shame on you", "chutiya hai tu", "fake page hai ye", "Happy birthday you idiot"))
+            assertEquals(t, SocialReplyRules.Kind.INSULT, SocialReplyRules.classify(t))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun questionsAndUnknownStayWithTheOldRules() {
+        for (t in listOf("Price kya hai?", "size available hai kya", "order kab aayega", "ok", "kal milte hain", "Birthday kab hai?", "nice but price?", ""))
+            assertEquals(t, SocialReplyRules.Kind.OTHER, SocialReplyRules.classify(t))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun decisionPerApp() {
+        val ins = SocialReplyRules.decide(ig, "shame on you")!!
+        assertNull(ins.reply); assertTrue(ins.report)                     // Instagram: no reply, to report
+        val wins = SocialReplyRules.decide(wa, "shame on you")!!
+        assertNull(wins.reply); assertTrue(!wins.report)                  // WhatsApp: no reply, owner told, nothing to report
+        assertEquals(SocialReplyRules.THANKS_WISH, SocialReplyRules.decide(ig, "Happy birthday 🎂")!!.reply)
+        assertEquals(SocialReplyRules.THANKS_NORMAL, SocialReplyRules.decide(wa, "nice pic")!!.reply)
+        assertNull(SocialReplyRules.decide(ig, "price kya hai?"))
+        assertNull(SocialReplyRules.decide("com.telegram.messenger", "happy birthday"))   // other apps unchanged
+    }
+
+    @Test fun twoMessagesPerPersonPerHour() {
+        val g = AutoReplyRules.Gate()
+        val t0 = 1_000_000L
+        assertNull(g.allow("Anand", t0))
+        assertNull(g.allow("Anand", t0 + 11 * 60_000L))
+        assertEquals("limit", g.allow("Anand", t0 + 25 * 60_000L))
+        assertNull(g.allow("Anand", t0 + 75 * 60_000L))
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.junit.Assert.*
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v6.99 Part 2: spoken STOP rules. Pure text. Written, NOT RUN. */
+class StopRulesTest {
+    @Test fun wholeUtteranceStopsAreStops() {
+        for (s in listOf("ruko", "Ruk jao", "nova ruko", "stop", "bas karo", "roko", "rehne do", "cancel karo", "  stop   it  "))
+            assertTrue(s, StopRules.isStop(s))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun stopWordInsideACommandIsNotAStop() {
+        for (s in listOf("", "music stop karo", "torch jala do", "ruko mat gaana chalao", "stop the music", "nova ek billi ki photo banao"))
+            assertFalse(s, StopRules.isStop(s))
+    }
+
+    @Test fun logicClassifiesNewStopPhrases() {
+        for (s in listOf("ruk jao", "bas karo", "roko", "stop it"))
+            assertEquals(s, "stop", Logic.classify(s)?.kind)
+    }
+
+    @Test fun stopCounterDetectsCancellation() {
+        assertFalse(StopRules.wasStopped(3, 3))
+        assertTrue(StopRules.wasStopped(3, 4))
+    }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** v7.05 H: the pure parts of VercelPublish (needs the real org.json, like the other JSON tests). */
+class VercelPublishTest {
+    @Test fun projectNameIsSafe() {
+        val n = VercelPublish.projectName("Mera Gym & Fitness!!", 12345L)
+        assertTrue(n.matches(Regex("[a-z0-9-]+")))
+        assertTrue(n.startsWith("nova-")); assertTrue(n.length <= 60)
+    }
+    @Test fun bodyHasIndexHtmlAndProduction() {
+        val o = JSONObject(VercelPublish.buildBody("nova-x-1", "<html>hi \"q\"</html>"))
+        assertEquals("nova-x-1", o.getString("name")); assertEquals("production", o.getString("target"))
+        val f = o.getJSONArray("files").getJSONObject(0)
+        assertEquals("index.html", f.getString("file")); assertEquals("utf-8", f.getString("encoding")); assertEquals("<html>hi \"q\"</html>", f.getString("data"))
+    }
+    @Test fun urlFromAliasThenUrl() {
+        assertEquals("https://my-site.vercel.app", VercelPublish.parseUrl("{\"url\":\"nova-x-1-abc.vercel.app\",\"alias\":[\"my-site.vercel.app\"]}"))
+        assertEquals("https://nova-x-1-abc.vercel.app", VercelPublish.parseUrl("{\"url\":\"nova-x-1-abc.vercel.app\"}"))
+    }
+    @Test fun neverInventsAUrl() {
+        assertEquals("", VercelPublish.parseUrl("{}"))
+        assertEquals("", VercelPublish.parseUrl("not json"))
+        assertEquals("", VercelPublish.parseUrl("{\"url\":\"evil.com/x?a=b\"}"))
+    }
+}

+/** v7.12 AUTOPSY R3: result texts for video recording (pure). The real camera / MediaRecorder behaviour is NOT tested here. */
+class VideoResultRulesTest {
+    @Test fun savedStatusesAreGood() { for (s in listOf("saved", "saved_max", "saved_space")) assertTrue(s, VideoResultRules.forStatus(s)!!.good) }
+
+    @Test fun failuresAreNeverCalledSaved() {
+        for (s in listOf("no_frames", "stop_failed", "save_failed", "permission", "not_visible", "failed:camera busy")) {
+            val t = VideoResultRules.forStatus(s)!!
+            assertFalse(s, t.good)
+            assertFalse(s, t.title.contains("save ho gayi"))
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test fun stopFailedTellsWhereTheFileIs() { assertTrue(VideoResultRules.forStatus("stop_failed")!!.body.contains("app folder")) }
+
+    @Test fun failedCarriesTheReason() { assertTrue(VideoResultRules.forStatus("failed:no_camera")!!.body.contains("no_camera")) }
+
+    @Test fun unknownStatusHasNoText() { assertNull(VideoResultRules.forStatus("already")); assertNull(VideoResultRules.forStatus("on")); assertNotNull(VideoResultRules.forStatus("saved")) }
+
+    @Test fun startingIsNotAResult() { assertEquals(null, VideoResultRules.forStatus("starting")) }

+# NOVA autopsy - PART 3 of 6 (music / YouTube / WhatsApp / calls) - v6.99 source
+Evidence: PASS = script in sandbox, NOT RUN = needs compile / phone.
+## Found + fixed (written + statically checked; Kotlin compile NOT RUN, phone NOT TESTED)
+1. MUSIC false success: musicPlay() used raw startActivity. Android 10+ blocks background starts WITHOUT an exception, so NOVA said "<app> mein <song> chala diya" while nothing opened. Fix: every start goes through launch() (checks the app came to front, 3 ways, honest reason). New flag lastLaunchBlocked stops the pointless extra fallbacks and gives the real reason at once.
+2. CALL false success: callTool() with CALL_PHONE allowed used raw startActivity(ACTION_CALL) and said "call request sent" even when blocked, with no dialer fallback. Fix: ACTION_CALL goes through launch(); if it does not open, the old dialer fallback runs.
+## Checked, no code change
+- WhatsApp send: installed-app check, exact package (never the other WhatsApp), wa.me + setPackage, duplicate guard, Accessibility presses Send only for the exact confirmed text. OK (static).
+- WhatsApp voice/video call: not possible via a public Android intent. NOVA refuses and offers the chat, never a normal call. By design.
+- executeChosenCall (contact picker): left as is. The picker only exists while the NOVA screen is visible, where starts are not blocked, and launch() would sleep on the main thread.
+- Answer / reject call: notification button first, TelecomManager needs ANSWER_PHONE_CALLS, "no_permission" is spoken. OK (static).
+- YouTube: tool play_music only opens YouTube SEARCH RESULTS (no autoplay). "youtube kholo" = open app. Music apps in MusicPlayRules do not include plain YouTube; "youtube pe X chalao" goes to AppSearchRules (play-from-search, then search box, then tap first result via Accessibility).
+- WhatsApp short form "whatsapp <name> <message>" takes the FIRST TWO words as the contact. One-word contact + message needs "<name> ko ... bhejo". Left as spec.
+- WhatsApp READ: notification-based (NovaNotificationService + drive/message modes). NOT traced in this part -> Part 4 or 5.
+## Tests
+tools/test_autopsy_part3_contract.py (6 checks PASS). run_all_checks: 68 PASS, 1 FAIL = test_vosk_model_packaging (needs .github/workflows, not in zip: ignore).
+Kotlin compile not possible here (android.jar host blocked): GitHub Actions build must be read first.

+# AUTOPSY R4 (routines / scheduled tasks / settings bridge)
+Traced: ScheduledTaskRules (parser), ScheduledTaskStore, ScheduledTaskReceiver, BootReceiver, WakeAlarmReceiver, RoutineRules, NovaService.scheduleCreate / onScheduledTask, MainActivity schedule bridge + getSettings / saveSettings, ui.html SAVE path.
+Fixed (all proven by reading the code):
+1. add(): if AlarmManager was missing or threw, the job was still reported "saved, may be delayed" although NO alarm existed. Now status alarm_failed, entry rolled back, voice + UI say NOT saved.
+2. consume(): a repeating job re-armed its next alarm only if the storage write succeeded; a failed write killed the job silently. Now always re-armed (failure logged).
+3. Android deletes ALL alarms when the APK is updated (owner updates often) and absolute alarms drift on time / zone change; only a reboot re-armed them. Now BootReceiver also re-arms on MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED, TIME_SET, TIMEZONE_CHANGED, exact-alarm permission change, and MainActivity re-arms at every open.
+4. rescheduleAll moved a past-due ONE-SHOT job to tomorrow (stale reminder a day late, and never saved). Now it is dropped and reported once as "missed"; repeating jobs move to the next real time and are saved.
+Checked, no bug: ScheduledTaskRules.parse / nextAtMillis / encode-decode, 30-job limit, sensitive-text refusal, spoken send needs yes (default skip_order_confirm = true runs at once and tells after), saveSettings return codes vs UI handling (ok / need_acc / bad_wake / error all handled).
+Owner-required UI guarded by a test (never remove): HOME tab, ACTIVATE button on home, Settings SAVE = done (closes panel + assemble animation), 3D live orb (WebGL2 canvas #orb3d).
+NOT TESTED: Kotlin compile (kotlinc absent), real phone alarms, notification permission denied case (notification cannot be shown then, nothing more to do).
+Open: WEATHER / SEND_MESSAGE jobs only run while NOVA is ACTIVE (else a notification); onScheduledTask returns silently when service not alive.
+Test: tools/test_autopsy_part7_contract.py (16 checks PASS). All contract scripts: 72 PASS, 1 known FAIL (test_vosk_model_packaging needs .github/workflows, not in zip).

+- Wake word "nova" (Vosk offline), listening, spoken replies, CHUP stop button.

+## ARCHITECTURE NOTES FOR THE NEW WORK
+- NovaService.launch(i, expectPkg, what) is the one way to start an activity (service, Accessibility and PendingIntent fallbacks, honest result). Use it for every tab.
+- R(ok, msg) is the result type; tr(hindi, english) picks the language; claimAction() blocks double sends; WhatsAppTarget has the two WhatsApp package ids.
+- QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES is in the manifest, so getApplicationInfo / getLaunchIntentForPackage work for any package.
+- Accessibility config only listens to window-state events, but rootInActiveWindow works anyway; `collect()` and `findLabel()` in NovaAccessibilityService.companion are private helpers you can reuse inside playInstall.

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.03 G: where on the website does a picked gallery photo / video go? Pure Kotlin (no Android classes), Roman text only
+ * (HindiRoman already converts Devanagari). "ye photo hero mein lagao", "dusri photo gallery mein", "m2 logo banao header mein".
+ * It only decides; it never sends anything anywhere.
+ * tools/test_site_media_contract.py reads the sets below straight from this file, so keep one set per line.
+ */
+object MediaPlace {
+    enum class Place { HERO, ABOUT, GALLERY, LOGO, MENU, FOOTER, BACKGROUND }
+
+    private val HERO = setOf("hero", "banner", "top", "upar", "oopar", "cover", "front")
+    private val ABOUT = setOf("about", "aboutus", "baare", "bare")
+    private val GALLERY = setOf("gallery", "album", "grid")
+    private val LOGO = setOf("logo", "header", "navbar", "brand")
+    private val MENU = setOf("menu", "products", "services", "offerings")
+    private val FOOTER = setOf("footer", "contact", "neeche", "niche", "bottom")
+    private val BACKGROUND = setOf("background", "bg", "wallpaper", "peeche")
+    private val ACTS = setOf("lagao", "lagana", "laga", "lagado", "lagade", "rakho", "rakhna", "daalo", "dalo", "daal", "dalna", "use", "set", "add", "jodo", "put", "place")
+    private val MEDIA_REF = setOf("ye", "yeh", "is", "isko", "ise", "photo", "photos", "image", "images", "video", "videos", "picture", "pic", "foto", "tasveer", "clip", "reel")
+    private val FIRST = setOf("pehli", "pehla", "pahli", "pahla", "first")
+    private val SECOND = setOf("dusri", "doosri", "dusra", "doosra", "second")
+    private val THIRD = setOf("teesri", "teesra", "third")
+    private val FOURTH = setOf("chauthi", "chautha", "fourth")
+    private val LAST = setOf("last", "aakhri", "akhri")
+
+    fun tokens(s: String): List<String> {
+        val sb = StringBuilder()
+        for (ch in s.lowercase()) {
+            if ((ch in 'a'..'z') || (ch in '0'..'9')) sb.append(ch) else sb.append(' ')
+        }
+        val out = ArrayList<String>()
+        for (w in sb.toString().split(' ')) if (w.isNotEmpty()) out.add(w)
+        return out
+    }
+
+    /** First place word in the sentence (sentence order), or null. */
+    fun parse(text: String): Place? {
+        for (w in tokens(text)) {
+            when (w) {
+                in HERO -> return Place.HERO
+                in ABOUT -> return Place.ABOUT
+                in GALLERY -> return Place.GALLERY
+                in LOGO -> return Place.LOGO
+                in MENU -> return Place.MENU
+                in FOOTER -> return Place.FOOTER
+                in BACKGROUND -> return Place.BACKGROUND
+            }
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    /** 1-based item number ("pehli photo" = 1, "m3" = 3), -1 = the last one, null = not said. */
+    fun itemIndex(text: String): Int? {
+        for (w in tokens(text)) {
+            when {
+                w in FIRST -> return 1
+                w in SECOND -> return 2
+                w in THIRD -> return 3
+                w in FOURTH -> return 4
+                w in LAST -> return -1
+                w.length == 2 && w[0] == 'm' && w[1] in '1'..'6' -> return w[1] - '0'
+            }
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    /** "ye photo hero mein lagao": a place word + a put-it verb + a media reference. Caller also checks that picked media exist. */
+    fun isPlaceCommand(text: String): Boolean {
+        val t = tokens(text)
+        if (t.isEmpty() || t.size > 30) return false
+        if (parse(text) == null) return false
+        if (t.none { it in ACTS }) return false
+        return itemIndex(text) != null || t.any { it in MEDIA_REF }
+    }
+
+    fun label(p: Place?): String = when (p) {
+        Place.HERO -> "hero"
+        Place.ABOUT -> "about"
+        Place.GALLERY -> "gallery"
+        Place.LOGO -> "logo"
+        Place.MENU -> "menu"
+        Place.FOOTER -> "footer"
+        Place.BACKGROUND -> "background"
+        null -> "sahi jagah"
+    }
+
+    fun fromName(name: String): Place? = Place.values().firstOrNull { it.name.equals(name.trim(), true) }
+}

+package com.nova.assistant
+
+/**
+ * v7.03 G: the photos / videos the owner picked from the phone gallery for the website (max 6, kept 6 hours, memory only).
+ * Each item already holds its final data: URI, so the website stays ONE self-contained html file. Pure Kotlin (no Android classes).
+ * The Android side (MainActivity) reads and shrinks the file, then calls add().
+ */
+object SiteMediaStore {
+    class Item(val id: String, val kind: String, val uri: String, val thumb: String, val addedAt: Long, @Volatile var place: MediaPlace.Place?)
+
+    const val MAX_ITEMS = 6
+    const val MAX_TOTAL_CHARS = 12_000_000      // data URIs are base64: ~9 MB of real files; the whole page must stay far below 16 MB
+    private const val KEEP_MS = 6 * 60 * 60_000L
+    private val list = ArrayList<Item>()
+
+    @Synchronized private fun prune(now: Long) { list.removeAll { now - it.addedAt > KEEP_MS } }
+
+    @Synchronized fun items(now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): List<Item> { prune(now); return ArrayList(list) }
+
+    /** Returns the new id (m1..m6) or null when full / too big. kind = "image" | "video". */
+    @Synchronized fun add(kind: String, uri: String, thumb: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): String? {
+        prune(now)
+        if (kind != "image" && kind != "video") return null
+        if (list.size >= MAX_ITEMS) return null
+        if (list.sumOf { it.uri.length } + uri.length > MAX_TOTAL_CHARS) return null
+        val used = list.map { it.id }.toSet()
+        val id = (1..MAX_ITEMS).map { "m$it" }.firstOrNull { it !in used } ?: return null
+        list.add(Item(id, kind, uri, thumb, now, null))
+        return id
+    }
+
+    @Synchronized fun setPlace(id: String, place: MediaPlace.Place?): Boolean {
+        val it = list.firstOrNull { x -> x.id == id } ?: return false
+        it.place = place
+        return true
+    }
+
+    @Synchronized fun clear() { list.clear() }
+
+    /**
+     * Typed / spoken placement: "dusri photo gallery mein lagao" (item 2), "ye photo hero mein" (the newest item that has no place yet).
+     * Returns how many items got a place. Never touches an item that already has a place unless the sentence names its number.
+     */
+    @Synchronized fun applyText(text: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Int {
+        prune(now)
+        if (list.isEmpty()) return 0
+        val place = MediaPlace.parse(text) ?: return 0
+        val idx = MediaPlace.itemIndex(text)
+        val target: Item? = when {
+            idx == null -> list.lastOrNull { it.place == null }
+            idx == -1 -> list.last()
+            else -> list.getOrNull(idx - 1)
+        }
+        if (target == null) return 0
+        target.place = place
+        return 1
+    }
+}

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""v7.00 AUTO MEDIA static contract (python mirror of MediaIntent + wiring checks). Evidence: static only, NOT a Kotlin compile."""
+import re, os, sys
+H = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); R = os.path.join(H, '..')
+rd = lambda p: open(os.path.join(R, p), encoding='utf8').read()
+mi = rd('app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/MediaIntent.kt')
+def sset(n):
+    m = re.search(r'private val ' + n + r' = setOf\((.*?)\)\n', mi, re.S); assert m, n
+    return set(re.findall(r'"([a-z0-9]+)"', m.group(1)))
+BUILD, SITE, IMG, VID, OTHER, NOMEDIA = (sset(n) for n in ('BUILD', 'SITE_NOUNS', 'IMG_WORDS', 'VIDEO_WORDS', 'OTHER', 'NO_MEDIA'))
+tok = lambda s: re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]', ' ', s.lower()).split()
+def classify(text):
+    t = tok(text)
+    if not t or len(t) > 60: return None
+    b = s = i = v = False
+    for w in t:
+        if w in OTHER: return None
+        b |= w in BUILD; s |= w in SITE; i |= w in IMG; v |= w in VID
+    if not b: return None
+    if s: return 'SITE' if (i or v) else None
+    if v and not i: return 'VIDEO'
+    return None
+def wants_images(text):
+    t = tok(text)
+    for k, w in enumerate(t):
+        if w in NOMEDIA and any(t[j] in IMG for j in range(max(0, k - 2), min(len(t) - 1, k + 2) + 1)): return False
+    return not ('text' in t and ('sirf' in t or 'only' in t))
+bad = 0
+def chk(n, c):
+    global bad; print(('PASS ' if c else 'FAIL ') + n); bad += 0 if c else 1
+chk('website + photos -> SITE', classify('nova ek restaurant ki website banao jisme photos ho') == 'SITE')
+chk('plain photo -> old ImageIntent (None)', classify('nova ek billi ki photo banao') is None)
+chk('plain website -> old SiteIntent (None)', classify('nova ek gym ki website banao') is None)
+chk('video banao -> VIDEO', classify('nova ek video banao') == 'VIDEO')
+chk('background video recording -> None', classify('nova background me video recording on karo') is None)
+chk('bina photo -> SITE, images off', classify('nova bina photo ke ek gym ki website banao') == 'SITE' and not wants_images('nova bina photo ke ek gym ki website banao'))
+chk('images default on', wants_images('nova restaurant website banao'))
+ns = rd('app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/NovaService.kt'); sg = rd('app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/SiteGen.kt'); sm = rd('app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/SiteMedia.kt')
+for n, c in [('NovaService mediaRoute', 'private fun mediaRoute' in ns and ns.count('mediaRoute(') >= 3), ('NovaService lastSiteRawHtml'.replace('NovaService ', ''), 'lastSiteRawHtml' in ns),
+             ('SiteMedia.resolve in siteBegin', 'SiteMedia.resolve(this' in ns), ('SiteGen uses SiteMedia.promptBlock', sg.count('SiteMedia.promptBlock') >= 3),
+             ('SiteMedia has no publish / no token', 'GitHubPublish' not in sm and 'SecureStore' not in sm), ('SiteMedia caps pictures', 'MAX_IMAGES = 4' in sm)]:
+    chk(n, c)
+ui = rd('app/src/main/assets/ui.html')
+for i in ['c_imode', 'c_photo', 'st_free', 'st_img', 'i_on', 'i_tog', 'i_hf', 'vr_start', 'vr_stop', 't_home', 't_orb', 'hub_b']:
+    chk('ui id still present: ' + i, ('id="' + i + '"' in ui) or ("id='" + i + "'" in ui) or ("'" + i + "'" in ui))
+chk('hide css for old image buttons', '#c_imode,#c_photo,#st_free,#st_img{display:none!important}' in ui)
+chk('old HOME tab not hidden', '#t_home{display:none' not in ui)
+sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""AUTOPSY PART 2 (static): wake word must work with screen off; overlay nudge must repeat; APK hash must be verified."""
+import pathlib, sys
+R = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "app/src/main"
+J = R / "java/com/nova/assistant"
+rd = lambda p: (R / p if "/" in p else J / p).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+svc, cfg, ma, ui, um = rd("NovaService.kt"), rd("ExtCfg.kt"), rd("MainActivity.kt"), rd("assets/ui.html"), rd("UpdateManager.kt")
+bad = 0
+def check(n, ok):
+    global bad
+    print(("PASS " if ok else "FAIL ") + n); bad += 0 if ok else 1
+check("listenLocked default ON", "fun listenLocked" in cfg and "getBoolean(LISTEN_LOCKED, true)" in cfg)
+check("lockQuiet uses listenLocked", "ExtCfg.lockGuard(this) && !ExtCfg.listenLocked(this)" in svc)
+check("bridge get/set listenLocked", "fun getListenLocked" in ma and "fun setListenLocked" in ma)
+check("UI switch wb_ll wired", 'id="wb_ll"' in ui and "Android.setListenLocked" in ui and "Android.getListenLocked" in ui)
+check("overlay nudge repeats after 10 min", "overlayNudgedAt" in svc and "600_000L" in svc.split("private fun overlayNudge")[1][:400])
+check("APK/model download hash verified", 'Updater.hex(md.digest()) != item.sha256' in um and "part.delete()" in um)
+print("failed:", bad); sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""AUTOPSY R5 (static): sanity of the R4 edits that could not be compiled (kotlinc absent) + final-report presence."""
+import pathlib, sys, re
+import xml.dom.minidom as md
+R = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+J = R / "app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant"
+bad = 0
+def check(n, ok):
+    global bad
+    print(("PASS " if ok else "FAIL ") + n); bad += 0 if ok else 1
+
+def strip(src):
+    src = re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/', '', src, flags=re.S)
+    src = re.sub(r'"(?:\\.|[^"\\\n])*"', '""', src)
+    src = re.sub(r"'(?:\\.|[^'\\\n])'", "''", src)
+    return re.sub(r'//[^\n]*', '', src)
+# The tiny stripper cannot read Kotlin raw strings / templates, so two big old files show a constant offset that was
+# ALREADY there in the zip before R4 (measured on the original). The R4 edits must keep that offset unchanged.
+BASE = {"ScheduledTaskStore.kt": (0, 0), "ScheduledTaskReceiver.kt": (0, 0), "BootReceiver.kt": (0, 0),
+        "MainActivity.kt": (5, 1), "NovaService.kt": (3, 4)}
+for f, (bb, bp) in BASE.items():
+    s = strip((J / f).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    check(f + " brace/paren balance unchanged by R4 edits", (s.count("{") - s.count("}"), s.count("(") - s.count(")")) == (bb, bp))
+try:
+    md.parse(str(R / "app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml")); ok = True
+except Exception as e:
+    ok = False
+check("AndroidManifest.xml is well-formed XML", ok)
+st = (J / "ScheduledTaskStore.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+check("every ALARM_* constant used is declared", all(("const val " + c) in st for c in ("ALARM_EXACT", "ALARM_INEXACT", "ALARM_FAILED")))
+rc = (J / "ScheduledTaskReceiver.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+check("notifyMissed/notifySkipped share postNote", rc.count("postNote(") >= 3)
+check("final report exists", (R / "docs/AUTOPSY_FINAL_REPORT.md").exists())
+check("live owner-UI check script exists", (R / "tools/ui_owner_check.py").exists())
+print("failed:", bad); sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""v6.99 Part 3 download / install speech spellings: static contract. Run from the project root."""
+import os,sys
+R=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),'..')
+J=os.path.join(R,'app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/')
+O=open(J+'OpenWebRules.kt',encoding='utf-8').read()
+bad=[]
+def need(c,m):
+    if not c: bad.append(m)
+i=O.find('private val DL_SPELLINGS'); b=O.find('private val BLOCK')
+need(i>0 and b>0 and i<b,'DL_SPELLINGS must be declared BEFORE BLOCK (Kotlin object init order, otherwise NPE)')
+blk=O[i:O.find(')',i)]
+for w in ('daunlod','instol','dawnload','downlod','download','install'): need('"%s"'%w in blk,'spelling missing: '+w)
+need('.plus(DL_SPELLINGS)' in O,'BLOCK must include DL_SPELLINGS')
+need('private val DL_VERBS = DL_SPELLINGS' in O,'DL_VERBS must reuse DL_SPELLINGS')
+need('bdown +lo+a?d' in O,'two-word "down load" fix missing in norm')
+need('"daunlod"' in open(J+'HelpGuide.kt',encoding='utf-8').read(),'HelpGuide download keys missing misspellings')
+need('downloadSpeechSpellingsAreUnderstood' in open(os.path.join(R,'app/src/test/java/com/nova/assistant/OpenWebRulesTest.kt'),encoding='utf-8').read(),'new JUnit tests missing')
+if bad:
+    for m in bad: print('FAIL',m)
+    sys.exit(1)
+print('PASS download spellings contract: Roman + Devanagari + two-word forms covered')

+"""NO FEATURE LOSS guard: every element id that existed in ui.html at baseline (v6.99 upload, tools/ui_ids_baseline.txt)
+must still exist. Adding ids is fine, removing one FAILS. If an id was truly renamed, ADD the new one and keep the old (hidden)."""
+import re, os, sys
+HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
+ui = open(os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'app', 'src', 'main', 'assets', 'ui.html'), encoding='utf8').read()
+now = set(re.findall(r'\bid="([^"]+)"', ui)) | set(re.findall(r"""\.id\s*=\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]""", ui))
+base = [l.strip() for l in open(os.path.join(HERE, 'ui_ids_baseline.txt')) if l.strip()]
+missing = [i for i in base if i not in now]
+print(('PASS' if not missing else 'FAIL') + ' no feature loss: %d baseline ids, missing %s' % (len(base), missing[:20]))
+for need in ['hub_b', 'au_open', 'ws_go', 'tr_box', 'c_plus', 'btn', 'quiet', 's_save', 'orb3d', 'u_feed', 'gh_save']:
+    ok = need in now
+    print(('PASS' if ok else 'FAIL') + ' id ' + need); missing += [] if ok else [need]
+sys.exit(1 if missing else 0)

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""v6.97 wip14: static contract for 'open websites in Chrome tabs' + 'Play Store download'. No Android, no network.
+Proves the SAFETY shape only (not that it works on a phone)."""
+import re, sys, pathlib
+
+root = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+src = root / "app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant"
+ctl = (src / "Control.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+acc = (src / "NovaAccessibilityService.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+svc = (src / "NovaService.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+rul = (src / "PlayInstallRules.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+fails = []
+
+def check(ok, msg):
+    if not ok:
+        fails.append(msg)
+
+# 1. the generic voice tap stays blocked in the Play Store
+m = re.search(r"BLOCKED_PACKAGES\s*=\s*setOf\((.*?)\)", ctl, re.S)
+check(m is not None and "com.android.vending" in m.group(1), "Control.BLOCKED_PACKAGES must still hold com.android.vending")
+
+# 2. the 60 s window
+check("WINDOW_MS = 60_000L" in rul, "PlayInstallRules.WINDOW_MS must be 60_000L")
+
+# 3. playInstall: only the exact Install node, only after mayTap, one press (disarm before click)
+pi = re.search(r"fun playInstall\(.*?\n        }\n", acc, re.S)
+check(pi is not None, "playInstall not found")
+if pi:
+    body = pi.group(0)
+    check(body.count("clickNode(") == 1, "playInstall must click exactly once")
+    check("PlayInstallRules.mayTap(" in body, "playInstall must call PlayInstallRules.mayTap before pressing")
+    check("exactInstallNode(root)" in body, "playInstall must press only exactInstallNode")
+    check(body.index("PlayInstallRules.disarm(key)") < body.index("clickNode("), "playInstall must disarm before the click (one press only)")
+    check(not re.search(r'"(Update|Buy|Install anyway|Sign in|Purchase)"', body), "playInstall must never name a button other than Install")
+ex = re.search(r"fun exactInstallNode\(.*?\n        }\n", acc, re.S)
+check(ex is not None and 't.equals("Install", ignoreCase = true)' in ex.group(0), "exactInstallNode must compare the WHOLE text with Install")
+rt = re.search(r"fun playRoot\(.*?\n        }\n", acc, re.S)
+check(rt is not None and acc.count("PLAY_PKG") >= 3 and "== PLAY_PKG" in rt.group(0), "playRoot must only return the com.android.vending window")
+
+# 4. nothing else in the accessibility service clicks inside the Play Store: tapLabel still goes through Control.blocked
+tl = re.search(r"fun tapLabel\(.*?\n        }\n", acc, re.S)
+check(tl is not None and 'Control.blocked(' in tl.group(0), "tapLabel must still call Control.blocked")
+
+# 5. handler order in NovaService.handle(): web open -> download -> agent task
+a = svc.find("OpenWebRules.parseOpen(text)")
+b = svc.find("OpenWebRules.parseDownload(text)")
+c = svc.find("AgentRules.isTask(text)")
+check(0 < a < b < c, "handle(): parseOpen, then parseDownload, both BEFORE AgentRules.isTask")
+
+# 6. downloadApp is honest: success word only for the 'started' code, and the Play intent is pinned to the Play Store
+dl = re.search(r"private fun downloadApp\(.*?\n    }\n", svc, re.S)
+check(dl is not None, "downloadApp not found")
+if dl:
+    d = dl.group(0)
+    check(d.count("download start ho gaya") == 1 and '"started" ->' in d, "'download start ho gaya' only for code started")
+    check("setPackage(PLAY_PKG)" in d, "Play intent must be pinned to com.android.vending")
+    check("PlayInstallRules.arm(" in d and "PlayInstallRules.disarm(" in d, "downloadApp must arm and disarm")
+
+# 7. Chrome tabs: no Browser.EXTRA_APPLICATION_ID (it would reuse one tab)
+check("EXTRA_APPLICATION_ID" not in svc.split("fun openChromeTab")[1].split("fun openAppOrSite")[0], "openChromeTab must not set EXTRA_APPLICATION_ID")
+
+# 8. NOVA KNOWS the features: Help topics (voice/chat answer + AI facts), MADAD popup (ui.html), Skills screen, self-test
+hg = (src / "HelpGuide.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+ui = (root / "app/src/main/assets/ui.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+fc = (src / "FeatureCheck.kt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+check('Topic("chrome"' in hg and 'Topic("download"' in hg, "HelpGuide must have the chrome and download topics")
+check('"id": "chrome"' in ui and '"id": "download"' in ui, "ui.html MADAD list (CAPS) must have chrome and download")
+check("nova whatsapp download karo" in ui and "nova youtube chrome pe kholo" in ui, "ui.html Skills screen must show both example commands")
+check("case 'download'" in ui and "download:['ACCESSIBILITY ON KARO'" in ui, "ui.html must show the Accessibility need for download")
+check("OpenWebRules.parseOpen(" in fc and "OpenWebRules.parseDownload(" in fc, "FeatureCheck self-test must check both new sentences")
+
+if fails:
+    for f in fails:
+        print("FAIL:", f)
+    sys.exit(1)
+print("OK: open-web / download contract")

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""v7.03 G static contract: gallery photo/video in websites. Python mirror of MediaPlace + wiring checks. Evidence: static only (the Kotlin compile is tools/local_compile_check.sh)."""
+import re, os, sys
+H = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); R = os.path.join(H, '..')
+rd = lambda p: open(os.path.join(R, p), encoding='utf8').read()
+J = 'app/src/main/java/com/nova/assistant/'
+mp = rd(J + 'MediaPlace.kt')
+def sset(n):
+    m = re.search(r'private val ' + n + r' = setOf\((.*?)\)\n', mp, re.S); assert m, n
+    return set(re.findall(r'"([a-z0-9]+)"', m.group(1)))
+order = ['HERO', 'ABOUT', 'GALLERY', 'LOGO', 'MENU', 'FOOTER', 'BACKGROUND']
+S = {n: sset(n) for n in order}
+ACTS, REF = sset('ACTS'), sset('MEDIA_REF')
+tok = lambda s: re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]', ' ', s.lower()).split()
+def parse(t):
+    for w in tok(t):
+        for n in order:
+            if w in S[n]: return n
+    return None
+def idx(t):
+    for w in tok(t):
+        if w in sset('FIRST'): return 1
+        if w in sset('SECOND'): return 2
+        if w in sset('THIRD'): return 3
+        if w in sset('FOURTH'): return 4
+        if w in sset('LAST'): return -1
+        if len(w) == 2 and w[0] == 'm' and w[1] in '123456': return int(w[1])
+    return None
+def is_cmd(t):
+    k = tok(t)
+    if not k or len(k) > 30 or parse(t) is None or not any(w in ACTS for w in k): return False
+    return idx(t) is not None or any(w in REF for w in k)
+bad = 0
+def chk(n, c):
+    global bad; print(('PASS ' if c else 'FAIL ') + n); bad += 0 if c else 1
+chk('place hero', parse('ye photo hero mein lagao') == 'HERO')
+chk('place gallery', parse('dusri photo gallery mein') == 'GALLERY')
+chk('no place', parse('ek restaurant ki website banao') is None)
+chk('index second', idx('dusri photo gallery mein') == 2 and idx('m3 logo') == 3 and idx('last photo') == -1)
+chk('place command yes', is_cmd('ye photo hero mein lagao') and is_cmd('m2 gallery mein rakho'))
+chk('place command no', not is_cmd('hero mein') and not is_cmd('photo lagao') and not is_cmd('aaj ka mausam batao'))
+sm, sg, ns, ma, ui, st = (rd(J + 'SiteMedia.kt'), rd(J + 'SiteGen.kt'), rd(J + 'NovaService.kt'), rd(J + 'MainActivity.kt'), rd('app/src/main/assets/ui.html'), rd(J + 'SiteMediaStore.kt'))
+chk('NOVA_USER_MEDIA in SiteMedia', 'NOVA_USER_MEDIA:' in sm and 'fun applyUser' in sm and 'fun userBlock' in sm)
+chk('SiteGen uses userBlock 3 times', sg.count('SiteMedia.userBlock(SiteMediaStore.items())') == 3)
+chk('NovaService applyUser + applyText + place command', 'SiteMedia.applyUser(' in ns and 'SiteMediaStore.applyText(' in ns and 'MediaPlace.isPlaceCommand(' in ns)
+chk('lastSiteRawHtml keeps placeholders', 'lastSiteRawHtml = r.html' in ns)
+chk('MainActivity REQ_SITE_MEDIA before data?.data', 'const val REQ_SITE_MEDIA' in ma and ma.index('requestCode == REQ_SITE_MEDIA') < ma.index('val uri = data?.data ?: return'))
+chk('MainActivity bridge methods', all(('fun ' + n) in ma for n in ('pickSiteMedia', 'setSiteMediaPlace', 'clearSiteMedia', 'getSiteMedia')))
+chk('video cap 5 MB', '5_000_000' in ma)
+chk('store max 6', 'MAX_ITEMS = 6' in st)
+for i in ('sm_pick', 'sm_clr', 'sm_list'): chk('ui id ' + i, ('id="%s"' % i) in ui)
+chk('ui id sm_box', "box.id='sm_box'" in ui)
+chk('ui onSiteMedia/Err', 'window.onSiteMedia=' in ui and 'window.onSiteMediaErr=' in ui)
+blk = ui[ui.index('id="nova-site-media-js"'):]
+chk("new JS has no $('literal') / g('literal')", not re.search(r"(?<![\w.])[$g]\('", blk))
+chk('new JS uses document.getElementById', 'document.getElementById' in blk)
+sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

diff --git a/tools/test_wiring_audit_contract.py b/tools/test_wiring_audit_contract.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..75307d6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/test_wiring_audit_contract.py
@@ -0,0 +1,34 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""AUTOPSY PART 1 wiring audit (static, no compile). Fails when a feature is written but not wired:
+ 1. every A('x') / Android.x() call in assets/ui.html has a @JavascriptInterface fun x in MainActivity.kt
+ 2. every @JavascriptInterface fun in MainActivity.kt is referenced by name in ui.html (no invisible feature)
+ 3. every command kind Logic.kt can produce has an executor branch in NovaService.kt or runCommand
+ 4. every manifest component class exists as Kotlin source
+ 5. every element hidden by default in ui.html is referenced by JS (can be shown)"""
+import re, sys, glob, os
+R = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), '..')
+M = os.path.join(R, 'app/src/main')
+J = os.path.join(M, 'java/com/nova/assistant')
+h = open(os.path.join(M, 'assets/ui.html'), encoding='utf8').read()
+k = open(os.path.join(J, 'MainActivity.kt'), encoding='utf8').read()
+svc = open(os.path.join(J, 'NovaService.kt'), encoding='utf8').read()
+lg = open(os.path.join(J, 'Logic.kt'), encoding='utf8').read()
+man = open(os.path.join(M, 'AndroidManifest.xml'), encoding='utf8').read()
+bad = []
+bm = set(re.findall(r'@JavascriptInterface\s+fun\s+(\w+)', k))
+called = set(re.findall(r"\bA\(\s*['\"](\w+)['\"]", h)) | set(re.findall(r"\bAndroid\.(\w+)\s*\(", h)) | set(re.findall(r"Android\[\s*['\"](\w+)['\"]", h))
+for n in sorted(called - bm): bad.append('dead button, no Kotlin bridge: ' + n)
+for n in sorted(x for x in bm if x not in h): bad.append('Kotlin feature with no UI reference: ' + n)
+kinds = set(re.findall(r'Cmd\(\s*"([a-z_]+)"', lg)) | set(re.findall(r'kind\s*=\s*"([a-z_]+)"', lg))
+for n in sorted(kinds):
+    if '"%s" ->' % n not in svc and ('"%s"' % n) not in svc: bad.append('command kind with no executor: ' + n)
+srcs = '\n'.join(open(f, encoding='utf8').read() for f in glob.glob(J + '/*.kt'))
+for nm in re.findall(r'<(?:activity|service|receiver)\b[^>]*?android:name="([^"]+)"', man):
+    short = nm.split('.')[-1]
+    if nm.startswith('.') or nm.startswith('com.nova.assistant'):
+        if not re.search(r'\b(class|object)\s+' + short + r'\b', srcs): bad.append('manifest class missing: ' + nm)
+for tag, i in re.findall(r'<(\w+)[^>]*\bid="([^"]+)"[^>]*style="[^"]*display:\s*none', h):
+    if not re.search(r'[\'"#]' + re.escape(i) + r'[\'"]', h): bad.append('hidden forever: ' + i)
+if bad:
+    print('FAIL wiring audit'); [print(' -', b) for b in bad]; sys.exit(1)
+print('OK wiring audit: %d UI calls, %d bridge funs, %d command kinds' % (len(called), len(bm), len(kinds)))

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..16c431e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/ui_owner_check.py
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+"""R5 live UI check (headless Chromium + fake Android bridge): the four owner-required things really work on screen.
+Evidence: ui.html behaviour only, NOT a real phone. Usage: python3 ui_owner_check.py"""
+import os, sys
+from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
+HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
+UI = os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'app', 'src', 'main', 'assets', 'ui.html')
+FAKE = r"""
+(function(){var on=false,saved=0;window.__saved=function(){return saved};
+var B={toggle:function(){on=!on;return on?'started':'stopped'},running:function(){return on},
+ saveSettings:function(j){saved++;return 'ok'},
+ getSettings:function(){return JSON.stringify({wake:'nova',lang:'hi',rate:1,pitch:1,model:'x',models:['x'],hasKey:true,keyCount:1,autostart:false,screenMonitor:false})}};
+window.Android=new Proxy({},{get:function(t,k){return function(){if(B[k])return B[k].apply(null,arguments);
+ if(k==='getStatus')return JSON.stringify({versionName:'6.99',versionCode:699,mic:true});
+ if(/^(has|get(Convo|Alerts|FreeChain|FreeImg|FreeKeyless|AutoModels|MemoryInChain|ChatSpeak))/.test(k))return false;
+ if(/^get(Errors|Learned|Updates|MemoryText|VoiceLimitText)$/.test(k))return k==='getUpdates'?'{"items":[]}':'';
+ if(/^get(Wait|Auto|Memory|Voice)/.test(k))return 0;return ''}}})})();
+"""
+bad = 0
+def check(n, c):
+    global bad
+    print(('PASS ' if c else 'FAIL ') + n)
+    if not c: bad += 1
+with sync_playwright() as pw:
+    b = pw.chromium.launch(args=['--use-gl=angle', '--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'])
+    pg = b.new_context(viewport={'width': 390, 'height': 844}).new_page()
+    errs = []; pg.on('pageerror', lambda e: errs.append(str(e)))
+    pg.add_init_script(FAKE); pg.goto('file://' + os.path.abspath(UI))
+    pg.wait_for_function("document.body.classList.contains('ready')", timeout=25000); pg.wait_for_timeout(1500)
+    vis = lambda s: pg.evaluate("(s)=>{var e=document.querySelector(s);if(!e)return false;var r=e.getBoundingClientRect(),c=getComputedStyle(e);return r.width>0&&r.height>0&&c.display!='none'&&c.visibility!='hidden'}", s)
+    check("HOME tab visible and active", vis('#t_home') and pg.evaluate("document.getElementById('t_home').classList.contains('act')"))
+    check("tab bar has HOME SKILLS LIMITS CHAT SETUP", pg.evaluate("[].map.call(document.querySelectorAll('#tabs button'),b=>b.textContent.trim()).join(',')").replace(' ', '').upper().startswith("HOME,SKILLS,LIMITS"))
+    check("ACTIVATE button visible on home", vis('#btn') and pg.inner_text('#btn').strip() == 'ACTIVATE')
+    dismiss = lambda: pg.evaluate("var u=document.getElementById('uc');if(u&&u.classList.contains('on'))u.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown',{bubbles:true}))")
+    dismiss(); pg.click('#btn'); pg.wait_for_timeout(600); dismiss(); pg.wait_for_timeout(400)
+    check("ACTIVATE -> STOP after tap", pg.inner_text('#btn').strip() == 'STOP')
+    pg.click('#btn'); pg.wait_for_timeout(500); dismiss(); pg.wait_for_timeout(300)
+    check("STOP -> ACTIVATE again", pg.inner_text('#btn').strip() == 'ACTIVATE')
+    check("3D live orb running (WebGL2)", pg.evaluate("window.__orb3d") is True and vis('#orb3d'))
+    dismiss(); pg.click('#gear'); pg.wait_for_timeout(500)
+    check("Settings opens with SAVE visible at the bottom", vis('#s_save'))
+    pg.click('#s_save'); pg.wait_for_timeout(1700)
+    check("SAVE (done) reached the bridge once", pg.evaluate("window.__saved()") == 1)
+    check("SAVE (done) closed the settings panel", not vis('#s_save'))
+    check("no JavaScript errors " + str(errs), not errs)
+    b.close()
+print("failed:", bad); sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
diff --git a/tools/ui_scroll_audit.py b/tools/ui_scroll_audit.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..6061af6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/ui_scroll_audit.py
@@ -0,0 +1,119 @@
+"""v7.06 no-cover / scroll audit (headless Chromium + FAKE Android bridge). Evidence = ui.html layout only, NOT a real phone.
+For every main screen and overlay: scroll to the very end, then the last visible element must end ABOVE the bottom tab bar
+(or above the screen bottom for overlays that sit over the tab bar). Usage: python3 tools/ui_scroll_audit.py [outdir]
+Exit code 1 if any check FAILs."""
+import os, sys
+from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
+
+HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
+UI = os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'app', 'src', 'main', 'assets', 'ui.html')
+OUT = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '/tmp/nova_scroll_audit'
+os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
+
+FAKE = r"""
+(function(){var on=false;
+var B={toggle:function(){on=!on;return on?'started':'stopped'},running:function(){return on},saveSettings:function(){return 'ok'},
+ getSettings:function(){return JSON.stringify({wake:'nova',lang:'hi',rate:1,pitch:1,model:'x',models:['x'],hasKey:true,keyCount:1,autostart:false,screenMonitor:false})}};
+window.Android=new Proxy({},{get:function(t,k){return function(){if(B[k])return B[k].apply(null,arguments);
+ if(k==='getStatus')return JSON.stringify({versionName:'7.06',versionCode:706,mic:true});
+ if(/^(has|get(Convo|Alerts|FreeChain|FreeImg|FreeKeyless|AutoModels|MemoryInChain|ChatSpeak))/.test(k))return false;
+ if(/^get(Errors|Learned|Updates|MemoryText|VoiceLimitText)$/.test(k))return k==='getUpdates'?'{"items":[]}':'';
+ if(/^get(Wait|Auto|Memory|Voice)/.test(k))return 0;return ''}}})})();
+"""
+
+# JS: scroll the element to the end and return how far the last visible descendant reaches (page px) + scroll facts.
+MEASURE = r"""
+(sel)=>{
+  var el=document.querySelector(sel); if(!el) return {missing:true};
+  var cs=getComputedStyle(el);
+  if(cs.display==='none') return {hidden:true};
+  el.style.scrollBehavior='auto'; el.scrollTop=1e7;
+  var best=0, n=0;
+  var all=el.querySelectorAll('*');
+  for(var i=0;i<all.length;i++){
+    var e=all[i], c=getComputedStyle(e);
+    if(c.display==='none'||c.visibility==='hidden'||c.position==='fixed') continue;
+    var r=e.getBoundingClientRect();
+    if(r.width<2||r.height<2) continue;
+    if(e.children.length>0 && c.overflowY!=='visible' && c.overflowY!=='') {} // inner scrollers count by their own box
+    var p=e.parentElement, inner=false; while(p&&p!==el){var po=getComputedStyle(p).overflowY; if(po==='auto'||po==='scroll'){inner=true;break;} p=p.parentElement;}
+    if(inner) continue;
+    if(r.bottom>best){best=r.bottom;} n++;
+  }
+  var t=document.getElementById('tabs'); var tt=t?t.getBoundingClientRect().top:innerHeight;
+  return {lastBottom:Math.round(best), tabsTop:Math.round(tt), vh:innerHeight, scrollable:el.scrollHeight>el.clientHeight+1, sh:el.scrollHeight, ch:el.clientHeight, count:n};
+}
+"""
+
+bad = 0
+def report(name, m, limit_key):
+    global bad
+    if m.get('missing'):
+        print('NOT TESTED %s (selector not found)' % name); return
+    if m.get('hidden'):
+        print('NOT TESTED %s (not visible after opening)' % name); return
+    limit = m[limit_key]
+    ok = m['lastBottom'] <= limit + 1
+    print(('PASS ' if ok else 'FAIL ') + '%s: last element bottom %d <= %s %d (scrollHeight %d / client %d)' % (name, m['lastBottom'], limit_key, limit, m['sh'], m['ch']))
+    if not ok: bad += 1
+
+TABS = [('SKILLS', 't_skills', '#v_skills'), ('LIMITS', 't_limits', '#v_limits'), ('CONNECT', 't_conn', '#v_conn')]
+OVERLAYS = [  # name, selector, how to open, limit
+    ('HELP sheet #helpw', '#helpw .aib', "document.getElementById('helpw').classList.add('on')", 'vh'),
+    ('AI choice #aiw', '#aiw .aib', "document.getElementById('aiw').classList.add('on')", 'vh'),
+    ('SITE overlay #ws_ov', '#ws_ov .bd', "document.getElementById('ws_ov').classList.add('on')", 'vh'),
+    ('AUTOMATION overlay #au_ov', '#au_ov', "document.getElementById('au_ov').classList.add('on')", 'vh'),
+    ('CHAT sheet #sheet', '#sheet', "document.getElementById('sheet').classList.add('on')", 'tabsTop'),
+    ('HISTORY #hist', '#hist .hl', "document.getElementById('hist').style.display='flex'", 'vh'),
+]
+SIZES = [(390, 844), (360, 640), (844, 390)]  # normal phone, small phone, landscape
+
+with sync_playwright() as pw:
+    b = pw.chromium.launch(args=['--use-gl=angle', '--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'])
+    for (w, h) in SIZES:
+        print('=== viewport %dx%d' % (w, h))
+        pg = b.new_context(viewport={'width': w, 'height': h}).new_page()
+        errs = []; pg.on('pageerror', lambda e: errs.append(str(e)))
+        pg.add_init_script(FAKE); pg.goto('file://' + os.path.abspath(UI))
+        pg.wait_for_function("document.body.classList.contains('ready')", timeout=25000); pg.wait_for_timeout(1200)
+        dismiss = lambda: pg.evaluate("var u=document.getElementById('uc');if(u&&u.classList.contains('on'))u.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown',{bubbles:true}))")
+        dismiss(); pg.wait_for_timeout(300)
+        # HOME: the page itself (#app) must reach the end above the tab bar
+        report('HOME #app', pg.evaluate(MEASURE, '#app'), 'tabsTop')
+        for name, tab, sel in TABS:
+            dismiss()
+            try: pg.evaluate("(i)=>document.getElementById(i).click()", tab); pg.wait_for_timeout(350)
+            except Exception as e: print('NOT TESTED %s (tab click failed)' % name); continue
+            report(name + ' ' + sel, pg.evaluate(MEASURE, sel), 'tabsTop')
+        try:
+            dismiss(); pg.evaluate("document.getElementById('chatb').click()"); pg.wait_for_timeout(400)
+            report('CHAT #chat', pg.evaluate(MEASURE, '#chat'), 'tabsTop')
+        except Exception: print('NOT TESTED CHAT (click failed)')
+        try:
+            dismiss(); pg.evaluate("var p=document.getElementById('panel');if(p&&getComputedStyle(p).display!=='none'){}else{document.getElementById('gear').click()}"); pg.wait_for_timeout(700)
+            report('SETTINGS #panel', pg.evaluate(MEASURE, '#panel'), 'tabsTop')
+            pg.evaluate("[].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#panel details'),function(d){d.open=true})"); pg.wait_for_timeout(200)
+            report('SETTINGS #panel (all accordions open)', pg.evaluate(MEASURE, '#panel'), 'tabsTop')
+        except Exception: print('NOT TESTED SETTINGS (click failed)')
+        # overlays slide in with a CSS animation; headless + software WebGL can leave it at its start offset -> measure the settled layout
+        pg.add_style_tag(content='#helpw .aib,#aiw .aib,#ws_ov,#au_ov,#sheet .sh{animation:none!important;transform:none!important}')
+        for name, sel, opener, key in OVERLAYS:
+            try:
+                pg.evaluate(opener); pg.wait_for_timeout(900)
+                report(name, pg.evaluate(MEASURE, sel), key)
+                pg.screenshot(path=os.path.join(OUT, '%dx%d_%s.png' % (w, h, name.split('#')[-1].replace(' ', '_'))))
+                pg.evaluate("(sel)=>{var o=document.querySelector(sel.split(' ')[0]);if(o){o.classList.remove('on');o.style.display=''}}", sel)
+            except Exception as e:
+                print('NOT TESTED %s (%s)' % (name, str(e)[:60]))
+        # fut_edge / HUD layers must never swallow taps
+        for fid in ('fut_bg', 'fut_scan', 'fut_edge'):
+            pe = pg.evaluate("(i)=>{var e=document.getElementById(i);return e?getComputedStyle(e).pointerEvents:'absent'}", fid)
+            if pe == 'absent': continue
+            ok = pe == 'none'
+            print(('PASS ' if ok else 'FAIL ') + '#%s pointer-events=%s' % (fid, pe))
+            if not ok: bad += 1
+        print(('PASS ' if not errs else 'FAIL ') + 'no JavaScript errors %s' % errs[:2])
+        if errs: bad += 1
+        pg.close()
+    b.close()
+print('failed:', bad); sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
